# 門市店員 App（Expo）Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 做出 7-ELEVEN 門市店員 POC Demo App（Expo），在 iPad 與 Android 平板上用 Expo Go 跑完五個情境：語音對話、拍照辨識貨架→補貨單、問庫存→補貨單、知識庫問答、活動與優惠券（拍照＋掃條碼）。

**Architecture:** 後端新增兩支通用 API（直接呼叫唯讀工具、語音轉文字），門市 Demo MCP 服務新增今日重點與補貨單列表兩個唯讀工具。App 是 monorepo 內的獨立 npm 專案（不加入 workspaces，避免 React 18／19 衝突），以 Expo Router 分頁、React Query 取資料、`useReducer` 管對話，透過路徑別名直接引用 `shared/` 的型別。原生功能（錄音、相機、朗讀）都包在可替換的介面後面，邏輯以純函式與注入依賴的方式測試。

**Tech Stack:** 後端：Node 20+／TypeScript、Express 4、multer、`openai` 6、jest + ts-jest。門市服務：vitest。App：Expo SDK 57、Expo Router、React Native、`@tanstack/react-query`、`expo-audio`、`expo-speech`、`expo-camera`、`expo-image-picker`、`expo-image-manipulator`、`expo-secure-store`、`@react-native-async-storage/async-storage`、jest-expo + `@testing-library/react-native`。

**Spec:** [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-04-store-app-design.md](../specs/2026-10-04-store-app-design.md)

## Global Constraints

- 分支：`feature/store-app`；不得合併或 push 到 `main`，也不得 push 任何分支。
- `app/` **不加入**根目錄 `package.json` 的 `workspaces`；在 `app/` 內自行 `npm install`。App 用 `import … from '@hyagent/shared'`，由 tsconfig `paths`、Metro、Jest `moduleNameMapper` 指到 `../shared/src/index.ts`。
- App 只能用 Expo Go 內建的原生套件；不得加入需要開發版（dev client）的套件；新增 Expo 套件一律用 `npx expo install <套件>`，讓版本對齊 SDK 57。
- 平板版面門檻：寬度 `>= 900` 為寬版（左右兩欄），否則單欄。常數 `WIDE_BREAKPOINT = 900`。
- 門市 MCP 工具 id 前綴：`mcp.<serverId>.`，serverId 預設 `store-demo`，可用 `EXPO_PUBLIC_STORE_SERVER_ID` 改。
- 後端 API：
  - `POST /api/tools/:toolId/invoke`，body `{ input }`，回應 `{ data: { result, block? } }`；需 `service:agent`；未知或未開放給 agent → 404 `TOOL_NOT_FOUND`；寫入工具 → 422 `NOT_A_READ_TOOL`；MCP 錯誤 → 503 `TOOL_UNAVAILABLE`。
  - `POST /api/speech/transcribe`，multipart 欄位 `audio`（`audio/m4a`、`audio/x-m4a`、`audio/mp4`、`audio/aac`、`audio/webm`、`audio/wav`、`audio/x-wav`，≤ 10MB），回應 `{ data: { text } }`；需 `service:agent`；格式／大小錯誤 422；未設定 `OPENAI_API_KEY` → 503 `SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE`；上游失敗 → 502 `SPEECH_FAILED`；語言 `zh`，模型 `env.SPEECH_MODEL`（預設 `gpt-4o-transcribe`）。
- App 錄音上限 60 秒；短於 500 毫秒視為沒聽到，不上傳。
- 照片上傳前長邊縮到 1600px（原本較小則不放大）、轉 JPEG、品質 0.8。
- 朗讀：`expo-speech`，語言 `zh-TW`；只唸文字回覆（先去掉 markdown），表格與卡片不唸；朗讀開關預設開，存在 AsyncStorage。
- 錯誤回應格式（後端既有）：`{ error: { code, message, details? } }`。
- 所有畫面文字、註解、commit message 用繁體中文；commit 結尾 `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`。
- 後端測試一律 `DATA_BACKEND=file`（jest setup 已設）；不得使用或修改 `server/.env`；`sdk/bin/keygen.mjs` 的檔案權限變更不得提交。
- Open醬與 7-ELEVEN 標誌只用 `app/assets/brand/` 的占位圖（灰底），不得自行繪製品牌角色。

## Review Focus

- **確認彈窗連點兩下「確認」** → 只送出一次確認請求。測試在 Task 9。
- **打開彈窗時動作已過期（`expiresAt` 已過）** → 顯示「已逾時」，確認鈕不能按，不呼叫 API。測試在 Task 9。
- **錄音太短或轉出空字串** → 顯示「沒聽清楚，再說一次好嗎？」，不送出空訊息、不上傳不到 500 毫秒的錄音。測試在 Task 10。
- **兩個請求同時遇到 401** → 只換發一次 token，兩個請求都用新 token 重試；上傳類請求重試時重新建立 FormData。測試在 Task 5。
- **掃條碼時同一張券連續觸發多次** → 只查詢一次、只跳轉一次。測試在 Task 14。

## 檔案結構

| 檔案 | 職責 |
|---|---|
| `mcp/store-demo/data/equipment.json` | 設備狀態假資料 |
| `mcp/store-demo/data/promotions.json` | 加入會員活動 |
| `mcp/store-demo/src/data.ts` | 載入設備資料 |
| `mcp/store-demo/src/tools.ts` | 補貨單記錄建立時間；新增 `store_today_summary`、`replenishment_list` |
| `server/src/modules/tools/tool-invoke.controller.ts`、`tool-invoke.routes.ts` | 直接呼叫唯讀工具 API |
| `server/src/middlewares/upload.ts` | 新增錄音上傳中介層 |
| `server/src/modules/speech/*` | 語音轉文字 API |
| `server/src/config/env.ts`、`.env.example` | `SPEECH_MODEL` |
| `app/`（Expo 專案） | App 本體 |
| `app/src/api/*` | 連線設定、token、請求包裝（401 換發）、各 API 函式 |
| `app/src/settings/*` | 伺服器位址、朗讀開關 |
| `app/src/auth/*` | 登入狀態、API 提供者 |
| `app/src/chat/*` | 對話狀態、訊息與表格元件、助理面板 |
| `app/src/confirm/*` | 補貨確認彈窗與純函式 |
| `app/src/voice/*` | 錄音介面、語音狀態機、朗讀 |
| `app/src/home/*` | 今日重點、常用情境 |
| `app/src/vision/*` | 照片縮圖、辨識結果暫存、貨架與優惠券的純函式 |
| `app/src/tasks/*` | 補貨單列表、任務紅點 |
| `app/app/*` | Expo Router 路由畫面 |
| `app/README.md`、`docs/store-app-demo-checklist.md` | 執行說明與 Demo 檢查清單 |

### Task 1：門市服務新增今日重點、補貨單列表與會員活動

**Files:**
- Create: `mcp/store-demo/data/equipment.json`
- Modify: `mcp/store-demo/data/promotions.json`
- Modify: `mcp/store-demo/src/data.ts`
- Modify: `mcp/store-demo/src/tools.ts`
- Test: `mcp/store-demo/tests/tools.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 既有 `callStoreTool(name, args, ctx)`、`StoreToolContext`（`caller: { tenantId, userId, scopes, jti, system }`）、`dateAfter(now, days)`。
- Produces（App 依賴這兩個結構）：
  - `store_today_summary` → `structuredContent`：
    ```ts
    { lowStock: { count: number; names: string[] };
      expiring: { count: number; names: string[] };
      equipmentAlerts: { count: number; items: { name: string; issue: string }[] };
      replenishmentsToday: { count: number } }
    ```
  - `replenishment_list`（輸入 `{ limit?: 1–50，預設 20 }`）→ `structuredContent`：
    ```ts
    { orders: { id: string; createdAt: string; arrivalDate: string; itemCount: number; summary: string;
                items: { productId: string; name: string; quantity: number }[] }[] }
    ```
  - `StoreState.replenishments[i]` 新增 `createdAt: string`（ISO）。

在檔案最後加入：

```ts
describe('補貨單記錄建立時間', () => {
  it('replenishment_create 記下 createdAt（ISO）', () => {
    const state = createStoreState();
    callStoreTool('replenishment_create', {
      items: [{ productId: 'P001', name: '鮭魚御飯糰', stock: 6, quantity: 24 }],
      arrivalDate: '2026-10-04',
    }, ctx({ state }));
    expect(state.replenishments[0].createdAt).toBe(NOW.toISOString());
  });
});

describe('store_today_summary', () => {
  it('庫存偏低、即期品、設備異常依資料計算', () => {
    const r = callStoreTool('store_today_summary', {}, ctx());
    const s = r.structuredContent as {
      lowStock: { count: number; names: string[] };
      expiring: { count: number; names: string[] };
      equipmentAlerts: { count: number; items: { name: string; issue: string }[] };
    };
    const low = products.filter((p) => p.stock < p.safetyStock * 0.5).map((p) => p.name);
    const exp = products.filter((p) => p.expiryInDays <= 1).map((p) => p.name);
    expect(s.lowStock).toEqual({ count: low.length, names: low });
    expect(s.expiring).toEqual({ count: exp.length, names: exp });
    expect(s.equipmentAlerts.count).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(s.equipmentAlerts.items).toContainEqual({ name: '咖啡機', issue: '紅燈閃爍' });
    expect(r.display).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('今日補貨單只算呼叫者本人、同租戶、台北時區今天建立的', () => {
    const state = createStoreState();
    const base = { items: [], arrivalDate: '2026-10-04' };
    state.replenishments.push(
      { ...base, id: 'R-0001', by: 'kevin', tenantId: 't1', createdAt: '2026-10-03T01:00:00.000Z' },
      { ...base, id: 'R-0002', by: 'kevin', tenantId: 't1', createdAt: '2026-10-03T09:00:00.000Z' },
      { ...base, id: 'R-0003', by: 'amy', tenantId: 't1', createdAt: '2026-10-03T09:00:00.000Z' },
      { ...base, id: 'R-0004', by: 'kevin', tenantId: 't2', createdAt: '2026-10-03T09:00:00.000Z' },
      // 台北時間 10/02 23:30（UTC 15:30）＝昨天
      { ...base, id: 'R-0005', by: 'kevin', tenantId: 't1', createdAt: '2026-10-02T15:30:00.000Z' },
    );
    const r = callStoreTool('store_today_summary', {}, ctx({ state }));
    expect((r.structuredContent as { replenishmentsToday: { count: number } }).replenishmentsToday).toEqual({ count: 2 });
  });
});

describe('replenishment_list', () => {
  it('只列呼叫者本人、同租戶的補貨單，新的在前，帶明細與摘要', () => {
    const state = createStoreState();
    const item = (productId: string, name: string, quantity: number) => ({ productId, name, stock: 1, quantity });
    state.replenishments.push(
      { id: 'R-0001', items: [item('P001', '鮭魚御飯糰', 24)], arrivalDate: '2026-10-04', by: 'kevin', tenantId: 't1', createdAt: '2026-10-03T01:00:00.000Z' },
      { id: 'R-0002', items: [item('P003', '明太子御飯糰', 24), item('P005', '三明治－火腿蛋', 20), item('P007', '沙拉－和風雞肉', 18)], arrivalDate: '2026-10-05', by: 'kevin', tenantId: 't1', createdAt: '2026-10-03T02:00:00.000Z' },
      { id: 'R-0003', items: [item('P001', '鮭魚御飯糰', 1)], arrivalDate: '2026-10-05', by: 'amy', tenantId: 't1', createdAt: '2026-10-03T03:00:00.000Z' },
      { id: 'R-0004', items: [item('P001', '鮭魚御飯糰', 1)], arrivalDate: '2026-10-05', by: 'kevin', tenantId: 't2', createdAt: '2026-10-03T03:00:00.000Z' },
    );
    const r = callStoreTool('replenishment_list', {}, ctx({ state }));
    const orders = (r.structuredContent as { orders: { id: string; itemCount: number; summary: string; items: unknown[] }[] }).orders;
    expect(orders.map((o) => o.id)).toEqual(['R-0002', 'R-0001']);
    expect(orders[0]).toMatchObject({
      itemCount: 3,
      summary: '明太子御飯糰、三明治－火腿蛋…',
      items: [
        { productId: 'P003', name: '明太子御飯糰', quantity: 24 },
        { productId: 'P005', name: '三明治－火腿蛋', quantity: 20 },
        { productId: 'P007', name: '沙拉－和風雞肉', quantity: 18 },
      ],
    });
    expect(orders[1].summary).toBe('鮭魚御飯糰');
    expect(r.display).toMatchObject({ type: 'table', rows: 'orders' });
  });

  it('limit 限制筆數；系統身分回空清單', () => {
    const state = createStoreState();
    for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
      state.replenishments.push({ id: `R-000${i}`, items: [], arrivalDate: '2026-10-04', by: 'kevin', tenantId: 't1', createdAt: `2026-10-03T0${i}:00:00.000Z` });
    }
    const r = callStoreTool('replenishment_list', { limit: 2 }, ctx({ state }));
    expect((r.structuredContent as { orders: unknown[] }).orders).toHaveLength(2);
    const sys = callStoreTool('replenishment_list', {}, ctx({ state, caller: { tenantId: '', userId: '', scopes: [], jti: 'j', system: true } }));
    expect((sys.structuredContent as { orders: unknown[] }).orders).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

2. 把 `StoreState` 改成：

```ts
export interface ReplenishmentItem { productId: string; image?: string; name: string; stock: number; quantity: number }

export interface StoreState {
  replenishments: { id: string; items: ReplenishmentItem[]; arrivalDate: string; by: string; tenantId: string; createdAt: string }[];
}
```

3. 在 `inventoryRow` 之前加入共用判斷，並讓 `inventoryRow` 的 `status`、`expiring` 改用它們：

```ts
/** 庫存低於安全庫存一半＝庫存偏低（表格狀態與今日重點共用） */
function isLowStock(p: Product): boolean {
  return p.stock < p.safetyStock * 0.5;
}

/** 到期日 ≤ 明天＝即期 */
function isExpiring(p: Product): boolean {
  return p.expiryInDays <= 1;
}
```

```ts
    status: isLowStock(p) ? '庫存偏低' : '正常',
    expiryDate: dateAfter(ctx.now(), p.expiryInDays),
    expiring: isExpiring(p),
```

6. 在 `STORE_TOOLS` 陣列最後（`coupon_check` 之後）加入兩個工具：

```ts
  {
    name: 'store_today_summary',
    title: '今日重點',
    description: '門市今日重點：庫存偏低、即期品、設備異常的項數與名稱，以及你今天建立的補貨單張數。',
    readOnly: true,
    inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: {}, additionalProperties: false },
    run(_args, ctx) {
      const low = products.filter(isLowStock).map((p) => p.name);
      const exp = products.filter(isExpiring).map((p) => p.name);
      const alerts = equipment.filter((e) => e.status === 'alert').map((e) => ({ name: e.name, issue: e.issue }));
      const today = dateAfter(ctx.now(), 0);
      const mine = ctx.caller.system
        ? []
        : ctx.state.replenishments.filter(
            (r) => r.by === ctx.caller.userId && r.tenantId === ctx.caller.tenantId && dateAfter(new Date(r.createdAt), 0) === today,
          );
      return {
        structuredContent: {
          lowStock: { count: low.length, names: low },
          expiring: { count: exp.length, names: exp },
          equipmentAlerts: { count: alerts.length, items: alerts },
          replenishmentsToday: { count: mine.length },
        },
      };
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'replenishment_list',
    title: '查詢補貨單',
    description: '列出你建立的補貨單（新的在前），含品項與數量。',
    readOnly: true,
    inputSchema: {
      type: 'object',
      properties: { limit: { type: 'integer', title: '筆數', minimum: 1, maximum: 50 } },
      additionalProperties: false,
    },
    run(args, ctx) {
      const limit = typeof args.limit === 'number' ? args.limit : 20;
      const orders = ctx.caller.system
        ? []
        : ctx.state.replenishments
            .filter((r) => r.by === ctx.caller.userId && r.tenantId === ctx.caller.tenantId)
            .sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt))
            .slice(0, limit)
            .map((r) => {
              const names = r.items.map((i) => i.name);
              return {
                id: r.id,
                createdAt: r.createdAt,
                arrivalDate: r.arrivalDate,
                itemCount: r.items.length,
                summary: names.length > 2 ? `${names.slice(0, 2).join('、')}…` : names.join('、'),
                items: r.items.map((i) => ({ productId: i.productId, name: i.name, quantity: i.quantity })),
              };
            });
      return {
        structuredContent: { orders },
        display: {
          type: 'table',
          title: '我的補貨單',
          rows: 'orders',
          columns: [
            { key: 'id', label: '單號' },
            { key: 'summary', label: '品項' },
            { key: 'itemCount', label: '品項數' },
            { key: 'arrivalDate', label: '到貨日', format: 'date' },
          ],
        },
      };
    },
  },
```

- [ ] **Step 5：執行測試，確認通過**

```ts
/** 直接呼叫唯讀工具：只放行 agent 可用的唯讀工具；表格提示轉成 block；MCP 錯誤 503。 */
import request from 'supertest';
import { createApp } from '@/app';
import { env } from '@/config/env';
import { McpToolCallError } from '@/modules/mcp/mcp.connection';
import { registerTool, toolOutput } from '@/modules/tools';
import { tenantStore } from '@/modules/tenant/tenant.store';

const app = createApp();
const calls: unknown[] = [];

registerTool({
  id: 'test.invokeTable', description: 'x', scopes: [], agentExposed: true, effect: 'read',
  inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: { keyword: { type: 'string' } } },
  handler: async (input) => {
    calls.push(input);
    return toolOutput({ items: [{ name: '鮭魚御飯糰', stock: 6 }] }, { type: 'table', title: '庫存', rows: 'items', columns: [{ key: 'name', label: '商品' }, { key: 'stock', label: '庫存' }] });
  },
});
registerTool({
  id: 'test.invokePlain', description: 'x', scopes: [], agentExposed: true, effect: 'read',
  inputSchema: { type: 'object' },
  handler: async () => ({ ok: 1 }),
});
registerTool({
  id: 'test.invokeWrite', description: 'x', scopes: [], agentExposed: true, effect: 'write',
  inputSchema: { type: 'object' },
  handler: async () => { calls.push('write'); return {}; },
});
registerTool({
  id: 'test.invokeUnmarked', description: 'x', scopes: [], agentExposed: true,
  inputSchema: { type: 'object' },
  handler: async () => { calls.push('unmarked'); return {}; },
});
registerTool({
  id: 'test.invokeHidden', description: 'x', scopes: [], effect: 'read',
  inputSchema: { type: 'object' },
  handler: async () => ({}),
});
registerTool({
  id: 'test.invokeScoped', description: 'x', scopes: ['erp:read'], agentExposed: true, effect: 'read',
  inputSchema: { type: 'object' },
  handler: async () => ({}),
});
registerTool({
  id: 'test.invokeSchema', description: 'x', scopes: [], agentExposed: true, effect: 'read',
  inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: { code: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['code'] },
  handler: async () => ({}),
});
registerTool({
  id: 'test.invokeDown', description: 'x', scopes: [], agentExposed: true, effect: 'read',
  inputSchema: { type: 'object' },
  handler: async () => { throw new McpToolCallError('門市 Demo 暫時無法使用：connect ECONNREFUSED'); },
});

async function token(): Promise<string> {
  const res = await request(app).post('/api/auth/login').send({ username: 'kevin', password: env.AUTH_DEV_PASSWORD });
  return res.body.data.accessToken as string;
}

function invoke(t: string, toolId: string, body: unknown = { input: {} }) {
  return request(app).post(`/api/tools/${encodeURIComponent(toolId)}/invoke`).set('Authorization', `Bearer ${t}`).send(body as object);
}

beforeEach(() => {

  tenantStore.updateTenant('default', { grantedScopes: ['service:agent'] });
});

describe('POST /api/tools/:toolId/invoke', () => {
  it('唯讀工具：回傳資料與表格 block，輸入原樣傳給工具', async () => {
    const res = await invoke(await token(), 'test.invokeTable', { input: { keyword: '御飯糰' } });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(calls).toEqual([{ keyword: '御飯糰' }]);
    expect(res.body.data.result).toEqual({ items: [{ name: '鮭魚御飯糰', stock: 6 }] });
    expect(res.body.data.block).toMatchObject({ type: 'table', title: '庫存', rows: [{ name: '鮭魚御飯糰', stock: 6 }] });
  });

  it('沒有表格提示 → 沒有 block；省略 input 視為 {}', async () => {
    const res = await invoke(await token(), 'test.invokePlain', {});
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data).toEqual({ result: { ok: 1 } });
  });

  it.each(['test.invokeWrite', 'test.invokeUnmarked'])('寫入或未標示的工具 %s → 422 NOT_A_READ_TOOL，不執行', async (id) => {
    const res = await invoke(await token(), id);
    expect(res.status).toBe(422);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('NOT_A_READ_TOOL');
    expect(calls).toEqual([]);
  });

  it.each(['test.invokeHidden', 'test.nope'])('未開放給 agent 或不存在 %s → 404 TOOL_NOT_FOUND', async (id) => {
    const res = await invoke(await token(), id);
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('TOOL_NOT_FOUND');
  });

  it('缺少工具所需權限 → 403', async () => {
    const res = await invoke(await token(), 'test.invokeScoped');
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  it('輸入不符 schema → 422', async () => {
    const res = await invoke(await token(), 'test.invokeSchema', { input: {} });
    expect(res.status).toBe(422);
  });

  it('MCP 呼叫失敗 → 503 TOOL_UNAVAILABLE', async () => {
    const res = await invoke(await token(), 'test.invokeDown');
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('TOOL_UNAVAILABLE');
  });

  it('沒有 service:agent → 403；沒登入 → 401', async () => {
    tenantStore.updateTenant('default', { grantedScopes: [] });
    expect((await invoke(await token(), 'test.invokePlain')).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await request(app).post('/api/tools/test.invokePlain/invoke').send({})).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it('body 有多餘欄位 → 422', async () => {
    const res = await invoke(await token(), 'test.invokePlain', { input: {}, extra: 1 });
    expect(res.status).toBe(422);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3：實作 controller**

建立 `server/src/modules/tools/tool-invoke.controller.ts`：

```ts
/**
 * 直接呼叫唯讀工具（不經 AI）：給 App 的首頁摘要、列表、掃條碼查券等用。
 * 只放行 agent 可用的唯讀工具；寫入動作一律走確認流程（propose → confirm）。
 */
import type { Request, Response } from 'express';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { resolveScopes } from '@/modules/authz/resolve-scopes';
import { buildDisplayBlock } from '@/modules/display/display-block';
import { McpToolCallError } from '@/modules/mcp/mcp.connection';
import { isWriteTool, makeToolContext, toolRegistry, unwrapToolOutput, type ToolDef } from '@/modules/tools';
import { AppError } from '@/utils/app-error';

export const invokeParamSchema = z.object({ toolId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200) });
export const invokeBodySchema = z.object({ input: z.record(z.unknown()).default({}) }).strict();

function notFound(toolId: string): AppError {
  return new AppError(404, 'TOOL_NOT_FOUND', `找不到工具：${toolId}`);
}

export const toolInvokeController = {
  async invoke(req: Request, res: Response): Promise<void> {
    const tenantId = req.tenant?.id ?? 'default';
    const userId = req.user?.sub ?? '';
    if (!resolveScopes({ tenantId, userId }).includes('service:agent')) {
      throw AppError.forbidden('沒有使用智慧代理的權限');
    }
    const toolId = String(req.params.toolId);
    const { input } = req.body as z.infer<typeof invokeBodySchema>;
    const ctx = makeToolContext({ tenantId, userId });
    await toolRegistry.prepare(ctx);

    let tool: ToolDef;
    try {
      tool = toolRegistry.resolve(toolId, ctx);
    } catch {
      throw notFound(toolId);
    }
    // agentExposed 邊界與 propose 相同：未開放給 agent 的工具視同不存在
    if (!tool.agentExposed) throw notFound(toolId);
    if (isWriteTool(tool)) throw new AppError(422, 'NOT_A_READ_TOOL', '只能直接呼叫唯讀工具；寫入動作請走確認流程');

    let out: unknown;
    try {
      out = await toolRegistry.invoke(toolId, input, ctx);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof McpToolCallError) throw new AppError(503, 'TOOL_UNAVAILABLE', err.message);
      throw err;
    }
    const { data, display } = unwrapToolOutput(out);
    const block = display !== undefined ? buildDisplayBlock(display, data) : undefined;
    res.status(200).json({ data: { result: data, ...(block ? { block } : {}) } });
  },
};
```

```ts
import { Router } from 'express';

import { asyncHandler } from '@/utils/async-handler';
import { invokeBodySchema, invokeParamSchema, toolInvokeController } from './tool-invoke.controller';

export const toolInvokeRouter = Router();

toolInvokeRouter.post(
  '/:toolId/invoke',

  asyncHandler(toolInvokeController.invoke),
);
```

```ts
/** 語音轉文字：只收錄音格式；沒有 OpenAI key → 503；上游失敗 → 502；成功回 trim 過的文字。 */
const transcriptionsCreate = jest.fn();
jest.mock('openai', () => {
  const actual = jest.requireActual('openai');
  return {
    __esModule: true,
    ...actual,
    default: jest.fn().mockImplementation(() => ({ audio: { transcriptions: { create: transcriptionsCreate } } })),
  };
});

import request from 'supertest';
import { createApp } from '@/app';
import { env } from '@/config/env';
import { tenantStore } from '@/modules/tenant/tenant.store';

const app = createApp();
const AUDIO = Buffer.from('fake-m4a-bytes');
const mutableEnv = env as { OPENAI_API_KEY: string };
const originalKey = env.OPENAI_API_KEY;

async function token(): Promise<string> {
  const res = await request(app).post('/api/auth/login').send({ username: 'kevin', password: env.AUTH_DEV_PASSWORD });
  return res.body.data.accessToken as string;
}

function upload(t: string, mime = 'audio/m4a', file: Buffer = AUDIO) {
  return request(app)
    .post('/api/speech/transcribe')
    .set('Authorization', `Bearer ${t}`)
    .attach('audio', file, { filename: 'speech.m4a', contentType: mime });
}

beforeEach(() => {
  transcriptionsCreate.mockReset();
  mutableEnv.OPENAI_API_KEY = 'sk-test';
  tenantStore.updateTenant('default', { grantedScopes: ['service:agent'] });
});

afterAll(() => {
  mutableEnv.OPENAI_API_KEY = originalKey;
});

describe('POST /api/speech/transcribe', () => {
  it('成功：回傳 trim 過的文字，以中文與設定的模型呼叫', async () => {
    transcriptionsCreate.mockResolvedValue({ text: '  幫我查御飯糰的庫存  ' });
    const res = await upload(await token());
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data).toEqual({ text: '幫我查御飯糰的庫存' });
    const arg = transcriptionsCreate.mock.calls[0][0] as { model: string; language: string; file: { name: string } };
    expect(arg.model).toBe(env.SPEECH_MODEL);
    expect(arg.language).toBe('zh');
    expect(arg.file.name).toBe('speech.m4a');
  });

  it.each(['audio/x-m4a', 'audio/mp4', 'audio/aac', 'audio/webm', 'audio/wav', 'audio/x-wav'])('接受 %s', async (mime) => {
    transcriptionsCreate.mockResolvedValue({ text: '好' });
    expect((await upload(await token(), mime)).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it('不支援的格式 → 422，不呼叫上游', async () => {
    const res = await upload(await token(), 'text/plain');
    expect(res.status).toBe(422);
    expect(transcriptionsCreate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('沒有附檔 → 422', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).post('/api/speech/transcribe').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${await token()}`).field('x', '1');
    expect(res.status).toBe(422);
  });

  it('未設定 OPENAI_API_KEY → 503 SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE', async () => {
    mutableEnv.OPENAI_API_KEY = '';
    const res = await upload(await token());
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE');
    expect(transcriptionsCreate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('上游失敗 → 502 SPEECH_FAILED', async () => {
    transcriptionsCreate.mockRejectedValue(new Error('upstream 500'));
    const res = await upload(await token());
    expect(res.status).toBe(502);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe('SPEECH_FAILED');
  });

  it('沒有 service:agent → 403；沒登入 → 401', async () => {
    tenantStore.updateTenant('default', { grantedScopes: [] });
    expect((await upload(await token())).status).toBe(403);
    const anon = await request(app).post('/api/speech/transcribe').attach('audio', AUDIO, { filename: 'a.m4a', contentType: 'audio/m4a' });
    expect(anon.status).toBe(401);
  });
});
```

`server/src/middlewares/upload.ts` 檔案最後加入：

```ts
/** 語音轉文字專用：只收常見錄音格式，單檔 10MB（App 錄音上限 60 秒） */
export const MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_MB = 10;
const AUDIO_MIME = new Set(['audio/m4a', 'audio/x-m4a', 'audio/mp4', 'audio/aac', 'audio/webm', 'audio/wav', 'audio/x-wav']);

const audioUpload = multer({
  storage: multer.memoryStorage(),
  limits: { fileSize: MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_MB * 1024 * 1024, files: 1 },
  fileFilter(_req, file, cb) {
    if (AUDIO_MIME.has(file.mimetype)) cb(null, true);
    else cb(AppError.unprocessable(`不支援的錄音格式：${file.mimetype}`));
  },
});

export function singleAudioUpload(field = 'audio'): RequestHandler {
  const handler = audioUpload.single(field);
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
    handler(req, res, (err: unknown) => {
      if (err instanceof MulterError) {
        next(AppError.unprocessable(err.code === 'LIMIT_FILE_SIZE' ? `錄音超過上限 ${MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_MB}MB` : `上傳失敗：${err.message}`));
        return;
      }
      next(err);
    });
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4：實作 service、controller、routes**

建立 `server/src/modules/speech/speech.service.ts`：

```ts
/**
 * 語音轉文字：呼叫 OpenAI 轉錄，語言固定中文，並以提示詞偏向繁體與門市用語。
 * 錄音只在記憶體中轉送，不落地、不寫日誌。
 */
import OpenAI, { toFile } from 'openai';
import { env } from '@/config/env';
import { logger } from '@/lib/logger';
import { AppError } from '@/utils/app-error';

const EXT: Record<string, string> = {
  'audio/m4a': 'm4a',
  'audio/x-m4a': 'm4a',
  'audio/mp4': 'm4a',
  'audio/aac': 'aac',
  'audio/webm': 'webm',
  'audio/wav': 'wav',
  'audio/x-wav': 'wav',
};

const PROMPT = '以下是台灣便利商店店員的繁體中文語音，內容多為商品、庫存、補貨、活動與優惠券。';

let client: OpenAI | null = null;
let clientKey = '';

function getClient(): OpenAI {
  // key 變更（測試或熱更新）時重建 client
  if (!client || clientKey !== env.OPENAI_API_KEY) {
    client = new OpenAI({ apiKey: env.OPENAI_API_KEY });
    clientKey = env.OPENAI_API_KEY;
  }
  return client;
}

export async function transcribe(audio: { data: Buffer; mediaType: string }): Promise<string> {
  if (!env.OPENAI_API_KEY) throw new AppError(503, 'SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE', '語音辨識未啟用，請改用打字');
  const file = await toFile(audio.data, `speech.${EXT[audio.mediaType] ?? 'm4a'}`, { type: audio.mediaType });
  try {
    const res = await getClient().audio.transcriptions.create({ file, model: env.SPEECH_MODEL, language: 'zh', prompt: PROMPT });
    return res.text.trim();
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err: (err as Error).message }, 'speech transcription failed');
    throw new AppError(502, 'SPEECH_FAILED', '語音辨識失敗，請再說一次或改用打字');
  }
}
```

建立 `server/src/modules/speech/speech.controller.ts`：

```ts
import type { Request, Response } from 'express';
import { resolveScopes } from '@/modules/authz/resolve-scopes';
import { AppError } from '@/utils/app-error';
import { transcribe } from './speech.service';

export const speechController = {
  async transcribe(req: Request, res: Response): Promise<void> {
    const tenantId = req.tenant?.id ?? 'default';
    const userId = req.user?.sub ?? '';
    if (!resolveScopes({ tenantId, userId }).includes('service:agent')) {
      throw AppError.forbidden('沒有使用語音對話的權限');
    }
    const file = req.file;
    if (!file) throw AppError.unprocessable('請附上錄音（欄位 audio）');
    const text = await transcribe({ data: file.buffer, mediaType: file.mimetype });
    res.status(200).json({ data: { text } });
  },
};
```

**Files:**

建立 `app/metro.config.js`：

```js
// App 不在 npm workspaces 內：套件一律從 app/node_modules 解析（根目錄是 React 18，不能被抓到），
// 只額外監看 shared/ 的原始碼。
const path = require('path');
const { getDefaultConfig } = require('expo/metro-config');

const projectRoot = __dirname;
const sharedRoot = path.resolve(projectRoot, '../shared');
const config = getDefaultConfig(projectRoot);

config.watchFolders = [sharedRoot];
config.resolver.nodeModulesPaths = [path.resolve(projectRoot, 'node_modules')];
config.resolver.disableHierarchicalLookup = true;
config.resolver.extraNodeModules = { '@hyagent/shared': sharedRoot };

module.exports = config;
```

建立 `app/src/layout/useIsWide.ts`：

```ts
import { useWindowDimensions } from 'react-native';

/** 寬度達此值走平板左右兩欄，否則單欄 */
export const WIDE_BREAKPOINT = 900;

export function isWide(width: number): boolean {
  return width >= WIDE_BREAKPOINT;
}

export function useIsWide(): boolean {
  const { width } = useWindowDimensions();
  return isWide(width);
}
```

建立 `app/scripts/make-placeholders.mjs`（不依賴任何套件，產生灰底 PNG）：

```js
// 產生品牌占位圖（灰底 PNG）。拿到正式的 Open醬與標誌後，直接用同檔名覆蓋即可。
import { deflateSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const CRC_TABLE = Array.from({ length: 256 }, (_, n) => {
  let c = n;
  for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1;
  return c >>> 0;
});
function crc32(buf) {
  let c = 0xffffffff;
  for (const b of buf) c = CRC_TABLE[(c ^ b) & 0xff] ^ (c >>> 8);
  return (c ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}
function chunk(type, data) {
  const len = Buffer.alloc(4);
  len.writeUInt32BE(data.length);
  const td = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(type), data]);
  const crc = Buffer.alloc(4);
  crc.writeUInt32BE(crc32(td));
  return Buffer.concat([len, td, crc]);
}
function png(w, h, [r, g, b]) {
  const raw = Buffer.alloc((w * 3 + 1) * h);
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    const o = y * (w * 3 + 1);
    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      raw[o + 1 + x * 3] = r;
      raw[o + 2 + x * 3] = g;
      raw[o + 3 + x * 3] = b;
    }
  }
  const ihdr = Buffer.alloc(13);
  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(w, 0);
  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(h, 4);
  ihdr[8] = 8;
  ihdr[9] = 2;
  return Buffer.concat([
    Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a]),
    chunk('IHDR', ihdr),
    chunk('IDAT', deflateSync(raw)),
    chunk('IEND', Buffer.alloc(0)),
  ]);
}

const dir = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', 'assets', 'brand');
mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
const gray = [0xd9, 0xde, 0xdb];
for (const name of ['mascot-wave', 'mascot-mic', 'mascot-idle']) writeFileSync(join(dir, `${name}.png`), png(512, 512, gray));
writeFileSync(join(dir, 'logo.png'), png(480, 120, gray));
console.log('placeholders written to', dir);
```

執行 `cd app && node scripts/make-placeholders.mjs`。

```md
目前是灰底占位圖。拿到正式素材後用**同檔名**覆蓋即可，程式不用改：
```

建立 `app/src/brand/Mascot.tsx`：

```tsx
/** Open醬與品牌標誌：圖檔放在 assets/brand（目前為占位圖）。 */
import { Image } from 'react-native';

const MASCOT = {
  wave: require('../../assets/brand/mascot-wave.png'),
  mic: require('../../assets/brand/mascot-mic.png'),
  idle: require('../../assets/brand/mascot-idle.png'),
} as const;

export type MascotPose = keyof typeof MASCOT;

export function Mascot({ pose, size }: { pose: MascotPose; size: number }) {
  return (
    <Image
      source={MASCOT[pose]}
      style={{ width: size, height: size, borderRadius: size / 2 }}
      resizeMode="contain"
      accessibilityLabel="Open醬"
    />
  );
}

export function BrandLogo({ height = 32 }: { height?: number }) {
  return (
    <Image
      source={require('../../assets/brand/logo.png')}
      style={{ width: height * 4, height, borderRadius: 6 }}
      resizeMode="contain"
      accessibilityLabel="7-ELEVEN"
    />
  );
}
```

### Task 5：API 連線層（token 換發、錯誤、各 API 函式）

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/api/errors.ts`、`app/src/api/config.ts`、`app/src/api/client.ts`、`app/src/api/types.ts`、`app/src/api/endpoints.ts`、`app/src/api/storeTools.ts`
- Test: `app/src/api/client.test.ts`、`app/src/api/endpoints.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `class ApiError extends Error { status: number; code?: string; details?: unknown }`；`errorMessage(e: unknown): string`
  - `normalizeServerUrl(raw: string): string`
  - `interface Tokens { accessToken: string; refreshToken: string }`、`interface TokenStore { get(); set(t); clear() }`
  - `createApiClient(deps: { baseUrl: () => string; tokens: TokenStore; fetchFn?: typeof fetch; onAuthLost?: () => void }): ApiClient`，`ApiClient.request<T>(path, opts?)`；`opts`：`{ method?, json?, form?: () => FormData, auth?: boolean, timeoutMs?: number }`；`path` 不含 `/api`
  - `createEndpoints(api: ApiClient)` → `Endpoints`：`login`、`me`、`startConversation`、`sendMessage`、`proposeAction`、`confirmAction`、`cancelAction`、`analyzeImage`、`transcribe`、`invokeTool`、`notifications`
  - `types.ts`：`LoginResult`、`TurnData`、`StartData`、`ShelfFinding`、`ShelfResult`、`CouponResult`、`InvokeResult<T>`、`StoreTodaySummary`、`ReplenishmentOrder`、`UploadFile`
  - `storeTool(name)`：回傳 `mcp.<serverId>.<name>`

- [ ] **Step 1：寫失敗的測試**

建立 `app/src/api/client.test.ts`：

```ts
import { createApiClient, type TokenStore, type Tokens } from './client';
import { normalizeServerUrl } from './config';
import { ApiError } from './errors';

type Handler = (url: string, init: RequestInit) => { status: number; body?: unknown } | Promise<{ status: number; body?: unknown }>;

function fakeFetch(handler: Handler) {
  const calls: { url: string; init: RequestInit }[] = [];
  const fn = jest.fn(async (url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
    calls.push({ url, init });
    const r = await handler(url, init);
    return { status: r.status, ok: r.status >= 200 && r.status < 300, json: async () => r.body } as unknown as Response;
  });
  return { fn: fn as unknown as typeof fetch, calls };
}

function memoryTokens(initial: Tokens | null = { accessToken: 'a1', refreshToken: 'r1' }): TokenStore & { value: Tokens | null } {
  const s = {
    value: initial,
    async get() { return s.value; },
    async set(t: Tokens) { s.value = t; },
    async clear() { s.value = null; },
  };
  return s;
}

const auth = (init: RequestInit) => (init.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization;

describe('normalizeServerUrl', () => {
  it.each([
    ['http://192.168.1.5:3000/', 'http://192.168.1.5:3000'],
    ['  https://api.example.com//  ', 'https://api.example.com'],
    ['192.168.1.5:3000', 'http://192.168.1.5:3000'],
    ['', ''],
  ])('%s → %s', (raw, out) => expect(normalizeServerUrl(raw)).toBe(out));
});

describe('createApiClient', () => {
  it('組出 /api 路徑、帶 Bearer、回傳 data', async () => {
    const f = fakeFetch(() => ({ status: 200, body: { data: { ok: true } } }));
    const api = createApiClient({ baseUrl: () => 'http://h', tokens: memoryTokens(), fetchFn: f.fn });
    await expect(api.request('/x', { method: 'POST', json: { a: 1 } })).resolves.toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(f.calls[0].url).toBe('http://h/api/x');
    expect(auth(f.calls[0].init)).toBe('Bearer a1');
    expect(f.calls[0].init.body).toBe('{"a":1}');
  });

  it('auth:false 不帶 token', async () => {
    const f = fakeFetch(() => ({ status: 200, body: { data: 1 } }));
    const api = createApiClient({ baseUrl: () => 'http://h', tokens: memoryTokens(), fetchFn: f.fn });
    await api.request('/auth/login', { method: 'POST', json: {}, auth: false });
    expect(auth(f.calls[0].init)).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('錯誤回應轉成 ApiError（狀態、code、訊息）', async () => {
    const f = fakeFetch(() => ({ status: 422, body: { error: { code: 'ACTION_INPUT_INVALID', message: '數量太大' } } }));
    const api = createApiClient({ baseUrl: () => 'http://h', tokens: memoryTokens(), fetchFn: f.fn });
    const err = await api.request('/x').catch((e) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ status: 422, code: 'ACTION_INPUT_INVALID', message: '數量太大' });
  });

  it('網路失敗 → ApiError status 0 / NETWORK；沒設定伺服器 → NO_SERVER', async () => {
    const down = jest.fn(async () => { throw new TypeError('Network request failed'); }) as unknown as typeof fetch;
    const api = createApiClient({ baseUrl: () => 'http://h', tokens: memoryTokens(), fetchFn: down });
    await expect(api.request('/x')).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 0, code: 'NETWORK' });
    const none = createApiClient({ baseUrl: () => '', tokens: memoryTokens(), fetchFn: down });
    await expect(none.request('/x')).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 0, code: 'NO_SERVER' });
  });

  it('401 → 換發一次後用新 token 重試；上傳類請求重新建立 FormData', async () => {
    const tokens = memoryTokens();
    const f = fakeFetch((url, init) => {
      if (url.endsWith('/auth/refresh')) return { status: 200, body: { data: { accessToken: 'a2', refreshToken: 'r2' } } };
      return auth(init) === 'Bearer a2' ? { status: 200, body: { data: 'ok' } } : { status: 401, body: { error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED' } } };
    });
    const form = jest.fn(() => ({ fake: 'form' }) as unknown as FormData);
    const api = createApiClient({ baseUrl: () => 'http://h', tokens, fetchFn: f.fn });
    await expect(api.request('/upload', { method: 'POST', form })).resolves.toBe('ok');
    expect(form).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(tokens.value).toEqual({ accessToken: 'a2', refreshToken: 'r2' });
    expect(JSON.parse(f.calls.find((c) => c.url.endsWith('/auth/refresh'))!.init.body as string)).toEqual({ refreshToken: 'r1' });
  });

  it('兩個請求同時 401 → 只換發一次，兩個都成功', async () => {
    let refreshes = 0;
    const tokens = memoryTokens();
    const f = fakeFetch(async (url, init) => {
      if (url.endsWith('/auth/refresh')) {
        refreshes++;
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));
        return { status: 200, body: { data: { accessToken: 'a2', refreshToken: 'r2' } } };
      }
      return auth(init) === 'Bearer a2' ? { status: 200, body: { data: url } } : { status: 401 };
    });
    const api = createApiClient({ baseUrl: () => 'http://h', tokens, fetchFn: f.fn });
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([api.request('/a'), api.request('/b')]);
    expect([a, b]).toEqual(['http://h/api/a', 'http://h/api/b']);
    expect(refreshes).toBe(1);
  });

  it('換發失敗 → 清掉 token、通知登出、丟 401', async () => {
    const tokens = memoryTokens();
    const onAuthLost = jest.fn();
    const f = fakeFetch((url) => (url.endsWith('/auth/refresh') ? { status: 401 } : { status: 401 }));
    const api = createApiClient({ baseUrl: () => 'http://h', tokens, fetchFn: f.fn, onAuthLost });
    await expect(api.request('/x')).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    expect(tokens.value).toBeNull();
    expect(onAuthLost).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { createEndpoints } from './endpoints';
import type { ApiClient, RequestOptions } from './client';
import { storeTool } from './storeTools';

function recorder() {
  const calls: { path: string; opts?: RequestOptions }[] = [];
  const api: ApiClient = { request: jest.fn(async (path: string, opts?: RequestOptions) => { calls.push({ path, opts }); return {} as never; }) };
  return { api, calls };
}

describe('createEndpoints', () => {
  it('對話相關路徑與 body', async () => {
    const { api, calls } = recorder();
    const e = createEndpoints(api);
    await e.startConversation('你好');
    await e.startConversation();
    await e.sendMessage('c1', '查庫存');
    await e.confirmAction('c1', 'a1', { qty: 3 });
    await e.confirmAction('c1', 'a1');
    await e.cancelAction('c1', 'a1');
    await e.proposeAction('c1', 't', { x: 1 });
    expect(calls.map((c) => [c.path, c.opts?.json])).toEqual([
      ['/conversations', { message: '你好' }],
      ['/conversations', {}],
      ['/conversations/c1/messages', { message: '查庫存' }],
      ['/conversations/c1/actions/a1/confirm', { input: { qty: 3 } }],
      ['/conversations/c1/actions/a1/confirm', {}],
      ['/conversations/c1/actions/a1/cancel', {}],
      ['/conversations/c1/actions/propose', { toolId: 't', input: { x: 1 } }],
    ]);
  });

  it('工具 id 編碼進路徑；login 不帶 token', async () => {
    const { api, calls } = recorder();
    const e = createEndpoints(api);
    await e.invokeTool(storeTool('coupon_check'), { code: 'COFFEE50' });
    await e.login('kevin', 'pw');
    expect(calls[0]).toMatchObject({ path: '/tools/mcp.store-demo.coupon_check/invoke', opts: { json: { input: { code: 'COFFEE50' } } } });
    expect(calls[1]).toMatchObject({ path: '/auth/login', opts: { auth: false, json: { username: 'kevin', password: 'pw' } } });
  });

  it('上傳用 form 工廠（每次呼叫產生新的 FormData）', async () => {
    const { api, calls } = recorder();
    const e = createEndpoints(api);
    await e.analyzeImage('shelf', { uri: 'file:///a.jpg', name: 'photo.jpg', type: 'image/jpeg' });
    await e.transcribe({ uri: 'file:///a.m4a', name: 'speech.m4a', type: 'audio/m4a' });
    expect(calls[0].path).toBe('/vision/analyze');
    expect(calls[1].path).toBe('/speech/transcribe');
    const f1 = calls[0].opts!.form!();
    const f2 = calls[0].opts!.form!();
    expect(f1).not.toBe(f2);
  });
});
```

```ts
/** 帶 HTTP 狀態與後端錯誤碼的 API 錯誤；status 0 代表沒連上（NETWORK／TIMEOUT／NO_SERVER）。 */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly code?: string;
  readonly details?: unknown;

  constructor(message: string, status: number, code?: string, details?: unknown) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
    this.details = details;
  }
}

export function errorMessage(e: unknown): string {
  if (e instanceof Error && e.message) return e.message;
  return '發生錯誤，請稍後再試';
}
```

`app/src/api/config.ts`：

```ts
/** 使用者輸入的伺服器位址：去空白與結尾斜線，沒寫通訊協定時補 http://（區網 IP 常見寫法）。 */
export function normalizeServerUrl(raw: string): string {
  const t = raw.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');
  if (!t) return '';
  return /^https?:\/\//i.test(t) ? t : `http://${t}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3：實作 client**

`app/src/api/client.ts`：

```ts
/**
 * API 請求包裝：組網址、帶 token、逾時、錯誤轉 ApiError。
 * 401 時以 refresh token 換發一次（同時多個請求共用同一次換發），成功後重送；失敗則清 token 並通知登出。
 * 上傳用 form 工廠：重送時重新建立 FormData，不重用已送出的 body。
 */
import { ApiError } from './errors';

export interface Tokens {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
}

export interface TokenStore {
  get(): Promise<Tokens | null>;
  set(tokens: Tokens): Promise<void>;
  clear(): Promise<void>;
}

export interface RequestOptions {
  method?: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE';
  json?: unknown;
  form?: () => FormData;
  /** false：不帶 token、401 不換發（登入、換發本身） */
  auth?: boolean;
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

export interface ApiClient {
  request<T>(path: string, opts?: RequestOptions): Promise<T>;
}

export interface ApiClientDeps {
  baseUrl: () => string;
  tokens: TokenStore;
  fetchFn?: typeof fetch;
  onAuthLost?: () => void;
}

const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;

export function createApiClient(deps: ApiClientDeps): ApiClient {
  const fetchFn = deps.fetchFn ?? ((input, init) => fetch(input, init));
  let refreshing: Promise<boolean> | null = null;

  async function send(path: string, opts: RequestOptions, accessToken?: string): Promise<Response> {
    const base = deps.baseUrl();
    if (!base) throw new ApiError('尚未設定伺服器位址', 0, 'NO_SERVER');
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { Accept: 'application/json' };
    let body: BodyInit | undefined;
    if (opts.form) {
      body = opts.form();
    } else if (opts.json !== undefined) {
      headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
      body = JSON.stringify(opts.json);
    }
    if (accessToken && opts.auth !== false) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${accessToken}`;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), opts.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS);
    try {
      return await fetchFn(`${base}/api${path}`, {
        method: opts.method ?? (body !== undefined ? 'POST' : 'GET'),
        headers,
        body,
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      const aborted = (err as { name?: string }).name === 'AbortError';
      throw new ApiError(aborted ? '連線逾時，請再試一次' : '無法連線到伺服器，請檢查網路', 0, aborted ? 'TIMEOUT' : 'NETWORK');
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }

  async function doRefresh(): Promise<boolean> {
    const tokens = await deps.tokens.get();
    if (!tokens) return false;
    try {
      const res = await send('/auth/refresh', { method: 'POST', json: { refreshToken: tokens.refreshToken }, auth: false });
      if (!res.ok) return false;
      const body = (await res.json()) as { data: Tokens };
      await deps.tokens.set({ accessToken: body.data.accessToken, refreshToken: body.data.refreshToken });
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function refreshOnce(): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!refreshing) refreshing = doRefresh().finally(() => { refreshing = null; });
    return refreshing;
  }

  async function parse<T>(res: Response): Promise<T> {
    if (res.status === 204) return undefined as T;
    const body = (await res.json().catch(() => null)) as
      | { data?: T; error?: { code?: string; message?: string; details?: unknown } }
      | null;
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new ApiError(body?.error?.message ?? `伺服器錯誤（${res.status}）`, res.status, body?.error?.code, body?.error?.details);
    }
    return body?.data as T;
  }

  return {
    async request<T>(path: string, opts: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
      const tokens = opts.auth === false ? null : await deps.tokens.get();
      let res = await send(path, opts, tokens?.accessToken);
      if (res.status === 401 && tokens) {
        // 別的請求可能已換發完成：token 已不同就直接用新的重送，不再換發
        const current = await deps.tokens.get();
        const ok = current && current.accessToken !== tokens.accessToken ? true : await refreshOnce();
        if (ok) {
          const next = await deps.tokens.get();
          res = await send(path, opts, next?.accessToken);
        } else {
          await deps.tokens.clear();
          deps.onAuthLost?.();
        }
      }
      return parse<T>(res);
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
/** App 用到的後端回應型別；確認卡片、表格、使用者等共用型別來自 @hyagent/shared。 */
import type { Applicant, DisplayBlock, PendingActionView, SessionStatus } from '@hyagent/shared';

export interface LoginResult {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  user: Applicant;
}

export interface TurnData {
  status: SessionStatus;
  reply: string | null;
  suggestions?: string[];
  pendingAction?: PendingActionView;
  blocks?: DisplayBlock[];
  /** 確認端點回傳：執行是否成功 */
  actionOk?: boolean;
}

export interface StartData extends TurnData {
  id: string;
}

export interface UploadFile {
  uri: string;
  name: string;
  type: string;
}

export type ShelfStatus = 'out_of_stock' | 'expiring' | 'misplaced' | 'ok';

export interface ShelfFinding {
  name: string;
  productId?: string;
  status: ShelfStatus;
  /** 0–1 相對座標 */
  box: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number };
  stock?: number;
  image?: string;
  suggestedQty?: number;
  expiryDate?: string;
  note?: string;
}

export interface ShelfResult {
  mode: 'shelf';
  findings: ShelfFinding[];
  counts: { outOfStock: number; expiring: number; misplaced: number };
  replenishment?: { toolId: string; input: Record<string, unknown> };
  warnings?: string[];
}

export interface CouponResult {
  mode: 'coupon';
  extracted: { code?: string; title?: string; expiry?: string };
  result?: Record<string, unknown>;

}

  block?: DisplayBlock;
}

export interface StoreTodaySummary {
  lowStock: { count: number; names: string[] };
  expiring: { count: number; names: string[] };
  equipmentAlerts: { count: number; items: { name: string; issue: string }[] };
  replenishmentsToday: { count: number };
}

export interface ReplenishmentOrder {
  id: string;
  createdAt: string;
  arrivalDate: string;
  itemCount: number;
  summary: string;
  items: { productId: string; name: string; quantity: number }[];
}
```

`app/src/api/endpoints.ts`：

```ts
/** 後端 API 函式：路徑一律不含 /api（由 client 補上）。 */
import type { Applicant, NotificationFeed, PendingActionView } from '@hyagent/shared';
import type { ApiClient } from './client';
import type { CouponResult, InvokeResult, LoginResult, ShelfResult, StartData, TurnData, UploadFile } from './types';

/** RN 的 FormData 以 { uri, name, type } 表示檔案 */
function fileForm(field: string, file: UploadFile, extra: Record<string, string> = {}): () => FormData {
  return () => {
    const form = new FormData();
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(extra)) form.append(k, v);
    form.append(field, file as unknown as Blob);
    return form;
  };
}

const TURN_TIMEOUT_MS = 90_000;

export function createEndpoints(api: ApiClient) {
  return {
    login: (username: string, password: string) =>
      api.request<LoginResult>('/auth/login', { method: 'POST', json: { username, password }, auth: false }),
    me: () => api.request<Applicant>('/auth/me'),

    startConversation: (message?: string) =>
      api.request<StartData>('/conversations', { method: 'POST', json: message ? { message } : {}, timeoutMs: TURN_TIMEOUT_MS }),
    sendMessage: (id: string, message: string) =>
      api.request<TurnData>(`/conversations/${id}/messages`, { method: 'POST', json: { message }, timeoutMs: TURN_TIMEOUT_MS }),
    proposeAction: (id: string, toolId: string, input: Record<string, unknown>) =>
      api.request<{ pendingAction: PendingActionView }>(`/conversations/${id}/actions/propose`, { method: 'POST', json: { toolId, input } }),
    confirmAction: (id: string, actionId: string, input?: Record<string, unknown>) =>
      api.request<TurnData>(`/conversations/${id}/actions/${actionId}/confirm`, {
        method: 'POST',
        json: input ? { input } : {},
        timeoutMs: TURN_TIMEOUT_MS,
      }),
    cancelAction: (id: string, actionId: string) =>
      api.request<TurnData>(`/conversations/${id}/actions/${actionId}/cancel`, { method: 'POST', json: {} }),

    analyzeImage: (mode: 'shelf' | 'coupon', file: UploadFile) =>
      api.request<ShelfResult | CouponResult>('/vision/analyze', { method: 'POST', form: fileForm('image', file, { mode }), timeoutMs: TURN_TIMEOUT_MS }),
    transcribe: (file: UploadFile) =>
      api.request<{ text: string }>('/speech/transcribe', { method: 'POST', form: fileForm('audio', file), timeoutMs: 60_000 }),
    invokeTool: <T>(toolId: string, input: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
      api.request<InvokeResult<T>>(`/tools/${encodeURIComponent(toolId)}/invoke`, { method: 'POST', json: { input } }),
    notifications: () => api.request<NotificationFeed>('/notifications'),
  };
}

export type Endpoints = ReturnType<typeof createEndpoints>;
```

`app/src/settings/SettingsProvider.test.tsx`：

```tsx
import { act, renderHook, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { SettingsProvider, useSettings, type KeyValueStore } from './SettingsProvider';

function memoryStore(init: Record<string, string> = {}): KeyValueStore & { data: Record<string, string> } {
  const s = {
    data: { ...init },
    async getItem(k: string) { return s.data[k] ?? null; },
    async setItem(k: string, v: string) { s.data[k] = v; },
  };
  return s;
}

function setup(store: KeyValueStore, defaultServerUrl = '') {
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => (
    <SettingsProvider store={store} defaultServerUrl={defaultServerUrl}>{children}</SettingsProvider>
  );
  return renderHook(() => useSettings(), { wrapper });
}

describe('SettingsProvider', () => {
  it('沒有儲存值時：伺服器用預設值（正規化）、朗讀預設開', async () => {
    const { result } = setup(memoryStore(), 'http://10.0.0.2:3000/');
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.ready).toBe(true));
    expect(result.current.serverUrl).toBe('http://10.0.0.2:3000');
    expect(result.current.speakEnabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it('讀回儲存值；修改會正規化並寫回', async () => {
    const store = memoryStore({ 'settings.serverUrl': 'http://a', 'settings.speakEnabled': 'false' });
    const { result } = setup(store);
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.ready).toBe(true));
    expect(result.current.serverUrl).toBe('http://a');
    expect(result.current.speakEnabled).toBe(false);
    await act(() => result.current.setServerUrl('192.168.1.9:3000/'));
    await act(() => result.current.setSpeakEnabled(true));
    expect(result.current.serverUrl).toBe('http://192.168.1.9:3000');
    expect(store.data).toEqual({ 'settings.serverUrl': 'http://192.168.1.9:3000', 'settings.speakEnabled': 'true' });
  });
});
```

`app/src/auth/AuthProvider.test.tsx`：

```tsx
import { act, renderHook, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { TokenStore, Tokens } from '@/api/client';
import { SettingsProvider, type KeyValueStore } from '@/settings/SettingsProvider';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './AuthProvider';

const USER = { id: 'kevin', name: 'Kevin Yang', department: '門市', title: '店員' };

function memoryTokens(initial: Tokens | null): TokenStore & { value: Tokens | null } {
  const s = {
    value: initial,
    async get() { return s.value; },
    async set(t: Tokens) { s.value = t; },
    async clear() { s.value = null; },
  };
  return s;
}

const kv: KeyValueStore = { async getItem() { return null; }, async setItem() {} };

function fetchFrom(handler: (url: string, init: RequestInit) => { status: number; body?: unknown }) {
  return jest.fn(async (url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
    const r = handler(url, init);
    return { status: r.status, ok: r.status < 300, json: async () => r.body } as unknown as Response;
  }) as unknown as typeof fetch;
}

function setup(tokens: TokenStore, fetchFn: typeof fetch) {
  const qc = new QueryClient();
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => (
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <SettingsProvider store={kv} defaultServerUrl="http://h">
        <AuthProvider tokenStore={tokens} fetchFn={fetchFn}>{children}</AuthProvider>
      </SettingsProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
  return renderHook(() => useAuth(), { wrapper });
}

describe('AuthProvider', () => {
  it('沒有 token → signedOut；登入後存 token、signedIn', async () => {
    const tokens = memoryTokens(null);
    const fetchFn = fetchFrom((url) =>
      url.endsWith('/auth/login') ? { status: 200, body: { data: { accessToken: 'a', refreshToken: 'r', user: USER } } } : { status: 404 },
    );
    const { result } = setup(tokens, fetchFn);
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.status).toBe('signedOut'));
    await act(() => result.current.login('kevin', 'pw'));
    expect(result.current.status).toBe('signedIn');
    expect(result.current.user).toEqual(USER);
    expect(tokens.value).toEqual({ accessToken: 'a', refreshToken: 'r' });
  });

  it('有 token → 以 /auth/me 還原登入', async () => {
    const fetchFn = fetchFrom((url) => (url.endsWith('/auth/me') ? { status: 200, body: { data: USER } } : { status: 404 }));
    const { result } = setup(memoryTokens({ accessToken: 'a', refreshToken: 'r' }), fetchFn);
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.status).toBe('signedIn'));
    expect(result.current.user).toEqual(USER);
  });

  it('token 失效且換發失敗 → signedOut、token 清除', async () => {
    const tokens = memoryTokens({ accessToken: 'a', refreshToken: 'r' });
    const { result } = setup(tokens, fetchFrom(() => ({ status: 401 })));
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.status).toBe('signedOut'));
    expect(tokens.value).toBeNull();
  });

  it('登出清 token、回到 signedOut', async () => {
    const tokens = memoryTokens({ accessToken: 'a', refreshToken: 'r' });
    const { result } = setup(tokens, fetchFrom(() => ({ status: 200, body: { data: USER } })));
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.status).toBe('signedIn'));
    await act(() => result.current.logout());
    expect(result.current.status).toBe('signedOut');
    expect(tokens.value).toBeNull();
  });

  it('登入失敗 → 丟出錯誤、維持 signedOut', async () => {
    const fetchFn = fetchFrom(() => ({ status: 401, body: { error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED', message: '帳號或密碼錯誤' } } }));
    const { result } = setup(memoryTokens(null), fetchFn);
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.status).toBe('signedOut'));
    await expect(result.current.login('kevin', 'bad')).rejects.toMatchObject({ message: '帳號或密碼錯誤' });
    expect(result.current.status).toBe('signedOut');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { TaskBadgeProvider, useTaskBadge } from './TaskBadge';

it('bump 累加、clear 歸零', () => {
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => <TaskBadgeProvider>{children}</TaskBadgeProvider>;
  const { result } = renderHook(() => useTaskBadge(), { wrapper });
  act(() => result.current.bump());
  act(() => result.current.bump());
  expect(result.current.count).toBe(2);
  act(() => result.current.clear());
  expect(result.current.count).toBe(0);
});
```

`app/src/settings/SettingsProvider.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 裝置設定：伺服器位址、朗讀開關（存在 AsyncStorage；測試可注入 store）。 */
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { normalizeServerUrl } from '@/api/config';

export interface KeyValueStore {
  getItem(key: string): Promise<string | null>;
  setItem(key: string, value: string): Promise<void>;
}

const KEYS = { server: 'settings.serverUrl', speak: 'settings.speakEnabled' } as const;

export interface Settings {
  ready: boolean;
  serverUrl: string;
  speakEnabled: boolean;
  setServerUrl(url: string): Promise<void>;
  setSpeakEnabled(on: boolean): Promise<void>;
}

const SettingsContext = createContext<Settings | null>(null);

export function SettingsProvider({
  children,
  store = AsyncStorage,
  defaultServerUrl = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE ?? '',
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  store?: KeyValueStore;
  defaultServerUrl?: string;
}) {
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [serverUrl, setServer] = useState('');
  const [speakEnabled, setSpeak] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void (async () => {
      const [s, sp] = await Promise.all([store.getItem(KEYS.server), store.getItem(KEYS.speak)]);
      if (!alive) return;
      setServer(normalizeServerUrl(s ?? defaultServerUrl));
      setSpeak(sp === null ? true : sp === 'true');
      setReady(true);
    })();
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [store, defaultServerUrl]);

  const setServerUrl = useCallback(
    async (url: string) => {
      const v = normalizeServerUrl(url);
      setServer(v);
      await store.setItem(KEYS.server, v);
    },
    [store],
  );

  const setSpeakEnabled = useCallback(
    async (on: boolean) => {
      setSpeak(on);
      await store.setItem(KEYS.speak, String(on));
    },
    [store],
  );

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({ ready, serverUrl, speakEnabled, setServerUrl, setSpeakEnabled }),
    [ready, serverUrl, speakEnabled, setServerUrl, setSpeakEnabled],
  );
  return <SettingsContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</SettingsContext.Provider>;
}

export function useSettings(): Settings {
  const v = useContext(SettingsContext);
  if (!v) throw new Error('useSettings 必須在 SettingsProvider 內使用');
  return v;
}
```

`app/src/api/secureTokens.ts`：

```ts
/** 登入 token 存在系統安全儲存區（iOS Keychain／Android Keystore）。 */
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import type { TokenStore, Tokens } from './client';

const KEY = 'auth.tokens';

export const secureTokenStore: TokenStore = {
  async get() {
    const raw = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    try {
      return JSON.parse(raw) as Tokens;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  },
  async set(tokens) {
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(KEY, JSON.stringify(tokens));
  },
  async clear() {
    await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(KEY);
  },
};
```

`app/src/tasks/TaskBadge.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 任務分頁紅點：補貨單建立成功時 +1，進入任務分頁時歸零。 */
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';

interface TaskBadge {
  count: number;
  bump(): void;
  clear(): void;
}

const TaskBadgeContext = createContext<TaskBadge | null>(null);

export function TaskBadgeProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);
  const bump = useCallback(() => setCount((c) => c + 1), []);
  const clear = useCallback(() => setCount(0), []);
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ count, bump, clear }), [count, bump, clear]);
  return <TaskBadgeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</TaskBadgeContext.Provider>;
}

export function useTaskBadge(): TaskBadge {
  const v = useContext(TaskBadgeContext);
  if (!v) throw new Error('useTaskBadge 必須在 TaskBadgeProvider 內使用');
  return v;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3：實作 AuthProvider**

`app/src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * 登入狀態與 API：token 存安全儲存區，啟動時以 /auth/me 還原；任何請求換發失敗即登出。
 * api 的伺服器位址讀最新設定（ref），不因修改設定而重建。
 */
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { Applicant } from '@hyagent/shared';
import { createApiClient, type TokenStore } from '@/api/client';
import { createEndpoints, type Endpoints } from '@/api/endpoints';
import { ApiError } from '@/api/errors';
import { secureTokenStore } from '@/api/secureTokens';
import { useSettings } from '@/settings/SettingsProvider';

export type AuthStatus = 'loading' | 'signedOut' | 'signedIn';

interface AuthContextValue {
  status: AuthStatus;
  user: Applicant | null;
  api: Endpoints;
  login(username: string, password: string): Promise<void>;
  logout(): Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

export function AuthProvider({
  children,
  tokenStore = secureTokenStore,
  fetchFn,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  tokenStore?: TokenStore;
  fetchFn?: typeof fetch;
}) {
  const { ready, serverUrl } = useSettings();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const urlRef = useRef(serverUrl);
  urlRef.current = serverUrl;
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AuthStatus>('loading');
  const [user, setUser] = useState<Applicant | null>(null);

  const api = useMemo(
    () =>
      createEndpoints(
        createApiClient({
          baseUrl: () => urlRef.current,
          tokens: tokenStore,
          fetchFn,
          onAuthLost: () => {
            setUser(null);
            setStatus('signedOut');
          },
        }),
      ),
    [tokenStore, fetchFn],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ready) return;
    let alive = true;
    void (async () => {
      const tokens = await tokenStore.get();
      if (!tokens || !urlRef.current) {
        if (alive) setStatus('signedOut');
        return;
      }
      try {
        const me = await api.me();
        if (!alive) return;
        setUser(me);
        setStatus('signedIn');
      } catch (e) {
        if (!alive) return;
        if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 401) await tokenStore.clear();
        setStatus('signedOut');
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [ready, api, tokenStore]);

  const login = useCallback(
    async (username: string, password: string) => {
      const res = await api.login(username, password);
      await tokenStore.set({ accessToken: res.accessToken, refreshToken: res.refreshToken });
      setUser(res.user);
      setStatus('signedIn');
    },
    [api, tokenStore],
  );

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    await tokenStore.clear();
    queryClient.clear();
    setUser(null);
    setStatus('signedOut');
  }, [tokenStore, queryClient]);

  const value = useMemo(() => ({ status, user, api, login, logout }), [status, user, api, login, logout]);
  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const v = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!v) throw new Error('useAuth 必須在 AuthProvider 內使用');
  return v;
}

export function useApi(): Endpoints {
  return useAuth().api;
}
```

```tsx
/** 頂部列：標誌、門市（固定一間）、通知數、店員資訊；窄版只留標誌、通知與頭像。 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useApi, useAuth } from '@/auth/AuthProvider';
import { BrandLogo } from '@/brand/Mascot';
import { useIsWide } from '@/layout/useIsWide';
import { colors, font, radius, space } from '@/theme/tokens';

export const STORE_NAME = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_STORE_NAME || '中山門市';

export function TopBar() {
  const wide = useIsWide();
  const api = useApi();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const notifications = useQuery({ queryKey: ['notifications'], queryFn: () => api.notifications(), refetchInterval: 60_000 });
  const unread = notifications.data?.unreadCount ?? 0;
  const initial = (user?.name ?? '?').slice(0, 1);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView edges={['top']} style={styles.safe}>
      <View style={styles.bar}>
        <BrandLogo height={wide ? 32 : 26} />
        {wide && (
          <View style={styles.store}>
            <Ionicons name="storefront-outline" size={16} color={colors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={styles.storeText}>{STORE_NAME}</Text>
          </View>
        )}
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }} />
        <View accessibilityLabel={`通知 ${unread} 則未讀`} style={styles.bell}>
          <Ionicons name="notifications-outline" size={24} color={colors.text} />
          {unread > 0 && (
            <View style={styles.dot}>
              <Text style={styles.dotText}>{unread > 99 ? '99+' : unread}</Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
        <View style={styles.avatar}>
          <Text style={styles.avatarText}>{initial}</Text>
        </View>
        {wide && (
          <View>
            <Text style={styles.name}>{user?.name ?? ''}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.role}>{user?.title ?? '店員'}</Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safe: { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: colors.border },
  bar: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.md, paddingHorizontal: space.lg, paddingVertical: space.sm },
  store: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, backgroundColor: colors.bg, borderRadius: radius.md, paddingHorizontal: space.md, paddingVertical: 6 },
  storeText: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  bell: { padding: 4 },
  dot: { position: 'absolute', top: -2, right: -4, minWidth: 18, height: 18, borderRadius: 9, backgroundColor: colors.danger, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', paddingHorizontal: 4 },
  dotText: { color: '#fff', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '700' },
  avatar: { width: 36, height: 36, borderRadius: 18, backgroundColor: colors.primarySoft, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  avatarText: { color: colors.primaryDark, fontWeight: '700', fontSize: font.md },
  name: { fontSize: font.md, fontWeight: '600', color: colors.text },
  role: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.textMuted },
});
```

```tsx
/** 根版面：資料快取、設定、登入、任務紅點；未登入只能到 /login。 */
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Stack } from 'expo-router';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from '@/auth/AuthProvider';
import { SplashView } from '@/chrome/SplashView';
import { SettingsProvider } from '@/settings/SettingsProvider';
import { TaskBadgeProvider } from '@/tasks/TaskBadge';

const queryClient = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: 1, staleTime: 30_000 } } });

function RootStack() {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  if (status === 'loading') return <SplashView />;
  const signedIn = status === 'signedIn';
  return (
    <Stack screenOptions={{ headerShown: false }}>
      <Stack.Protected guard={signedIn}>
        <Stack.Screen name="(tabs)" />
      </Stack.Protected>
      <Stack.Protected guard={!signedIn}>
        <Stack.Screen name="login" />
      </Stack.Protected>
    </Stack>
  );
}

export default function RootLayout() {
  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <SettingsProvider>
        <AuthProvider>
          <TaskBadgeProvider>
            <StatusBar style="dark" />
            <RootStack />
          </TaskBadgeProvider>
        </AuthProvider>
      </SettingsProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}
```

`app/app/login.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 登入：帳號密碼；伺服器位址可展開修改（區網 Demo 用）。 */
import { useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
import { errorMessage } from '@/api/errors';
import { useAuth } from '@/auth/AuthProvider';
import { Mascot } from '@/brand/Mascot';
import { useSettings } from '@/settings/SettingsProvider';
import { colors, font, radius, shadow, space } from '@/theme/tokens';

export default function Login() {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const { serverUrl, setServerUrl } = useSettings();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [server, setServer] = useState(serverUrl);
  const [showServer, setShowServer] = useState(!serverUrl);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function submit() {
    if (busy || !username.trim() || !password) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      if (server !== serverUrl) await setServerUrl(server);
      await login(username.trim(), password);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined} style={styles.page}>
      <View style={styles.card}>
        <Mascot pose="wave" size={120} />
        <Text style={styles.title}>門市小幫手</Text>
        <Text style={styles.subtitle}>歡迎回來！請先登入</Text>
        <TextInput style={styles.input} placeholder="帳號" autoCapitalize="none" autoCorrect={false} value={username} onChangeText={setUsername} />
        <TextInput style={styles.input} placeholder="密碼" secureTextEntry value={password} onChangeText={setPassword} onSubmitEditing={submit} />
        {showServer ? (
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            placeholder="伺服器位址，例如 http://192.168.1.10:3000"
            autoCapitalize="none"
            autoCorrect={false}
            keyboardType="url"
            value={server}
            onChangeText={setServer}
          />
        ) : (
          <Pressable onPress={() => setShowServer(true)}>
            <Text style={styles.link}>伺服器設定</Text>
          </Pressable>
        )}
        {error && <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text>}
        <Pressable style={[styles.button, busy && { opacity: 0.6 }]} onPress={submit} disabled={busy} accessibilityRole="button">
          {busy ? <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" /> : <Text style={styles.buttonText}>登入</Text>}
        </Pressable>
      </View>
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  page: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: space.lg },
  card: { width: '100%', maxWidth: 420, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.xl, padding: space.xl, alignItems: 'center', gap: space.md, ...shadow.card },
  title: { fontSize: font.xxl, fontWeight: '700', color: colors.text },
  subtitle: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.textMuted, marginBottom: space.sm },
  input: { alignSelf: 'stretch', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, borderRadius: radius.md, paddingHorizontal: space.md, paddingVertical: 12, fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  link: { color: colors.primaryDark, fontSize: font.sm },
  error: { alignSelf: 'stretch', color: colors.danger, fontSize: font.sm },
  button: { alignSelf: 'stretch', backgroundColor: colors.primary, borderRadius: radius.md, paddingVertical: 14, alignItems: 'center' },
  buttonText: { color: '#fff', fontSize: font.lg, fontWeight: '700' },
});
```

```tsx
}
```

### Task 7：對話狀態（reducer 與 ChatProvider）

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/chat/types.ts`、`app/src/chat/chatReducer.ts`、`app/src/chat/ChatProvider.tsx`
