**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useApi()`、`Endpoints`（Task 5／6）；`ApiError`、`errorMessage`；`useTaskBadge()`；React Query `useQueryClient()`。
- Produces:
  - `ChatMessage`、`ChatState`（`{ conversationId, messages, pending: PendingActionView | null, sending }`）
  - `chatReducer`、`initialChatState`、`ChatAction`
  - `<ChatProvider api?>`；`useChat(): { state, send(text): Promise<boolean>, newConversation(), propose(toolId, input): Promise<void>, confirm(input?): Promise<ConfirmOutcome>, cancel(): Promise<void> }`
  - `ConfirmOutcome = { kind: 'done'; ok: boolean } | { kind: 'invalid'; message: string } | { kind: 'unavailable'; message: string } | { kind: 'gone' }`
  - 補貨等寫入成功後：`taskBadge.bump()`，並讓 React Query 的 `['todaySummary']`、`['replenishments']` 失效。

- [ ] **Step 1：寫失敗的測試**

`app/src/chat/chatReducer.test.ts`：

```ts
import type { PendingActionView } from '@hyagent/shared';
import { chatReducer, initialChatState } from './chatReducer';

const action: PendingActionView = { id: 'a1', summary: { title: '建立補貨單', fields: [] }, expiresAt: '2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z' };

describe('chatReducer', () => {
  it('送出 → 加使用者訊息、sending；收到回合 → 加 AI 訊息（含表格）、記下對話 id 與待確認動作', () => {
    let s = chatReducer(initialChatState, { type: 'userSent', text: '查庫存' });
    expect(s.sending).toBe(true);
    expect(s.messages[0]).toMatchObject({ role: 'user', text: '查庫存' });
    const block = { type: 'table' as const, columns: [], rows: [] };
    s = chatReducer(s, { type: 'turnReceived', conversationId: 'c1', turn: { status: 'collecting', reply: '查到了', blocks: [block], suggestions: ['補貨'], pendingAction: action } });
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ sending: false, conversationId: 'c1', pending: action });
    expect(s.messages[1]).toMatchObject({ role: 'agent', text: '查到了', blocks: [block], suggestions: ['補貨'] });
  });

  it('沒有 reply 不加訊息；新回合沒有待確認動作 → 清掉舊的', () => {
    let s = { ...initialChatState, conversationId: 'c1', pending: action };
    s = chatReducer(s, { type: 'turnReceived', turn: { status: 'collecting', reply: null } });
    expect(s.messages).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(s.pending).toBeNull();
    expect(s.conversationId).toBe('c1');
  });

  it('送出失敗 → 錯誤系統訊息；動作完成／取消／失效 → 清掉待確認', () => {
    let s = chatReducer({ ...initialChatState, sending: true }, { type: 'sendFailed', message: '無法連線' });
    expect(s.sending).toBe(false);
    expect(s.messages.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ role: 'system', tone: 'error', text: '無法連線' });
    s = chatReducer({ ...s, pending: action }, { type: 'actionFinished', text: '已完成「建立補貨單」。' });
    expect(s.pending).toBeNull();
    expect(s.messages.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ role: 'agent', text: '已完成「建立補貨單」。' });
    s = chatReducer({ ...s, pending: action }, { type: 'actionGone', message: '已逾時' });
    expect(s.pending).toBeNull();
    expect(s.messages.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ role: 'system', tone: 'info' });
  });

  it('提議動作記下對話 id；reset 回到初始', () => {
    let s = chatReducer(initialChatState, { type: 'actionProposed', conversationId: 'c9', action });
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ conversationId: 'c9', pending: action });
    s = chatReducer(s, { type: 'reset' });
    expect(s).toEqual(initialChatState);
  });
});
```

`app/src/chat/ChatProvider.test.tsx`：

```tsx
import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { PendingActionView } from '@hyagent/shared';
import type { Endpoints } from '@/api/endpoints';
import { ApiError } from '@/api/errors';
import { TaskBadgeProvider, useTaskBadge } from '@/tasks/TaskBadge';
import { ChatProvider, useChat } from './ChatProvider';

const pending: PendingActionView = { id: 'a1', summary: { title: '建立補貨單', fields: [] }, expiresAt: '2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z' };

function fakeApi(over: Partial<Endpoints> = {}): Endpoints {
  return {
    startConversation: jest.fn(async (m?: string) => ({ id: 'c1', status: 'collecting', reply: m ? `回覆：${m}` : null })),
    sendMessage: jest.fn(async (_id: string, m: string) => ({ status: 'collecting', reply: `回覆：${m}` })),
    proposeAction: jest.fn(async () => ({ pendingAction: pending })),
    confirmAction: jest.fn(async () => ({ status: 'collecting', reply: '已完成「建立補貨單」。', actionOk: true })),
    cancelAction: jest.fn(async () => ({ status: 'collecting', reply: '已取消「建立補貨單」。' })),
    ...over,
  } as unknown as Endpoints;
}

function setup(api: Endpoints) {
  const qc = new QueryClient();
  const invalidate = jest.spyOn(qc, 'invalidateQueries');
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => (
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <TaskBadgeProvider>
        <ChatProvider api={api}>{children}</ChatProvider>
      </TaskBadgeProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
  const hook = renderHook(() => ({ chat: useChat(), badge: useTaskBadge() }), { wrapper });
  return { ...hook, invalidate };
}

describe('ChatProvider', () => {
  it('第一句建立對話，之後用同一個對話送訊息', async () => {
    const api = fakeApi();
    const { result } = setup(api);
    await act(async () => { await result.current.chat.send('你好'); });
    await act(async () => { await result.current.chat.send('查庫存'); });
    expect(api.startConversation).toHaveBeenCalledWith('你好');
    expect(api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledWith('c1', '查庫存');
    expect(result.current.chat.state.messages.map((m) => m.text)).toEqual(['你好', '回覆：你好', '查庫存', '回覆：查庫存']);
  });

  it('空白訊息不送；送出中再送被擋下', async () => {
    let release!: () => void;
    const api = fakeApi({
      startConversation: jest.fn(() => new Promise((r) => { release = () => r({ id: 'c1', status: 'collecting', reply: 'ok' }); })) as never,
    });
    const { result } = setup(api);
    let first!: Promise<boolean>;
    await act(async () => {
      expect(await result.current.chat.send('   ')).toBe(false);
      first = result.current.chat.send('一');
      expect(await result.current.chat.send('二')).toBe(false);
      release();
      await first;
    });
    expect(api.startConversation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('對話已不存在（404）→ 自動重開一個對話再送', async () => {
    const api = fakeApi({ sendMessage: jest.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError('not found', 404, 'NOT_FOUND'); }) as never });
    const { result } = setup(api);
    await act(async () => { await result.current.chat.send('一'); });
    await act(async () => { expect(await result.current.chat.send('二')).toBe(true); });
    expect(api.startConversation).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('二');
  });

  it('送出失敗 → 回 false、加錯誤訊息', async () => {
    const api = fakeApi({ startConversation: jest.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError('無法連線到伺服器，請檢查網路', 0, 'NETWORK'); }) as never });
    const { result } = setup(api);
    await act(async () => { expect(await result.current.chat.send('一')).toBe(false); });
    expect(result.current.chat.state.messages.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ role: 'system', tone: 'error' });
  });

  it('propose：沒有對話時先建立空對話，再記下待確認動作', async () => {
    const api = fakeApi();
    const { result } = setup(api);
    await act(async () => { await result.current.chat.propose('mcp.store-demo.replenishment_create', { items: [] }); });
    expect(api.startConversation).toHaveBeenCalledWith();
    expect(api.proposeAction).toHaveBeenCalledWith('c1', 'mcp.store-demo.replenishment_create', { items: [] });
    expect(result.current.chat.state.pending).toEqual(pending);
  });

  it('confirm 成功 → done、紅點 +1、今日重點與補貨單失效、加結果訊息', async () => {
    const api = fakeApi();
    const { result, invalidate } = setup(api);
    await act(async () => { await result.current.chat.propose('t', {}); });
    let outcome: unknown;
    await act(async () => { outcome = await result.current.chat.confirm({ items: [] }); });
    expect(outcome).toEqual({ kind: 'done', ok: true });
    expect(api.confirmAction).toHaveBeenCalledWith('c1', 'a1', { items: [] });
    expect(result.current.badge.count).toBe(1);
    expect(invalidate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: ['todaySummary'] });
    expect(invalidate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: ['replenishments'] });
    expect(result.current.chat.state.pending).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each([
    [new ApiError('數量太大', 422, 'ACTION_INPUT_INVALID'), { kind: 'invalid', message: '數量太大' }, true],
    [new ApiError('稍後再試', 503, 'TOOL_UNAVAILABLE'), { kind: 'unavailable', message: '稍後再試' }, true],
    [new ApiError('逾時', 409, 'ACTION_EXPIRED'), { kind: 'gone' }, false],
  ])('confirm 錯誤 %p → %p（待確認動作是否保留：%p）', async (err, expected, keeps) => {
    const api = fakeApi({ confirmAction: jest.fn(async () => { throw err; }) as never });
    const { result } = setup(api);
    await act(async () => { await result.current.chat.propose('t', {}); });
    let outcome: unknown;
    await act(async () => { outcome = await result.current.chat.confirm(); });
    expect(outcome).toEqual(expected);
    expect(result.current.chat.state.pending !== null).toBe(keeps);
    expect(result.current.badge.count).toBe(0);
  });

  it('cancel → 清掉待確認、加取消訊息、不加紅點', async () => {
    const api = fakeApi();
    const { result } = setup(api);
    await act(async () => { await result.current.chat.propose('t', {}); });
    await act(async () => { await result.current.chat.cancel(); });
    expect(api.cancelAction).toHaveBeenCalledWith('c1', 'a1');
    expect(result.current.chat.state.pending).toBeNull();
    expect(result.current.chat.state.messages.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ text: '已取消「建立補貨單」。' });
    expect(result.current.badge.count).toBe(0);
  });

  it('newConversation 清空對話', async () => {
    const { result } = setup(fakeApi());
    await act(async () => { await result.current.chat.send('一'); });
    act(() => result.current.chat.newConversation());
    expect(result.current.chat.state.conversationId).toBeNull();
    expect(result.current.chat.state.messages).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`app/src/chat/types.ts`：

```ts
import type { DisplayBlock, PendingActionView } from '@hyagent/shared';

export type ChatMessage =
  | { id: string; role: 'user'; text: string; at: number }
  | { id: string; role: 'agent'; text: string; blocks?: DisplayBlock[]; suggestions?: string[]; at: number }
  | { id: string; role: 'system'; text: string; tone: 'info' | 'error'; at: number };

export interface ChatState {
  conversationId: string | null;
  messages: ChatMessage[];
  pending: PendingActionView | null;
  sending: boolean;
}

let seq = 0;
export function nextId(): string {
  seq += 1;
  return `m${Date.now().toString(36)}${seq.toString(36)}`;
}
```

`app/src/chat/chatReducer.ts`：

```ts
/** 對話狀態轉換（純函式）：訊息串、對話 id、目前待確認的寫入動作。 */
import type { PendingActionView } from '@hyagent/shared';
import type { TurnData } from '@/api/types';
import { nextId, type ChatMessage, type ChatState } from './types';

export type ChatAction =
  | { type: 'reset' }
  | { type: 'userSent'; text: string }
  | { type: 'turnReceived'; conversationId?: string; turn: TurnData }
  | { type: 'sendFailed'; message: string }
  | { type: 'conversationLost' }
  | { type: 'actionProposed'; conversationId: string; action: PendingActionView }
  | { type: 'actionFinished'; text: string }
  | { type: 'actionGone'; message: string };

export const initialChatState: ChatState = { conversationId: null, messages: [], pending: null, sending: false };

function system(text: string, tone: 'info' | 'error'): ChatMessage {
  return { id: nextId(), role: 'system', text, tone, at: Date.now() };
}

export function chatReducer(state: ChatState, action: ChatAction): ChatState {
  switch (action.type) {
    case 'reset':
      return initialChatState;
    case 'userSent':
      return { ...state, sending: true, messages: [...state.messages, { id: nextId(), role: 'user', text: action.text, at: Date.now() }] };
    case 'turnReceived': {
      const t = action.turn;
      const messages: ChatMessage[] = t.reply
        ? [...state.messages, { id: nextId(), role: 'agent', text: t.reply, blocks: t.blocks, suggestions: t.suggestions, at: Date.now() }]
        : state.messages;
      // 後端在新回合會作廢上一輪未確認的動作：沒帶就清掉
      return { ...state, sending: false, conversationId: action.conversationId ?? state.conversationId, messages, pending: t.pendingAction ?? null };
    }
    case 'sendFailed':
      return { ...state, sending: false, messages: [...state.messages, system(action.message, 'error')] };
    case 'conversationLost':
      return { ...state, conversationId: null, pending: null };
    case 'actionProposed':
      return { ...state, conversationId: action.conversationId, pending: action.action };
    case 'actionFinished':
      return {
        ...state,
        pending: null,
        messages: action.text ? [...state.messages, { id: nextId(), role: 'agent', text: action.text, at: Date.now() }] : state.messages,
      };
    case 'actionGone':
      return { ...state, pending: null, messages: [...state.messages, system(action.message, 'info')] };
    default:
      return state;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3：實作 ChatProvider**

`app/src/chat/ChatProvider.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * 對話：一個 App 工作階段一個對話；第一句建立對話，之後沿用。
 * 寫入動作的確認／取消也在這裡，成功後讓今日重點與補貨單重新取資料、任務分頁亮紅點。
 */
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useReducer, useRef, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { Endpoints } from '@/api/endpoints';
import { ApiError, errorMessage } from '@/api/errors';
import { useApi } from '@/auth/AuthProvider';
import { useTaskBadge } from '@/tasks/TaskBadge';
import { chatReducer, initialChatState } from './chatReducer';
import type { ChatState } from './types';

export type ConfirmOutcome =
  | { kind: 'done'; ok: boolean }
  | { kind: 'invalid'; message: string }
  | { kind: 'unavailable'; message: string }
  | { kind: 'gone' };

interface ChatContextValue {
  state: ChatState;
  send(text: string): Promise<boolean>;
  newConversation(): void;
  propose(toolId: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;
  confirm(input?: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<ConfirmOutcome>;
  cancel(): Promise<void>;
}

const ChatContext = createContext<ChatContextValue | null>(null);

export function ChatProvider({ children, api: injected }: { children: ReactNode; api?: Endpoints }) {
  const fromAuth = useApiOptional();
  const api = injected ?? fromAuth!;
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const badge = useTaskBadge();
  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(chatReducer, initialChatState);
  const stateRef = useRef(state);
  stateRef.current = state;
  // 同步旗標：dispatch 要等重新渲染才生效，同一個 tick 內連送兩次靠它擋
  const sendingRef = useRef(false);

  const startWith = useCallback(
    async (text: string) => {
      const d = await api.startConversation(text);
      dispatch({ type: 'turnReceived', conversationId: d.id, turn: d });
    },
    [api],
  );

  const send = useCallback(
    async (raw: string) => {
      const text = raw.trim();
      if (!text || sendingRef.current) return false;
      sendingRef.current = true;
      dispatch({ type: 'userSent', text });
      try {
        const id = stateRef.current.conversationId;
        if (!id) {
          await startWith(text);
        } else {
          try {
            const d = await api.sendMessage(id, text);
            dispatch({ type: 'turnReceived', turn: d });
          } catch (e) {
            // 對話已不存在（例如伺服器資料重置）：重開一個對話再送
            if (!(e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404)) throw e;
            dispatch({ type: 'conversationLost' });
            await startWith(text);
          }
        }
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
        dispatch({ type: 'sendFailed', message: errorMessage(e) });
        return false;
      } finally {
        sendingRef.current = false;
      }
    },
    [api, startWith],
  );

  const newConversation = useCallback(() => dispatch({ type: 'reset' }), []);

  const propose = useCallback(
    async (toolId: string, input: Record<string, unknown>) => {
      let id = stateRef.current.conversationId;
      if (!id) {
        const d = await api.startConversation();
        id = d.id;
        dispatch({ type: 'turnReceived', conversationId: id, turn: d });
      }
      const { pendingAction } = await api.proposeAction(id, toolId, input);
      dispatch({ type: 'actionProposed', conversationId: id, action: pendingAction });
    },
    [api],
  );

  const confirm = useCallback(
    async (input?: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<ConfirmOutcome> => {
      const { conversationId: id, pending } = stateRef.current;
      if (!id || !pending) return { kind: 'gone' };
      try {
        const turn = await api.confirmAction(id, pending.id, input);
        const ok = turn.actionOk !== false;
        dispatch({ type: 'actionFinished', text: turn.reply ?? '' });
        if (ok) {
          badge.bump();
          void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['todaySummary'] });
          void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['replenishments'] });
        }
        return { kind: 'done', ok };
      } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof ApiError) {
          if (e.status === 422 && e.code === 'ACTION_INPUT_INVALID') return { kind: 'invalid', message: e.message };
          if (e.status === 409) {
            dispatch({ type: 'actionGone', message: e.code === 'ACTION_EXPIRED' ? '這個動作已逾時失效，請重新提出。' : '這個動作已處理過或已不存在。' });
            return { kind: 'gone' };
          }
        }
        return { kind: 'unavailable', message: errorMessage(e) };
      }
    },
    [api, badge, queryClient],
  );

  const cancel = useCallback(async () => {
    const { conversationId: id, pending } = stateRef.current;
    if (!id || !pending) return;
    try {
      const turn = await api.cancelAction(id, pending.id);
      dispatch({ type: 'actionFinished', text: turn.reply ?? '' });
    } catch (e) {
      dispatch({ type: 'actionGone', message: e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 409 ? '這個動作已處理過或已不存在。' : errorMessage(e) });
    }
  }, [api]);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({ state, send, newConversation, propose, confirm, cancel }),
    [state, send, newConversation, propose, confirm, cancel],
  );
  return <ChatContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ChatContext.Provider>;
}

/** 測試可直接注入 api，不需要 AuthProvider */
function useApiOptional(): Endpoints | null {
  try {
    return useApi();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function useChat(): ChatContextValue {
  const v = useContext(ChatContext);
  if (!v) throw new Error('useChat 必須在 ChatProvider 內使用');
  return v;
}
```

（`useApiOptional` 每次渲染都呼叫 `useApi()` → `useContext`，hook 呼叫順序固定，符合 hooks 規則。）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useChat()`（Task 7）；`useSettings()`（朗讀開關）；`Mascot`、tokens、`useIsWide`。
- Produces:
  - `formatCell(col, value): string`、`cellTone(col, value): DisplayTone | null`、`imageUri(row, col): string | null`

  - `parseRichText(text): RichLine[]`、`<RichText text />`
  - `<ChatInput onSend(text): Promise<boolean> micAvailable onMicPress? disabled? inputRef? />`
  - `type AssistantMode = 'idle' | 'recording' | 'transcribing'`；`<AssistantPanel mode hint? compact micAvailable onMicPress onStopPress onKeyboard onCamera />`
  - `useAutoAsk(q?: string, nonce?: string, send: (text) => Promise<boolean>)`：同一個 `nonce` 只送一次
  - AI 對話路由參數：`q`（要問的問題）、`nonce`（每次導頁唯一值）、`focus`（`'1'` 時聚焦輸入框）、`voice`（`'1'` 時開始錄音，Task 10 接上）

`app/src/chat/tableFormat.test.ts`：

```ts
import type { TableColumn } from '@hyagent/shared';
import { cellTone, formatCell, imageUri } from './tableFormat';

const stock: TableColumn = { key: 'stock', label: '庫存', unit: '個' };
const delta: TableColumn = { key: 'suggested', label: '建議補貨', unit: '個', format: 'delta' };
const status: TableColumn = { key: 'status', label: '狀態', badge: { 庫存偏低: 'danger', 正常: 'success' } };
const name: TableColumn = { key: 'name', label: '商品', image: 'image' };

describe('formatCell', () => {
  it('一般值加單位；空值顯示 –', () => {
    expect(formatCell(stock, 6)).toBe('6 個');
    expect(formatCell(stock, null)).toBe('–');
    expect(formatCell(stock, '')).toBe('–');
    expect(formatCell(name, '鮭魚御飯糰')).toBe('鮭魚御飯糰');
  });

  it('delta：正數顯示 +N 單位，0、負數、非數字顯示 –', () => {
    expect(formatCell(delta, 24)).toBe('+24 個');
    expect(formatCell(delta, 0)).toBe('–');
    expect(formatCell(delta, -3)).toBe('–');
    expect(formatCell(delta, 'x')).toBe('–');
  });
});

describe('cellTone', () => {
  it('badge 依值取色，未列出的值為 neutral；delta 正數為 danger；其他沒有色調', () => {
    expect(cellTone(status, '庫存偏低')).toBe('danger');
    expect(cellTone(status, '其他')).toBe('neutral');
    expect(cellTone(delta, 24)).toBe('danger');
    expect(cellTone(delta, 0)).toBeNull();
    expect(cellTone(stock, 6)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('imageUri', () => {
  it('只接受 http(s)', () => {
    expect(imageUri({ image: 'https://s/a.png' }, name)).toBe('https://s/a.png');
    expect(imageUri({ image: 'javascript:alert(1)' }, name)).toBeNull();
    expect(imageUri({ image: 'https://s/a.png' }, stock)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { ChatInput } from './ChatInput';

describe('ChatInput', () => {
  it('送出成功清空輸入；失敗保留文字', async () => {
    const onSend = jest.fn().mockResolvedValueOnce(false).mockResolvedValueOnce(true);
    render(<ChatInput onSend={onSend} micAvailable={false} />);
    const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText('請說出或輸入你的需求…');
    fireEvent.changeText(input, '查庫存');
    await act(async () => { fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('送出')); });
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('查庫存')).toBeTruthy();
    await act(async () => { fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('送出')); });
    expect(onSend).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(screen.queryByDisplayValue('查庫存')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('麥克風可用時顯示並可按', () => {
    const onMicPress = jest.fn();
    render(<ChatInput onSend={jest.fn()} micAvailable onMicPress={onMicPress} />);
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('開始說話'));
    expect(onMicPress).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```ts
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2：實作 tableFormat 與 TableBlockView**

`app/src/chat/tableFormat.ts`：

```ts
/** 表格儲存格格式（與網頁版 DisplayBlockView 一致）。 */
import type { DisplayTone, TableColumn } from '@hyagent/shared';

type Cell = string | number | null | undefined;

export function formatCell(col: TableColumn, v: Cell): string {
  if (col.format === 'delta') {
    return typeof v === 'number' && v > 0 ? `+${v}${col.unit ? ` ${col.unit}` : ''}` : '–';
  }
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') return '–';
  return `${v}${col.unit ? ` ${col.unit}` : ''}`;
}

export function cellTone(col: TableColumn, v: Cell): DisplayTone | null {
  if (col.badge && v !== null && v !== undefined) {
    const key = String(v);
    return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(col.badge, key) ? col.badge[key] : 'neutral';
  }
  if (col.format === 'delta' && typeof v === 'number' && v > 0) return 'danger';
  return null;
}

export function imageUri(row: Record<string, unknown>, col: TableColumn): string | null {
  if (!col.image) return null;
  const u = row[col.image];
  return typeof u === 'string' && /^https?:\/\//i.test(u) ? u : null;
}
```

```tsx
/** 回覆中的表格（資料來自工具，不經 AI 轉述）；欄位多時可左右滑。 */
import { Image, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import type { DisplayBlock } from '@hyagent/shared';
import { colors, font, radius, space, toneColors } from '@/theme/tokens';
import { cellTone, formatCell, imageUri } from './tableFormat';

const MIN_COL = 96;

export function TableBlockView({ block }: { block: DisplayBlock }) {
  return (
    <View style={styles.card}>
      {block.title ? <Text style={styles.title}>{block.title}</Text> : null}
      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}>
        <View>
          <View style={[styles.row, styles.head]}>
            {block.columns.map((c, i) => (
              <Text key={c.key} style={[styles.cell, styles.headText, i === 0 && styles.first]}>{c.label}</Text>
            ))}
          </View>
          {block.rows.map((row, r) => (
            <View key={r} style={[styles.row, r > 0 && styles.divider]}>
              {block.columns.map((c, i) => {
                const v = row[c.key];
                const tone = cellTone(c, v);
                const uri = imageUri(row, c);
                const text = c.badge && v !== null && v !== undefined ? String(v) : formatCell(c, v);
                return (
                  <View key={c.key} style={[styles.cell, styles.cellRow, i === 0 && styles.first]}>
                    {uri ? <Image source={{ uri }} style={styles.thumb} /> : null}
                    {tone ? (
                      <Text style={[styles.pill, { color: toneColors[tone].fg, backgroundColor: toneColors[tone].bg }]}>{text}</Text>
                    ) : (
                      <Text style={styles.value}>{text}</Text>
                    )}
                  </View>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  card: { marginTop: space.sm, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.lg, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, paddingVertical: space.sm },
  title: { fontSize: font.md, fontWeight: '700', color: colors.text, paddingHorizontal: space.md, paddingBottom: space.sm },
  row: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' },
  head: { backgroundColor: colors.bg },
  divider: { borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopColor: colors.border },
  cell: { minWidth: MIN_COL, paddingHorizontal: space.md, paddingVertical: 10 },
  first: { minWidth: 168 },
  cellRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.sm },
  headText: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.textMuted },
  value: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  pill: { fontSize: font.sm, fontWeight: '700', paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: radius.sm, overflow: 'hidden' },
  thumb: { width: 36, height: 36, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: colors.bg },
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3：實作 RichText、MessageBubble**

`app/src/chat/RichText.tsx`：

```tsx
/** AI 回覆的簡易排版：只處理條列（- 、* 、1. ）與 **粗體**，其餘 markdown 符號照原文顯示。 */
import { StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { colors, font } from '@/theme/tokens';

export interface RichLine {
  bullet: boolean;
  parts: { text: string; bold: boolean }[];
}

export function parseRichText(text: string): RichLine[] {
  return text
    .split('\n')
    .map((l) => l.trimEnd())
    .filter((l) => l.trim() !== '')
    .map((line) => {
      const m = /^\s*(?:[-*•]|\d+\.)\s+(.*)$/.exec(line);
      const body = m ? m[1] : line;
      const parts = body
        .split(/(\*\*[^*]+\*\*)/)
        .filter((p) => p !== '')
        .map((p) => (p.startsWith('**') && p.endsWith('**') ? { text: p.slice(2, -2), bold: true } : { text: p, bold: false }));
      return { bullet: Boolean(m), parts };
    });
}

export function RichText({ text, color = colors.text }: { text: string; color?: string }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: 4 }}>
      {parseRichText(text).map((line, i) => (
        <View key={i} style={styles.line}>
          {line.bullet ? <Text style={[styles.text, { color }]}>•</Text> : null}
          <Text style={[styles.text, { color, flex: 1 }]}>
            {line.parts.map((p, j) => (
              <Text key={j} style={p.bold ? styles.bold : undefined}>{p.text}</Text>
            ))}
          </Text>
        </View>
      ))}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  line: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 },
  text: { fontSize: font.md, lineHeight: 22 },
  bold: { fontWeight: '700' },
});
```

`app/src/chat/MessageBubble.tsx`：

```tsx
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Mascot } from '@/brand/Mascot';
import { colors, font, radius, shadow, space } from '@/theme/tokens';
import { RichText } from './RichText';
import { TableBlockView } from './TableBlockView';
import type { ChatMessage } from './types';

function time(at: number): string {
  const d = new Date(at);
  return `${String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

export function MessageBubble({
  message,
  showSuggestions,
  onSuggestion,
}: {
  message: ChatMessage;
  showSuggestions: boolean;
  onSuggestion(text: string): void;
}) {
  if (message.role === 'system') {
    return (
      <Text style={[styles.system, message.tone === 'error' && { color: colors.danger }]} accessibilityRole="alert">
        {message.text}
      </Text>
    );
  }
  if (message.role === 'user') {
    return (
      <View style={styles.userWrap}>
        <View style={styles.userBubble}>
          <Text style={styles.userText}>{message.text}</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.time}>{time(message.at)}</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }
  return (
    <View style={styles.agentWrap}>
      <Mascot pose="wave" size={36} />
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <View style={styles.agentBubble}>
          <RichText text={message.text} />
          {message.blocks?.map((b, i) => <TableBlockView key={i} block={b} />)}
        </View>
        {showSuggestions && message.suggestions && message.suggestions.length > 0 ? (
          <View style={styles.chips}>
            {message.suggestions.map((s) => (
              <Pressable key={s} style={styles.chip} onPress={() => onSuggestion(s)} accessibilityRole="button">
                <Text style={styles.chipText}>{s}</Text>
              </Pressable>
            ))}
          </View>
        ) : null}
        <Text style={styles.time}>{time(message.at)}</Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  system: { alignSelf: 'center', fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.textMuted, marginVertical: space.sm, textAlign: 'center' },
  userWrap: { alignItems: 'flex-end', marginVertical: space.sm },
  userBubble: { maxWidth: '80%', backgroundColor: colors.primarySoft, borderRadius: radius.lg, borderBottomRightRadius: 4, paddingHorizontal: space.lg, paddingVertical: space.md },
  userText: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  agentWrap: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: space.sm, marginVertical: space.sm, alignItems: 'flex-start' },
  agentBubble: { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.lg, borderTopLeftRadius: 4, padding: space.md, ...shadow.card },
  time: { fontSize: 11, color: colors.textMuted, marginTop: 4 },
  chips: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: space.sm, marginTop: space.sm },
  chip: { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.pill, paddingHorizontal: space.md, paddingVertical: 6 },
  chipText: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.text },
});
```

```tsx
/** 輸入列：送出成功才清空（失敗保留文字）；麥克風可用時顯示在左邊。 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useState, type RefObject } from 'react';
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
import { colors, font, radius, space } from '@/theme/tokens';

export function ChatInput({
  onSend,
  micAvailable,
  onMicPress,
  disabled,
  inputRef,
}: {
  onSend(text: string): Promise<boolean>;
  micAvailable: boolean;
  onMicPress?: () => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  inputRef?: RefObject<TextInput | null>;
}) {
  const [text, setText] = useState('');

  async function submit() {
    if (disabled || !text.trim()) return;
    const ok = await onSend(text);
    if (ok) setText('');
  }

  return (
    <View style={styles.bar}>
      {micAvailable ? (
        <Pressable style={styles.mic} onPress={onMicPress} accessibilityLabel="開始說話" accessibilityRole="button">
          <Ionicons name="mic" size={22} color="#fff" />
        </Pressable>
      ) : null}
      <TextInput
        ref={inputRef}
        style={styles.input}
        placeholder="請說出或輸入你的需求…"
        placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
        value={text}
        onChangeText={setText}
        onSubmitEditing={submit}
        returnKeyType="send"
        editable={!disabled}
      />
      <Pressable style={[styles.send, (!text.trim() || disabled) && { opacity: 0.4 }]} onPress={submit} accessibilityLabel="送出" accessibilityRole="button">
        <Ionicons name="send" size={20} color={colors.primaryDark} />
      </Pressable>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  bar: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.sm, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.pill, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, padding: 6 },
  mic: { width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 22, backgroundColor: colors.primary, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  input: { flex: 1, fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text, paddingHorizontal: space.sm, paddingVertical: 10 },
  send: { width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 22, backgroundColor: colors.primarySoft, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
});
```

`app/src/chat/AssistantPanel.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * 助理面板（稿 3 左半）：待機、聆聽中、辨識中三種狀態。
 * compact＝窄版，改成橫條放在對話上方。
 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { ActivityIndicator, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Mascot } from '@/brand/Mascot';
import { colors, font, radius, space } from '@/theme/tokens';

export type AssistantMode = 'idle' | 'recording' | 'transcribing';

export const VOICE_EXAMPLES = ['「查詢御飯糰的庫存」', '「建立補貨單」', '「今天有哪些即期品？」', '「咖啡機紅燈怎麼處理？」'];

const TITLE: Record<AssistantMode, string> = {
  idle: '按麥克風開始說話',
  recording: '正在聽你說話…',
  transcribing: '辨識中…',
};

export function AssistantPanel({
  mode,
  hint,
  compact,
  micAvailable,
  onMicPress,
  onStopPress,
  onKeyboard,
  onCamera,
}: {
  mode: AssistantMode;
  hint?: string | null;
  compact: boolean;
  micAvailable: boolean;
  onMicPress(): void;
  onStopPress(): void;
  onKeyboard(): void;
  onCamera(): void;
}) {
  const title = micAvailable ? TITLE[mode] : '用打字或拍照問我';
  const center =
    mode === 'recording' ? (
      <RoundButton label="結束對話" color={colors.danger} icon="stop" onPress={onStopPress} />
    ) : mode === 'transcribing' ? (
      <View style={[styles.round, { backgroundColor: colors.primarySoft }]}>
        <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primary} />
      </View>
    ) : micAvailable ? (
      <RoundButton label="開始說話" color={colors.primary} icon="mic" onPress={onMicPress} />
    ) : null;

  if (compact) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.compact}>
        <Mascot pose={mode === 'recording' ? 'mic' : 'idle'} size={44} />
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <Text style={styles.compactTitle}>{title}</Text>
          {hint ? <Text style={styles.hint}>{hint}</Text> : null}
        </View>
        <SmallButton label="拍照" icon="camera-outline" onPress={onCamera} />
        {center}
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={styles.panel}>
      <Mascot pose={mode === 'recording' ? 'mic' : 'idle'} size={180} />
      <Text style={styles.title}>{title}</Text>
      {hint ? <Text style={styles.hint}>{hint}</Text> : null}
      <View style={styles.examples}>
        <Text style={styles.examplesLabel}>你可以說：</Text>
        {VOICE_EXAMPLES.map((e) => (
          <Text key={e} style={styles.example}>{e}</Text>
        ))}
      </View>
      <View style={styles.buttons}>
        <SmallButton label="鍵盤輸入" icon="keypad-outline" onPress={onKeyboard} />
        {center}
        <SmallButton label="拍照" icon="camera-outline" onPress={onCamera} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

function RoundButton({ label, color, icon, onPress }: { label: string; color: string; icon: 'mic' | 'stop'; onPress(): void }) {
  return (
    <Pressable onPress={onPress} accessibilityLabel={label} accessibilityRole="button" style={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
      <View style={[styles.round, { backgroundColor: color }]}>
        <Ionicons name={icon} size={30} color="#fff" />
      </View>
      <Text style={styles.btnLabel}>{label}</Text>
    </Pressable>
  );
}

function SmallButton({ label, icon, onPress }: { label: string; icon: 'keypad-outline' | 'camera-outline'; onPress(): void }) {
  return (
    <Pressable onPress={onPress} accessibilityLabel={label} accessibilityRole="button" style={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
      <View style={styles.small}>
        <Ionicons name={icon} size={24} color={colors.text} />
      </View>
      <Text style={styles.btnLabel}>{label}</Text>
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  panel: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: space.xl, gap: space.md },
  title: { fontSize: font.xl, fontWeight: '700', color: colors.text },
  hint: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.danger },
  examples: { alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: 2 },
  examplesLabel: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.textMuted },
  example: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.text },
  buttons: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'flex-end', justifyContent: 'space-around', alignSelf: 'stretch', marginTop: space.lg },
  round: { width: 72, height: 72, borderRadius: 36, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  small: { width: 52, height: 52, borderRadius: 26, backgroundColor: colors.surface, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border },
  btnLabel: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.text },
  compact: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.md, padding: space.md, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: colors.border },
  compactTitle: { fontSize: font.md, fontWeight: '600', color: colors.text },
});
```

`app/src/chat/useAutoAsk.ts`：

```ts
/** 從首頁帶問題進 AI 對話：同一個 nonce 只送一次（避免畫面重新渲染時重送）。 */
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export function useAutoAsk(q: string | undefined, nonce: string | undefined, send: (text: string) => Promise<boolean>): void {
  const handled = useRef<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!q || !nonce || handled.current === nonce) return;
    handled.current = nonce;
    void send(q);
  }, [q, nonce, send]);
}
```

`app/app/(tabs)/chat/index.tsx`：

```tsx
/** AI 對話（稿 3）：寬版左助理面板、右對話；窄版助理橫條在上。語音在 Task 10 接上。 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { FlatList, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
import { AssistantPanel } from '@/chat/AssistantPanel';
import { ChatInput } from '@/chat/ChatInput';
import { useChat } from '@/chat/ChatProvider';
import { MessageBubble } from '@/chat/MessageBubble';
import { useAutoAsk } from '@/chat/useAutoAsk';
import { useIsWide } from '@/layout/useIsWide';
import { useSettings } from '@/settings/SettingsProvider';
import { colors, font, radius, shadow, space } from '@/theme/tokens';

export const GREETING = '嗨！我是 Open醬，可以幫你查庫存、建補貨單、查活動和優惠券，也能回答門市作業問題。';

export default function ChatScreen() {
  const wide = useIsWide();
  const chat = useChat();
  const { speakEnabled, setSpeakEnabled } = useSettings();
  const params = useLocalSearchParams<{ q?: string; nonce?: string; focus?: string }>();
  const inputRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const listRef = useRef<FlatList>(null);
  const { messages, sending } = chat.state;
  const lastAgentId = [...messages].reverse().find((m) => m.role === 'agent')?.id;

  useAutoAsk(params.q, params.nonce, chat.send);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (params.focus === '1') setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 300);
  }, [params.focus, params.nonce]);

  const openCamera = () => router.push({ pathname: '/camera', params: { mode: 'shelf' } });

  const panel = (
    <AssistantPanel
      mode="idle"
      compact={!wide}
      micAvailable={false}
      onMicPress={() => undefined}
      onStopPress={() => undefined}
      onKeyboard={() => inputRef.current?.focus()}
      onCamera={openCamera}
    />
  );

  const conversation = (
    <View style={[styles.chatCard, !wide && styles.chatCardNarrow]}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>AI 小幫手</Text>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }} />
        <Pressable onPress={() => setSpeakEnabled(!speakEnabled)} style={styles.headerBtn} accessibilityRole="switch" accessibilityState={{ checked: speakEnabled }} accessibilityLabel="朗讀">
          <Ionicons name={speakEnabled ? 'volume-high-outline' : 'volume-mute-outline'} size={18} color={colors.text} />
          <Text style={styles.headerBtnText}>朗讀 {speakEnabled ? '開' : '關'}</Text>
        </Pressable>
        <Pressable onPress={chat.newConversation} style={styles.headerBtn} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="新對話">
          <Ionicons name="refresh-outline" size={18} color={colors.text} />
          <Text style={styles.headerBtnText}>新對話</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
      <FlatList
        ref={listRef}
        data={messages}
        keyExtractor={(m) => m.id}
        contentContainerStyle={{ padding: space.md }}
        ListHeaderComponent={<Text style={styles.greeting}>{GREETING}</Text>}
        ListFooterComponent={sending ? <Text style={styles.typing}>Open醬思考中…</Text> : null}
        renderItem={({ item }) => <MessageBubble message={item} showSuggestions={item.id === lastAgentId && !sending} onSuggestion={chat.send} />}
        onContentSizeChange={() => listRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true })}
      />
      <View style={{ padding: space.md }}>
        <ChatInput onSend={chat.send} micAvailable={false} disabled={sending} inputRef={inputRef} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );

  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView style={styles.page} behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined} keyboardVerticalOffset={80}>
      {wide ? (
        <View style={styles.row}>
          <View style={{ width: '38%' }}>{panel}</View>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, padding: space.md }}>{conversation}</View>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          {panel}
          {conversation}
        </View>
      )}
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
```

```ts
import {
  buildInput, coerce, dateOptions, hasEmptyTable, initialValues, isChanged, isExpired, rawOf, validateLocal,
} from './confirmForm';
import { FIELDS } from './fixtures';

describe('confirmForm', () => {
  it('沒改：buildInput 只含表格與可編輯欄位，isChanged=false', () => {
    const v = initialValues(FIELDS);
    expect(buildInput(FIELDS, v, {})).toEqual({ items: (FIELDS[0] as { rows: unknown[] }).rows, arrivalDate: '2026-10-05' });
    expect(isChanged(FIELDS, v, {})).toBe(false);
  });

  it('取消勾選一列、改數量 → 只送剩下的列，isChanged=true', () => {
    const v = initialValues(FIELDS);
    const rows = (v.items as Record<string, unknown>[]).map((r, i) => (i === 0 ? { ...r, quantity: 36 } : r));
    const input = buildInput(FIELDS, { ...v, items: rows }, { items: [1] });
    expect(input.items).toEqual([{ productId: 'P001', image: 'https://s/P001.png', name: '鮭魚御飯糰', stock: 6, quantity: 36 }]);
    expect(isChanged(FIELDS, { ...v, items: rows }, { items: [1] })).toBe(true);
  });

  it('全部取消勾選 → hasEmptyTable', () => {
    expect(hasEmptyTable(FIELDS, initialValues(FIELDS), { items: [0, 1] })).toBe(true);
    expect(hasEmptyTable(FIELDS, initialValues(FIELDS), { items: [0] })).toBe(false);
  });

  it('validateLocal：數量非數字或超出範圍回錯誤訊息（只檢查勾選中的列）', () => {
    const v = initialValues(FIELDS);
    const bad = (q: unknown) => ({ ...v, items: (v.items as Record<string, unknown>[]).map((r, i) => (i === 0 ? { ...r, quantity: q } : r)) });
    expect(validateLocal(FIELDS, v, {})).toBeNull();
    expect(validateLocal(FIELDS, bad(0), {})).toBe('鮭魚御飯糰 的建議補貨數量需介於 1–200');
    expect(validateLocal(FIELDS, bad('abc'), {})).toBe('鮭魚御飯糰 的建議補貨數量需填數字');
    expect(validateLocal(FIELDS, bad(0), { items: [0] })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('coerce / rawOf', () => {
    expect(coerce('number', '12')).toBe(12);
    expect(coerce('number', '')).toBe('');
    expect(coerce('number', 'x')).toBe('x');
    expect(coerce('text', '12')).toBe('12');
    expect(rawOf([{ value: '1', label: '一', raw: 1 }], '1')).toBe(1);
    expect(rawOf(undefined, 'a')).toBe('a');
  });

  it('isExpired', () => {
    const a = { id: 'a', summary: { title: 't', fields: [] }, expiresAt: '2026-10-04T10:00:00.000Z' };
    expect(isExpired(a, Date.parse('2026-10-04T09:59:59.000Z'))).toBe(false);
    expect(isExpired(a, Date.parse('2026-10-04T10:00:00.000Z'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('dateOptions：從今天起 7 天，標示今天／明天；目前值不在範圍內時放第一個', () => {
    const opts = dateOptions('2026-10-04', 7);
    expect(opts).toHaveLength(7);
    expect(opts[0]).toEqual({ value: '2026-10-04', label: '2026/10/04（日）今天' });
    expect(opts[1]).toEqual({ value: '2026-10-05', label: '2026/10/05（一）明天' });
    expect(opts[6].value).toBe('2026-10-10');
    expect(dateOptions('2026-10-04', 7, '2026-10-20')[0]).toEqual({ value: '2026-10-20', label: '2026/10/20（二）' });
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import type { PendingActionView } from '@hyagent/shared';
import { FIELDS } from './fixtures';
import { ConfirmModal } from './ConfirmModal';

const NOW = Date.parse('2026-10-04T02:00:00.000Z');
const action: PendingActionView = {
  id: 'a1',
  summary: { title: '建立補貨單', fields: [] },
  form: { title: '建立補貨單', fields: FIELDS.slice(0, 2) },
  expiresAt: '2026-10-04T03:00:00.000Z',
};

describe('ConfirmModal', () => {
  it('改數量、取消一列後確認 → 送出修改後的內容', async () => {
    const onConfirm = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ kind: 'done', ok: true });
    render(<ConfirmModal action={action} onConfirm={onConfirm} onCancel={jest.fn()} now={NOW} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('我已準備好建立補貨單！')).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('鮭魚御飯糰 建議補貨數量'), '36');
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('選取 明太子御飯糰'));
    await act(async () => { fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('確認建立補貨單')); });
    expect(onConfirm).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      items: [{ productId: 'P001', image: 'https://s/P001.png', name: '鮭魚御飯糰', stock: 6, quantity: 36 }],
      arrivalDate: '2026-10-05',
    });
  });

  it('沒改 → 不帶內容確認', async () => {
    const onConfirm = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ kind: 'done', ok: true });
    render(<ConfirmModal action={action} onConfirm={onConfirm} onCancel={jest.fn()} now={NOW} />);
    await act(async () => { fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('確認建立補貨單')); });
    expect(onConfirm).toHaveBeenCalledWith(undefined);
  });

  it('連點兩下確認只送一次', async () => {
    let release!: (v: unknown) => void;
    const onConfirm = jest.fn(() => new Promise((r) => { release = r; }));
    render(<ConfirmModal action={action} onConfirm={onConfirm as never} onCancel={jest.fn()} now={NOW} />);
    await act(async () => {
      fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('確認建立補貨單'));
      fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('確認建立補貨單'));
    });
    expect(onConfirm).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await act(async () => { release({ kind: 'done', ok: true }); });
  });

  it('422 → 顯示錯誤、可修正後再送', async () => {
    const onConfirm = jest.fn().mockResolvedValueOnce({ kind: 'invalid', message: '數量超出範圍' }).mockResolvedValueOnce({ kind: 'done', ok: true });
    render(<ConfirmModal action={action} onConfirm={onConfirm} onCancel={jest.fn()} now={NOW} />);
    await act(async () => { fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('確認建立補貨單')); });
    expect(screen.getByText('數量超出範圍')).toBeTruthy();
    await act(async () => { fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('確認建立補貨單')); });
    expect(onConfirm).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it('本地檢查不通過 → 顯示原因、不呼叫 API', async () => {
    const onConfirm = jest.fn();
    render(<ConfirmModal action={action} onConfirm={onConfirm} onCancel={jest.fn()} now={NOW} />);
    fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('鮭魚御飯糰 建議補貨數量'), '0');
    await act(async () => { fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('確認建立補貨單')); });
    expect(screen.getByText('鮭魚御飯糰 的建議補貨數量需介於 1–200')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(onConfirm).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('全部取消勾選 → 確認鈕停用', async () => {
    const onConfirm = jest.fn();
    render(<ConfirmModal action={action} onConfirm={onConfirm} onCancel={jest.fn()} now={NOW} />);
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('全選'));
    await act(async () => { fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('確認建立補貨單')); });
    expect(onConfirm).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('已過期 → 顯示逾時、確認鈕不能按，關閉會呼叫 onCancel', async () => {
    const onConfirm = jest.fn();
    const onCancel = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    render(<ConfirmModal action={action} onConfirm={onConfirm} onCancel={onCancel} now={Date.parse('2026-10-04T04:00:00.000Z')} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('這個動作已逾時，請重新提出。')).toBeTruthy();
    await act(async () => { fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('確認建立補貨單')); });
    expect(onConfirm).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await act(async () => { fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('取消')); });
    expect(onCancel).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('沒有 action → 不顯示', () => {
    render(<ConfirmModal action={null} onConfirm={jest.fn()} onCancel={jest.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('取消')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2：實作 confirmForm 純函式**

`app/src/confirm/confirmForm.ts`：

```ts
/**
 * 確認表單的純邏輯（與網頁版 ConfirmActionCard 規則一致）：
 * 表格列可取消勾選；只送表格與可編輯的純量欄位（不可編輯欄位由後端以原值補齊）；沒有修改就不帶內容。
 */
import type { ConfirmField, ConfirmTableField, PendingActionView } from '@hyagent/shared';

export type Values = Record<string, unknown>;
/** 每個表格欄位被取消勾選的列索引 */
export type Removed = Record<string, number[]>;

type Row = Record<string, unknown>;

export function initialValues(fields: ConfirmField[]): Values {
  const v: Values = {};
  for (const f of fields) v[f.key] = f.kind === 'table' ? f.rows.map((r) => ({ ...r })) : f.value;
  return v;
}

export function coerce(kind: string, raw: string): unknown {
  if (kind !== 'number') return raw;
  const n = Number(raw);
  return raw.trim() === '' || Number.isNaN(n) ? raw : n;
}

/** 選項的顯示值還原成原本的型別（數字 enum 要送回數字） */
export function rawOf(options: { value: string; raw?: string | number | boolean }[] | undefined, value: string): unknown {
  const o = options?.find((x) => x.value === value);
  return o ? (o.raw ?? o.value) : value;
}

export function rowLabel(field: ConfirmTableField, row: Row, i: number): string {
  const col = field.columns.find((c) => !c.editable && typeof row[c.key] === 'string');
  return col ? String(row[col.key]) : `#${i + 1}`;
}

function keptRows(field: ConfirmTableField, values: Values, removed: Removed): Row[] {
  const gone = new Set(removed[field.key] ?? []);
  return (values[field.key] as Row[]).filter((_, i) => !gone.has(i));
}

export function buildInput(fields: ConfirmField[], values: Values, removed: Removed): Values {
  const out: Values = {};
  for (const f of fields) {
    if (f.kind === 'table') out[f.key] = keptRows(f, values, removed);
    else if (f.editable) out[f.key] = values[f.key];
  }
  return out;
}

export function isChanged(fields: ConfirmField[], values: Values, removed: Removed): boolean {
  const now = buildInput(fields, values, removed);
  const orig = buildInput(fields, initialValues(fields), {});
  return Object.keys(now).some((k) => JSON.stringify(now[k]) !== JSON.stringify(orig[k]));
}

/** 原本有列、被使用者全部取消勾選 */
export function hasEmptyTable(fields: ConfirmField[], values: Values, removed: Removed): boolean {
  return fields.some((f) => f.kind === 'table' && f.rows.length > 0 && keptRows(f, values, removed).length === 0);
}

function rangeError(label: string, v: unknown, min?: number, max?: number): string | null {
  if (typeof v !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(v)) return `${label}需填數字`;
  if ((min !== undefined && v < min) || (max !== undefined && v > max)) {
    return `${label}需介於 ${min ?? '-∞'}–${max ?? '∞'}`;
  }
  return null;
}

/** 送出前的本地檢查（只檢查勾選中的列）；通過回 null */
export function validateLocal(fields: ConfirmField[], values: Values, removed: Removed): string | null {
  for (const f of fields) {
    if (f.kind === 'table') {
      const gone = new Set(removed[f.key] ?? []);
      const rows = values[f.key] as Row[];
      for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
        if (gone.has(i)) continue;
        for (const c of f.columns) {
          if (!c.editable || c.kind !== 'number') continue;
          const err = rangeError(`${rowLabel(f, rows[i], i)} 的${c.label}`, rows[i][c.key], c.min, c.max);
          if (err) return err;
        }
      }
    } else if (f.editable && f.kind === 'number') {
      const err = rangeError(f.label, values[f.key], f.min, f.max);
      if (err) return err;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function isExpired(action: PendingActionView, now: number = Date.now()): boolean {
  return Date.parse(action.expiresAt) <= now;
}

/** 裝置時區的今天（YYYY-MM-DD） */
export function localToday(now: Date = new Date()): string {
  const y = now.getFullYear();
  const m = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const d = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
}

const WEEKDAY = ['日', '一', '二', '三', '四', '五', '六'];

function dateLabel(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`);
  return `${iso.replace(/-/g, '/')}（${WEEKDAY[d.getUTCDay()]}）`;
}

/** 到貨日期下拉：從今天起 days 天；目前值不在範圍內時放在第一個 */
export function dateOptions(today: string, days = 7, current?: string): { value: string; label: string }[] {
  const base = new Date(`${today}T00:00:00Z`);
  const opts = Array.from({ length: days }, (_, i) => {
    const d = new Date(base);
    d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + i);
    const iso = d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const tag = i === 0 ? '今天' : i === 1 ? '明天' : '';
    return { value: iso, label: `${dateLabel(iso)}${tag}` };
  });
  if (current && !opts.some((o) => o.value === current)) opts.unshift({ value: current, label: dateLabel(current) });
  return opts;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3：實作 ConfirmModal 與 Host**

`app/src/confirm/ConfirmModal.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * 補貨確認彈窗（稿 1）：依 ConfirmForm 產生——表格可勾選列、改數字，日期用下拉，其餘欄位依型別。
 * 一次性：送出中鎖住；422／服務不可用顯示原因並允許再送；逾時不能確認。
 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Image, Modal, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
import type { ConfirmField, ConfirmScalarField, ConfirmTableField, PendingActionView } from '@hyagent/shared';
import { Mascot } from '@/brand/Mascot';
import type { ConfirmOutcome } from '@/chat/ChatProvider';
import { colors, font, radius, shadow, space } from '@/theme/tokens';
import {
  buildInput, coerce, dateOptions, hasEmptyTable, initialValues, isChanged, isExpired, localToday, rawOf, rowLabel, validateLocal,
  type Removed, type Values,
} from './confirmForm';

type Row = Record<string, unknown>;

export function ConfirmModal({
  action,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
  now,
}: {
  action: PendingActionView | null;
  onConfirm(input?: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<ConfirmOutcome>;
  onCancel(): Promise<void>;
  /** 測試用：固定「現在」 */
  now?: number;
}) {
  if (!action) return null;
  return (
    <Modal transparent animationType="fade" visible onRequestClose={() => void onCancel()}>
      <ConfirmCard action={action} onConfirm={onConfirm} onCancel={onCancel} now={now} />
    </Modal>
  );
}

function ConfirmCard({
  action,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
  now,
}: {
  action: PendingActionView;
  onConfirm(input?: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<ConfirmOutcome>;
  onCancel(): Promise<void>;
  now?: number;
}) {
  const fields = useMemo(() => action.form?.fields ?? [], [action.form]);
  const title = action.form?.title ?? action.summary.title;
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Values>(() => initialValues(fields));
  const [removed, setRemoved] = useState<Removed>({});
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const inFlight = useRef(false);
  const expired = isExpired(action, now ?? Date.now());
  const empty = hasEmptyTable(fields, values, removed);
  const canConfirm = !busy && !expired && !empty;

  async function confirm() {
    if (!canConfirm || inFlight.current) return;
    const local = validateLocal(fields, values, removed);
    if (local) {
      setError(local);
      return;
    }
    inFlight.current = true;
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    const outcome = await onConfirm(isChanged(fields, values, removed) ? buildInput(fields, values, removed) : undefined);
    if (outcome.kind === 'invalid' || outcome.kind === 'unavailable') {
      setError(outcome.message);
      inFlight.current = false;
      setBusy(false);
    }
    // done／gone：待確認動作已清除，彈窗會隨之關閉
  }

  async function cancel() {
    if (inFlight.current) return;
    inFlight.current = true;
    setBusy(true);
    await onCancel();
  }

  function setCell(key: string, index: number, col: string, value: unknown) {
    setValues((v) => ({ ...v, [key]: (v[key] as Row[]).map((r, i) => (i === index ? { ...r, [col]: value } : r)) }));
  }

  function toggleRow(key: string, index: number) {
    setRemoved((r) => {
      const cur = new Set(r[key] ?? []);
      if (cur.has(index)) cur.delete(index);
      else cur.add(index);
      return { ...r, [key]: [...cur] };
    });
  }

  function toggleAll(field: ConfirmTableField) {
    setRemoved((r) => ({ ...r, [field.key]: (r[field.key]?.length ?? 0) > 0 ? [] : field.rows.map((_, i) => i) }));
  }

  return (
    <View style={styles.overlay}>
      <View style={styles.card}>
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <Mascot pose="wave" size={72} />
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={styles.brand}>Open醬</Text>
            <Text style={styles.title}>{`我已準備好${title}！`}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>請確認以下內容：</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        <ScrollView style={{ maxHeight: 460 }} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: space.md }}>
          {fields.length === 0
            ? action.summary.fields.map((f) => (
                <View key={f.label} style={styles.scalarRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.scalarLabel}>{f.label}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.scalarValue}>{f.value}</Text>
                </View>
              ))
            : fields.map((f) =>
                f.kind === 'table' ? (
                  <TableField key={f.key} field={f} rows={values[f.key] as Row[]} removed={removed[f.key] ?? []} disabled={busy || expired}
                    onToggle={(i) => toggleRow(f.key, i)} onToggleAll={() => toggleAll(f)} onCell={(i, c, v) => setCell(f.key, i, c, v)} />
                ) : (
                  <ScalarField key={f.key} field={f} value={values[f.key]} disabled={busy || expired}
                    onChange={(v) => setValues((cur) => ({ ...cur, [f.key]: v }))} />
                ),
              )}
        </ScrollView>
        {expired ? <Text style={styles.error}>這個動作已逾時，請重新提出。</Text> : null}
        {empty && !expired ? <Text style={styles.error}>請至少勾選一項。</Text> : null}
        {error ? <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text> : null}
        <View style={styles.footer}>
          <Pressable style={[styles.btn, styles.cancel]} onPress={cancel} accessibilityLabel="取消" accessibilityRole="button">
            <Text style={styles.cancelText}>{expired ? '關閉' : '取消'}</Text>
          </Pressable>
          <Pressable
            style={[styles.btn, styles.ok, !canConfirm && { opacity: 0.5 }]}
            onPress={confirm}
            accessibilityLabel={`確認${title}`}
            accessibilityRole="button"
            accessibilityState={{ disabled: !canConfirm }}
          >
            {busy ? <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" /> : <Ionicons name="checkmark-circle" size={22} color="#fff" />}
            <Text style={styles.okText}>{`確認${title}`}</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

function Check({ on, label, onPress, disabled }: { on: boolean; label: string; onPress(): void; disabled: boolean }) {
  return (
    <Pressable onPress={onPress} disabled={disabled} accessibilityLabel={label} accessibilityRole="checkbox" accessibilityState={{ checked: on }} hitSlop={8}>
      <Ionicons name={on ? 'checkbox' : 'square-outline'} size={24} color={on ? colors.info : colors.textMuted} />
    </Pressable>
  );
}

function TableField({
  field, rows, removed, disabled, onToggle, onToggleAll, onCell,
}: {
  field: ConfirmTableField;
  rows: Row[];
  removed: number[];
  disabled: boolean;
  onToggle(i: number): void;
  onToggleAll(): void;
  onCell(i: number, col: string, v: unknown): void;
}) {
  const gone = new Set(removed);
  return (
    <View style={styles.table}>
      <View style={[styles.tr, styles.thead]}>
        {field.selectable ? <Check on={gone.size === 0} label="全選" onPress={onToggleAll} disabled={disabled} /> : null}
        {field.columns.map((c, i) => (
          <Text key={c.key} style={[styles.th, i === 0 && styles.firstCol]}>{c.label}</Text>
        ))}
      </View>
      {rows.map((row, i) => {
        const off = gone.has(i);
        const name = rowLabel(field, row, i);
        return (
          <View key={i} style={[styles.tr, i > 0 && styles.divider, off && { opacity: 0.4 }]}>
            {field.selectable ? <Check on={!off} label={`選取 ${name}`} onPress={() => onToggle(i)} disabled={disabled} /> : null}
            {field.columns.map((c, ci) => {
              const v = row[c.key];
              const img = c.image && typeof row[c.image] === 'string' && /^https?:\/\//i.test(row[c.image] as string) ? (row[c.image] as string) : null;
              if (c.editable && (c.kind === 'number' || c.kind === 'text')) {
                return (
                  <View key={c.key} style={[styles.td, styles.editCell]}>
                    <TextInput
                      style={styles.qty}
                      value={v === undefined || v === null ? '' : String(v)}
                      keyboardType={c.kind === 'number' ? 'number-pad' : 'default'}
                      editable={!disabled && !off}
                      onChangeText={(t) => onCell(i, c.key, coerce(c.kind, t))}
                      accessibilityLabel={`${name} ${c.label}`}
                    />
                    {c.unit ? <Text style={styles.unit}>{c.unit}</Text> : null}
                  </View>
                );
              }
              return (
                <View key={c.key} style={[styles.td, ci === 0 && styles.firstCol, styles.cellRow]}>
                  {img ? <Image source={{ uri: img }} style={styles.thumb} /> : null}
                  <Text style={styles.cellText}>{v === undefined || v === null || v === '' ? '–' : `${v}${c.unit ? ` ${c.unit}` : ''}`}</Text>
                </View>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        );
      })}
    </View>
  );
}

function ScalarField({ field, value, disabled, onChange }: { field: ConfirmScalarField; value: unknown; disabled: boolean; onChange(v: unknown): void }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  if (!field.editable) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.scalarRow}>
        <Text style={styles.scalarLabel}>{field.label}</Text>
        <Text style={styles.scalarValue}>{value === undefined || value === null ? '–' : String(value)}</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }
  if (field.kind === 'date' || field.kind === 'select') {
    const options =
      field.kind === 'date'
        ? dateOptions(localToday(), 7, typeof value === 'string' ? value : undefined)
        : (field.options ?? []).map((o) => ({ value: o.value, label: o.label }));
    const current = options.find((o) => o.value === String(value ?? ''));
    return (
      <View style={styles.scalarRow}>
        <Ionicons name={field.kind === 'date' ? 'calendar-outline' : 'list-outline'} size={20} color={colors.textMuted} />
        <Text style={styles.scalarLabel}>{field.label}</Text>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <Pressable style={styles.select} onPress={() => setOpen(!open)} disabled={disabled} accessibilityLabel={field.label} accessibilityRole="button">
            <Text style={styles.selectText}>{current?.label ?? String(value ?? '請選擇')}</Text>
            <Ionicons name={open ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'} size={18} color={colors.text} />
          </Pressable>
          {open ? (
            <View style={styles.options}>
              {options.map((o) => (
                <Pressable key={o.value} style={styles.option} onPress={() => { onChange(field.kind === 'select' ? rawOf(field.options, o.value) : o.value); setOpen(false); }}>
                  <Text style={[styles.selectText, o.value === current?.value && { color: colors.primaryDark, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{o.label}</Text>
                </Pressable>
              ))}
            </View>
          ) : null}
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }
  return (
    <View style={styles.scalarRow}>
      <Text style={styles.scalarLabel}>{field.label}</Text>
      <TextInput
        style={[styles.select, { flex: 1 }]}
        value={value === undefined || value === null ? '' : String(value)}
        keyboardType={field.kind === 'number' ? 'number-pad' : 'default'}
        editable={!disabled}
        onChangeText={(t) => onChange(coerce(field.kind, t))}
        accessibilityLabel={field.label}
      />
      {field.unit ? <Text style={styles.unit}>{field.unit}</Text> : null}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  overlay: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.overlay, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: space.lg },
  card: { width: '100%', maxWidth: 720, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.xl, padding: space.xl, gap: space.md, ...shadow.card },
  header: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.md },
  brand: { color: colors.primary, fontWeight: '700', fontSize: font.sm },
  title: { fontSize: font.xl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },
  subtitle: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.textMuted },
  table: { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, borderRadius: radius.lg, overflow: 'hidden' },
  tr: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.md, paddingHorizontal: space.md, paddingVertical: 10 },
  thead: { backgroundColor: colors.bg },
  divider: { borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopColor: colors.border },
  th: { flex: 1, fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.textMuted, textAlign: 'center' },
  td: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center' },
  firstCol: { flex: 1.6, alignItems: 'flex-start', textAlign: 'left' },
  cellRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.sm },
  cellText: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  thumb: { width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: colors.bg },
  editCell: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 4 },
  qty: { minWidth: 72, textAlign: 'center', backgroundColor: colors.dangerSoft, color: colors.danger, fontWeight: '800', fontSize: font.lg, borderRadius: radius.md, paddingVertical: 8 },
  unit: { color: colors.danger, fontWeight: '700' },
  scalarRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.md },
  scalarLabel: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  scalarValue: { flex: 1, fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  select: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, borderRadius: radius.md, paddingHorizontal: space.md, paddingVertical: 12 },
  selectText: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  options: { marginTop: 4, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, borderRadius: radius.md, backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  option: { paddingHorizontal: space.md, paddingVertical: 10 },
  error: { color: colors.danger, fontSize: font.sm },

});
```

```tsx
/** 有待確認動作時，在任何分頁上跳出確認彈窗；key 綁動作 id，換新動作就重置編輯狀態。 */
import { useChat } from '@/chat/ChatProvider';
import { ConfirmModal } from './ConfirmModal';

export function ConfirmModalHost() {
  const chat = useChat();
  const pending = chat.state.pending;
  return <ConfirmModal key={pending?.id ?? 'none'} action={pending} onConfirm={chat.confirm} onCancel={chat.cancel} />;
}
```

```ts
import { initialVoice, voiceReducer } from './voiceMachine';

describe('voiceReducer', () => {
  it('idle → recording → transcribing → idle', () => {
    let s = voiceReducer(initialVoice, { type: 'start', at: 100 });
    expect(s).toEqual({ mode: 'recording', startedAt: 100 });
    s = voiceReducer(s, { type: 'stop' });
    expect(s).toEqual({ mode: 'transcribing' });
    s = voiceReducer(s, { type: 'transcribed' });
    expect(s).toEqual({ mode: 'idle', hint: null });
  });

  it('非對應狀態的事件不改變狀態；失敗回 idle 並帶提示', () => {
    expect(voiceReducer(initialVoice, { type: 'stop' })).toBe(initialVoice);

    expect(voiceReducer(rec, { type: 'start', at: 2 })).toBe(rec);
    expect(voiceReducer({ mode: 'transcribing' }, { type: 'failed', hint: '沒聽清楚' })).toEqual({ mode: 'idle', hint: '沒聽清楚' });
  });
});
```

`app/src/voice/useVoiceInput.test.ts`：

```ts
import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { ApiError } from '@/api/errors';
import type { Recorder } from './types';
import { MAX_RECORDING_MS, NOT_HEARD, PERMISSION_HINT } from './voiceMachine';
import { useVoiceInput } from './useVoiceInput';

function fakeRecorder(over: Partial<Recorder> = {}): Recorder {
  return {
    requestPermission: jest.fn(async () => true),
    start: jest.fn(async () => undefined),
    stop: jest.fn(async () => ({ uri: 'file:///rec.m4a' })),
    ...over,
  };
}

function setup(recorder: Recorder, transcribe: jest.Mock, clock: { t: number }) {
  const onText = jest.fn();
  const hook = renderHook(() => useVoiceInput({ recorder, transcribe, onText, now: () => clock.t }));
  return { ...hook, onText };
}

describe('useVoiceInput', () => {
  it('錄音 → 停止 → 轉文字 → onText', async () => {
    const clock = { t: 0 };
    const transcribe = jest.fn(async () => ({ text: ' 查庫存 ' }));
    const { result, onText } = setup(fakeRecorder(), transcribe, clock);
    await act(() => result.current.start());
    expect(result.current.state.mode).toBe('recording');
    clock.t = 2000;
    await act(() => result.current.stop());
    expect(transcribe).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ uri: 'file:///rec.m4a', name: 'speech.m4a', type: 'audio/m4a' });
    expect(onText).toHaveBeenCalledWith('查庫存');
    expect(result.current.state).toEqual({ mode: 'idle', hint: null });
  });

  it('不到 500 毫秒 → 不上傳、提示沒聽清楚', async () => {
    const clock = { t: 0 };
    const transcribe = jest.fn();
    const { result, onText } = setup(fakeRecorder(), transcribe, clock);
    await act(() => result.current.start());
    clock.t = 300;
    await act(() => result.current.stop());
    expect(transcribe).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(onText).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(result.current.state).toEqual({ mode: 'idle', hint: NOT_HEARD });
  });

  it('轉出空字串 → 不送出、提示沒聽清楚', async () => {
    const clock = { t: 0 };
    const { result, onText } = setup(fakeRecorder(), jest.fn(async () => ({ text: '   ' })), clock);
    await act(() => result.current.start());
    clock.t = 1500;
    await act(() => result.current.stop());
    expect(onText).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(result.current.state).toEqual({ mode: 'idle', hint: NOT_HEARD });
  });

  it('沒有麥克風權限 → 不錄音、提示權限', async () => {
    const recorder = fakeRecorder({ requestPermission: jest.fn(async () => false) });
    const { result } = setup(recorder, jest.fn(), { t: 0 });
    await act(() => result.current.start());
    expect(recorder.start).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(result.current.state).toEqual({ mode: 'idle', hint: PERMISSION_HINT });
  });

  it('語音未啟用（503 SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE）→ available=false，之後 start 不動作', async () => {
    const clock = { t: 0 };
    const recorder = fakeRecorder();
    const transcribe = jest.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError('未啟用', 503, 'SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE'); });
    const { result } = setup(recorder, transcribe, clock);
    await act(() => result.current.start());
    clock.t = 1000;
    await act(() => result.current.stop());
    expect(result.current.available).toBe(false);
    await act(() => result.current.start());
    expect(recorder.start).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('錄滿 60 秒自動停止並轉文字', async () => {
    jest.useFakeTimers();
    try {
      const clock = { t: 0 };
      const transcribe = jest.fn(async () => ({ text: '好' }));
      const { result, onText } = setup(fakeRecorder(), transcribe, clock);
      await act(() => result.current.start());
      clock.t = MAX_RECORDING_MS;
      await act(async () => { jest.advanceTimersByTime(MAX_RECORDING_MS); });
      await act(async () => { await Promise.resolve(); });
      expect(transcribe).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(onText).toHaveBeenCalledWith('好');
    } finally {
      jest.useRealTimers();
    }
  });

  it('上傳失敗 → 回 idle 並顯示錯誤訊息', async () => {
    const clock = { t: 0 };
    const transcribe = jest.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError('無法連線到伺服器，請檢查網路', 0, 'NETWORK'); });
    const { result } = setup(fakeRecorder(), transcribe, clock);
    await act(() => result.current.start());
    clock.t = 1000;
    await act(() => result.current.stop());
    expect(result.current.state).toEqual({ mode: 'idle', hint: '無法連線到伺服器，請檢查網路' });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { renderHook } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import type { ChatMessage } from '@/chat/types';
import { useSpeakReplies } from './useSpeakReplies';

const agent = (id: string, text: string): ChatMessage => ({ id, role: 'agent', text, at: 0 });
const user = (id: string, text: string): ChatMessage => ({ id, role: 'user', text, at: 0 });

describe('useSpeakReplies', () => {
  it('不唸既有的歷史；新的 AI 回覆才唸；使用者訊息不唸', () => {
    const speak = jest.fn();
    const stop = jest.fn();
    const { rerender } = renderHook(({ m, on }: { m: ChatMessage[]; on: boolean }) => useSpeakReplies(m, on, speak, stop), {
      initialProps: { m: [agent('a1', '舊的')], on: true },
    });
    expect(speak).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    rerender({ m: [agent('a1', '舊的'), user('u1', '問')], on: true });
    expect(speak).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    rerender({ m: [agent('a1', '舊的'), user('u1', '問'), agent('a2', '新的回覆')], on: true });
    expect(speak).toHaveBeenCalledWith('新的回覆');
  });

  it('關閉時不唸並停止朗讀', () => {
    const speak = jest.fn();
    const stop = jest.fn();
    const { rerender } = renderHook(({ m, on }: { m: ChatMessage[]; on: boolean }) => useSpeakReplies(m, on, speak, stop), {
      initialProps: { m: [] as ChatMessage[], on: true },
    });
    rerender({ m: [], on: false });
    expect(stop).toHaveBeenCalled();
    rerender({ m: [agent('a1', '回覆')], on: false });
    expect(speak).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```
