`app/src/voice/types.ts`：

```ts
/** 錄音介面：正式版用 expo-audio（expoRecorder.ts），測試用假的。 */
export interface Recorder {
  requestPermission(): Promise<boolean>;
  start(): Promise<void>;
  stop(): Promise<{ uri: string } | null>;
}
```

`app/src/voice/voiceMachine.ts`：

```ts
/** 語音輸入狀態（純函式）：待機 → 錄音 → 轉文字 → 待機；失敗回待機並帶提示。 */
export const MAX_RECORDING_MS = 60_000;
export const MIN_RECORDING_MS = 500;
export const NOT_HEARD = '沒聽清楚，再說一次好嗎？';
export const PERMISSION_HINT = '需要麥克風權限才能語音對話，請到系統設定開啟，或改用打字。';
export const SPEECH_OFF_HINT = '語音辨識目前未啟用，請改用打字。';

export type VoiceState = { mode: 'idle'; hint: string | null } | { mode: 'recording'; startedAt: number } | { mode: 'transcribing' };

export type VoiceEvent =
  | { type: 'start'; at: number }
  | { type: 'stop' }
  | { type: 'transcribed' }
  | { type: 'failed'; hint: string };

export const initialVoice: VoiceState = { mode: 'idle', hint: null };

export function voiceReducer(s: VoiceState, e: VoiceEvent): VoiceState {
  switch (e.type) {
    case 'start':
      return s.mode === 'idle' ? { mode: 'recording', startedAt: e.at } : s;
    case 'stop':
      return s.mode === 'recording' ? { mode: 'transcribing' } : s;
    case 'transcribed':
      return { mode: 'idle', hint: null };
    case 'failed':
      return { mode: 'idle', hint: e.hint };
    default:
      return s;
  }
}
```

`app/src/voice/markdown.ts`：

```ts
/** 朗讀前去掉 markdown 符號；表格列整行不唸（表格本來就不唸）。 */
export function stripMarkdown(text: string): string {
  return text
    .split('\n')
    .filter((l) => !/^\s*\|/.test(l))
    .map((l) =>
      l
        .replace(/^\s*#{1,6}\s+/, '')
        .replace(/^\s*(?:[-*•]|\d+\.)\s+/, '')
        .replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '$1')
        .replace(/__([^_]+)__/g, '$1')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '$1')
        .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]*\)/g, '$1')
        .trim(),
    )
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join('\n');
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3：實作 useVoiceInput、expoRecorder、speech、useSpeakReplies**

`app/src/voice/useVoiceInput.ts`：

```ts
/**
 * 語音輸入：權限 → 錄音（最長 60 秒）→ 上傳轉文字 → 交給 onText。
 * 狀態用 ref 同步保存，讓「剛開始就按停止」或計時器觸發時讀得到最新狀態。
 */
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ApiError, errorMessage } from '@/api/errors';
import type { UploadFile } from '@/api/types';
import type { Recorder } from './types';
import {
  MAX_RECORDING_MS, MIN_RECORDING_MS, NOT_HEARD, PERMISSION_HINT, SPEECH_OFF_HINT, initialVoice, voiceReducer,
  type VoiceEvent, type VoiceState,
} from './voiceMachine';

export function useVoiceInput({
  recorder,
  transcribe,
  onText,
  now = Date.now,
}: {
  recorder: Recorder;
  transcribe(file: UploadFile): Promise<{ text: string }>;
  onText(text: string): void;
  now?: () => number;
}) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<VoiceState>(initialVoice);
  const [available, setAvailable] = useState(true);
  const ref = useRef<VoiceState>(initialVoice);
  const availableRef = useRef(true);
  const timer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const deps = useRef({ recorder, transcribe, onText, now });
  deps.current = { recorder, transcribe, onText, now };

  const apply = useCallback((e: VoiceEvent) => {
    ref.current = voiceReducer(ref.current, e);
    setState(ref.current);
  }, []);

  const stop = useCallback(async () => {
    const cur = ref.current;
    if (cur.mode !== 'recording') return;
    if (timer.current) clearTimeout(timer.current);
    timer.current = null;
    apply({ type: 'stop' });
    const d = deps.current;
    let rec: { uri: string } | null;
    try {
      rec = await d.recorder.stop();
    } catch {
      apply({ type: 'failed', hint: '錄音失敗，請再試一次。' });
      return;
    }
    if (!rec || d.now() - cur.startedAt < MIN_RECORDING_MS) {
      apply({ type: 'failed', hint: NOT_HEARD });
      return;
    }
    try {
      const { text } = await d.transcribe({ uri: rec.uri, name: 'speech.m4a', type: 'audio/m4a' });
      const t = text.trim();
      if (!t) {
        apply({ type: 'failed', hint: NOT_HEARD });
        return;
      }
      apply({ type: 'transcribed' });
      d.onText(t);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof ApiError && e.code === 'SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE') {
        availableRef.current = false;
        setAvailable(false);
        apply({ type: 'failed', hint: SPEECH_OFF_HINT });
        return;
      }
      apply({ type: 'failed', hint: errorMessage(e) });
    }
  }, [apply]);

  const start = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!availableRef.current || ref.current.mode !== 'idle') return;
    const d = deps.current;
    if (!(await d.recorder.requestPermission())) {
      apply({ type: 'failed', hint: PERMISSION_HINT });
      return;
    }
    try {
      await d.recorder.start();
    } catch {
      apply({ type: 'failed', hint: '無法開始錄音，請再試一次。' });
      return;
    }
    apply({ type: 'start', at: d.now() });
    timer.current = setTimeout(() => void stop(), MAX_RECORDING_MS);
  }, [apply, stop]);

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      if (timer.current) clearTimeout(timer.current);
    },
    [],
  );

  return { state, available, start, stop };
}
```

`app/src/voice/expoRecorder.ts`：

```ts
/** expo-audio 版錄音器（高品質 m4a）。錄音時切換音訊模式，結束後恢復讓朗讀能播放。 */
import { AudioModule, RecordingPresets, setAudioModeAsync, useAudioRecorder } from 'expo-audio';
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import type { Recorder } from './types';

export function useExpoRecorder(): Recorder {
  const rec = useAudioRecorder(RecordingPresets.HIGH_QUALITY);
  return useMemo<Recorder>(
    () => ({
      async requestPermission() {
        const r = await AudioModule.requestRecordingPermissionsAsync();
        return r.granted;
      },
      async start() {
        await setAudioModeAsync({ allowsRecording: true, playsInSilentMode: true });
        await rec.prepareToRecordAsync();
        rec.record();
      },
      async stop() {
        await rec.stop();
        await setAudioModeAsync({ allowsRecording: false, playsInSilentMode: true });
        return rec.uri ? { uri: rec.uri } : null;
      },
    }),
    [rec],
  );
}
```

`app/src/voice/speech.ts`：

```ts
/** 朗讀（裝置內建 TTS，zh-TW）。 */
import * as Speech from 'expo-speech';
import { stripMarkdown } from './markdown';

export function speak(text: string): void {
  const t = stripMarkdown(text);
  if (!t) return;
  void Speech.stop();
  Speech.speak(t, { language: 'zh-TW' });
}

export function stopSpeaking(): void {
  void Speech.stop();
}
```

`app/src/voice/useSpeakReplies.ts`：

```ts
/** 朗讀開關為開時，唸出新出現的 AI 回覆（不唸進入畫面前就有的歷史）；關閉或離開時停止。 */
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { ChatMessage } from '@/chat/types';
import { speak, stopSpeaking } from './speech';

export function useSpeakReplies(
  messages: ChatMessage[],
  enabled: boolean,
  speakFn: (text: string) => void = speak,
  stopFn: () => void = stopSpeaking,
): void {
  const lastId = useRef<string | null | undefined>(undefined);

  useEffect(() => {
    const last = [...messages].reverse().find((m) => m.role === 'agent');
    if (lastId.current === undefined) {
      lastId.current = last?.id ?? null;
      return;
    }
    if (last && last.id !== lastId.current) {
      lastId.current = last.id;
      if (enabled) speakFn(last.text);
    }
  }, [messages, enabled, speakFn]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) stopFn();
  }, [enabled, stopFn]);

  useEffect(() => () => stopFn(), [stopFn]);
}
```

```ts
import type { StoreTodaySummary } from '@/api/types';
import { SCENARIOS } from './scenarios';
import { formatMonthDay, greeting, summaryCards } from './summaryCards';

const S: StoreTodaySummary = {
  lowStock: { count: 3, names: ['鮭魚御飯糰', '明太子御飯糰', '三明治－火腿蛋', '沙拉－凱薩'] },
  expiring: { count: 2, names: ['沙拉－和風雞肉', '便當－雞腿飯'] },
  equipmentAlerts: { count: 1, items: [{ name: '咖啡機', issue: '紅燈閃爍' }] },
  replenishmentsToday: { count: 4 },
};

describe('summaryCards', () => {
  it('四張卡片：數量、說明、點擊動作', () => {
    const c = summaryCards(S);
    expect(c.map((x) => [x.key, x.count])).toEqual([['lowStock', 3], ['expiring', 2], ['equipment', 1], ['replenishments', 4]]);
    expect(c[0]).toMatchObject({ title: '商品庫存偏低', detail: '鮭魚御飯糰・明太子御飯糰・三明治－火腿蛋', action: { kind: 'ask', q: '哪些商品庫存偏低？' } });
    expect(c[1]).toMatchObject({ title: '即期品需要處理', detail: '有效期限 1 天內', action: { kind: 'ask', q: '今天有哪些即期品？' } });
    expect(c[2]).toMatchObject({ title: '設備異常', detail: '咖啡機紅燈閃爍', action: { kind: 'ask', q: '咖啡機紅燈閃爍怎麼處理？' } });
    expect(c[3]).toMatchObject({ title: '今日補貨單', action: { kind: 'tasks' } });
  });

  it('都沒有狀況時的說明文字', () => {
    const c = summaryCards({
      lowStock: { count: 0, names: [] },
      expiring: { count: 0, names: [] },
      equipmentAlerts: { count: 0, items: [] },
      replenishmentsToday: { count: 0 },
    });
    expect(c[0].detail).toBe('庫存都充足');
    expect(c[1].detail).toBe('目前沒有即期品');
    expect(c[2]).toMatchObject({ detail: '設備運作正常', action: { kind: 'ask', q: '設備故障怎麼排除？' } });
  });
});

describe('greeting / formatMonthDay / SCENARIOS', () => {
  it('依時段問候', () => {
    expect(greeting(6)).toBe('早安');
    expect(greeting(10)).toBe('早安');
    expect(greeting(11)).toBe('午安');
    expect(greeting(17)).toBe('午安');
    expect(greeting(18)).toBe('晚安');
  });

  it('月/日（星期）', () => {
    expect(formatMonthDay(new Date(2026, 9, 4))).toBe('10/4（日）');
  });

      ['查詢庫存', '幫我查庫存'],
      ['建立補貨單', '哪些商品需要補貨？'],

      ['促銷活動查詢', '現在有什麼活動？'],

});
```

- [ ] **Step 2：實作純函式與資料**

`app/src/navigation/chatRoute.ts`：

```ts
/** 導到 AI 對話：nonce 每次唯一，讓同一個問題也能再問一次（畫面用 nonce 判斷是否已處理）。 */
let seq = 0;

export function chatRoute(params: { q?: string; voice?: '1'; focus?: '1' } = {}) {
  seq += 1;
  return { pathname: '/chat' as const, params: { ...params, nonce: `${Date.now()}-${seq}` } };
}
```

`app/src/home/summaryCards.ts`：

```ts
/** 今日重點卡片：由門市 store_today_summary 結果組成；點擊帶問題進 AI 對話或到任務分頁。 */
import type { StoreTodaySummary } from '@/api/types';

export type CardAction = { kind: 'ask'; q: string } | { kind: 'tasks' };

export interface SummaryCard {
  key: 'lowStock' | 'expiring' | 'equipment' | 'replenishments';
  count: number;
  title: string;
  detail: string;
  tone: 'danger' | 'warning' | 'equipment' | 'info';
  icon: 'trending-down' | 'alarm-outline' | 'construct-outline' | 'clipboard-outline';
  action: CardAction;
}

export function summaryCards(s: StoreTodaySummary): SummaryCard[] {
  const eq = s.equipmentAlerts.items[0];
  return [
    {
      key: 'lowStock', count: s.lowStock.count, title: '商品庫存偏低', tone: 'danger', icon: 'trending-down',
      detail: s.lowStock.count > 0 ? s.lowStock.names.slice(0, 3).join('・') : '庫存都充足',
      action: { kind: 'ask', q: '哪些商品庫存偏低？' },
    },
    {
      key: 'expiring', count: s.expiring.count, title: '即期品需要處理', tone: 'warning', icon: 'alarm-outline',
      detail: s.expiring.count > 0 ? '有效期限 1 天內' : '目前沒有即期品',
      action: { kind: 'ask', q: '今天有哪些即期品？' },
    },
    {
      key: 'equipment', count: s.equipmentAlerts.count, title: '設備異常', tone: 'equipment', icon: 'construct-outline',
      detail: eq ? `${eq.name}${eq.issue}` : '設備運作正常',
      action: { kind: 'ask', q: eq ? `${eq.name}${eq.issue}怎麼處理？` : '設備故障怎麼排除？' },
    },
    {
      key: 'replenishments', count: s.replenishmentsToday.count, title: '今日補貨單', tone: 'info', icon: 'clipboard-outline',
      detail: '點此查看補貨單',
      action: { kind: 'tasks' },
    },
  ];
}

export function greeting(hour: number): string {
  if (hour < 11) return '早安';
  if (hour < 18) return '午安';
  return '晚安';
}

const WEEKDAY = ['日', '一', '二', '三', '四', '五', '六'];

export function formatMonthDay(d: Date): string {
  return `${d.getMonth() + 1}/${d.getDate()}（${WEEKDAY[d.getDay()]}）`;
}
```

```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { storeTool } from '@/api/storeTools';
import type { StoreTodaySummary } from '@/api/types';
import { useApi } from '@/auth/AuthProvider';

export function useTodaySummary() {
  const api = useApi();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['todaySummary'],
    queryFn: async () => (await api.invokeTool<StoreTodaySummary>(storeTool('store_today_summary'))).result,
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
  });
}
```

`app/src/home/TodayPanel.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 今日重點（稿 4 右欄）。 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { ActivityIndicator, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { colors, font, radius, shadow, space } from '@/theme/tokens';
import type { SummaryCard } from './summaryCards';

const TONE: Record<SummaryCard['tone'], { fg: string; bg: string; icon: string }> = {
  danger: { fg: colors.danger, bg: colors.dangerSoft, icon: colors.danger },
  warning: { fg: colors.warning, bg: colors.warningSoft, icon: colors.warning },
  equipment: { fg: colors.primaryDark, bg: colors.primarySoft, icon: colors.primaryDark },
  info: { fg: colors.info, bg: colors.infoSoft, icon: colors.info },
};

export function TodayPanel({
  cards, loading, error, onRetry, onPress, dateLabel,
}: {
  cards?: SummaryCard[];
  loading: boolean;
  error: boolean;
  onRetry(): void;
  onPress(card: SummaryCard): void;
  dateLabel: string;
}) {
  return (
    <View style={styles.panel}>
      <View style={styles.head}>
        <Text style={styles.title}>今日重點</Text>
        <Text style={styles.date}>{dateLabel}</Text>
      </View>
      {loading && !cards ? <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primary} style={{ marginVertical: space.xl }} /> : null}
      {error && !cards ? (
        <View style={styles.error}>
          <Text style={styles.errorText}>暫時無法取得</Text>
          <Pressable onPress={onRetry} accessibilityLabel="重試" accessibilityRole="button" style={styles.retry}>
            <Text style={{ color: colors.primaryDark, fontWeight: '600' }}>重試</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      ) : null}
      {cards?.map((c) => {
        const t = TONE[c.tone];
        return (
          <Pressable
            key={c.key}
            style={[styles.card, { backgroundColor: t.bg }]}
            onPress={() => onPress(c)}
            accessibilityRole="button"
            accessibilityLabel={`${c.title} ${c.count} 項`}
          >
            <View style={[styles.icon, { backgroundColor: t.icon }]}>
              <Ionicons name={c.icon} size={24} color="#fff" />
            </View>
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[styles.count, { color: t.fg }]}>{`${c.count} ${c.key === 'replenishments' ? '張' : '項'}`}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>{c.title}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.detail} numberOfLines={1}>{c.detail}</Text>
            </View>
            <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color={colors.textMuted} />
          </Pressable>
        );
      })}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  panel: { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.xl, padding: space.lg, gap: space.md, ...shadow.card },
  head: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'baseline', gap: space.sm },
  title: { fontSize: font.lg, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },
  date: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.textMuted },
  card: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.md, borderRadius: radius.lg, padding: space.md },
  icon: { width: 48, height: 48, borderRadius: radius.md, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  count: { fontSize: font.xl, fontWeight: '800' },
  cardTitle: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text, fontWeight: '600' },
  detail: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.textMuted },
  error: { alignItems: 'center', gap: space.sm, paddingVertical: space.lg },
  errorText: { color: colors.textMuted },
  retry: { paddingHorizontal: space.lg, paddingVertical: space.sm, borderRadius: radius.md, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border },
});
```

```tsx
/** 首頁（稿 4）：問候、三個入口、常用情境；寬版右欄今日重點。 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Mascot } from '@/brand/Mascot';
import { SCENARIOS } from '@/home/scenarios';
import { formatMonthDay, greeting, summaryCards, type SummaryCard } from '@/home/summaryCards';
import { TodayPanel } from '@/home/TodayPanel';
import { useTodaySummary } from '@/home/useTodaySummary';
import { useIsWide } from '@/layout/useIsWide';
import { chatRoute } from '@/navigation/chatRoute';
import { colors, font, radius, shadow, space } from '@/theme/tokens';

export default function Home() {
  const wide = useIsWide();
  const summary = useTodaySummary();
  const now = new Date();

  const onCard = (c: SummaryCard) => {
    if (c.action.kind === 'tasks') router.navigate('/tasks');
    else router.navigate(chatRoute({ q: c.action.q }));
  };

  const today = (
    <TodayPanel
      cards={summary.data ? summaryCards(summary.data) : undefined}
      loading={summary.isLoading}
      error={summary.isError}
      onRetry={() => void summary.refetch()}
      onPress={onCard}
      dateLabel={formatMonthDay(now)}
    />
  );

  const main = (
    <View style={{ gap: space.lg }}>
      <View style={styles.hero}>
        <Mascot pose="wave" size={wide ? 200 : 120} />
        <View style={styles.bubble}>
          <Text style={styles.hello}>{`${greeting(now.getHours())}！\n今天需要我幫你做什麼？`}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.helloSub}>可以用語音、拍照或文字告訴我，我會幫你查詢、分析並執行任務！</Text>
        </View>
      </View>
      <View style={[styles.entries, !wide && { flexWrap: 'wrap' }]}>
        <Entry primary icon="mic" title="語音對話" sub="直接說出你的需求" onPress={() => router.navigate(chatRoute({ voice: '1' }))} />
        <Entry icon="camera" title="拍照問 AI" sub="拍貨架、優惠券" onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/camera', params: { mode: 'shelf' } })} />
        <Entry icon="keypad" title="輸入文字" sub="用打字詢問" onPress={() => router.navigate(chatRoute({ focus: '1' }))} />
      </View>
      {!wide ? today : null}
      <View style={{ gap: space.sm }}>
        <Text style={styles.section}>常用情境</Text>
        <View style={styles.scenarios}>
          {SCENARIOS.map((s) => (
            <Pressable key={s.label} style={styles.scenario} onPress={() => router.navigate(chatRoute({ q: s.q }))} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={s.label}>
              <Ionicons name={s.icon} size={28} color={s.color} />
              <Text style={styles.scenarioText}>{s.label}</Text>
            </Pressable>
          ))}
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );

  return (
    <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }} contentContainerStyle={styles.page}>
      {wide ? (
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: space.lg }}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>{main}</View>
          <View style={{ width: 340 }}>{today}</View>
        </View>
      ) : (
        main
      )}
    </ScrollView>
  );
}

function Entry({ icon, title, sub, onPress, primary }: { icon: 'mic' | 'camera' | 'keypad'; title: string; sub: string; onPress(): void; primary?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <Pressable style={[styles.entry, primary && { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]} onPress={onPress} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={title}>
      <View style={[styles.entryIcon, primary && { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)' }]}>
        <Ionicons name={icon} size={28} color={primary ? '#fff' : colors.info} />
      </View>
      <Text style={[styles.entryTitle, primary && { color: '#fff' }]}>{title}</Text>
      <Text style={[styles.entrySub, primary && { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)' }]}>{sub}</Text>
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  page: { padding: space.lg },
  hero: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.lg },
  bubble: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.xl, padding: space.lg, ...shadow.card },
  hello: { fontSize: font.xxl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },
  helloSub: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.textMuted, marginTop: space.sm },
  entries: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: space.md },
  entry: { flex: 1, minWidth: 150, alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.xl, paddingVertical: space.xl, ...shadow.card },
  entryIcon: { width: 56, height: 56, borderRadius: 28, backgroundColor: colors.infoSoft, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  entryTitle: { fontSize: font.xl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },
  entrySub: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.textMuted },
  section: { fontSize: font.lg, fontWeight: '700', color: colors.text },
  scenarios: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: space.md },
  scenario: { width: 120, alignItems: 'center', gap: space.sm, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.lg, paddingVertical: space.lg, ...shadow.card },
  scenarioText: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.text },
});
```

```ts
import type { ShelfFinding, ShelfResult } from '@/api/types';
import { boxRect, daysLeft, findingDetail, fitContain, groupFindings, issueCount, replenishmentCount } from './shelf';

const f = (status: ShelfFinding['status'], extra: Partial<ShelfFinding> = {}): ShelfFinding => ({ name: 'x', status, box: { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0.1, h: 0.1 }, ...extra });

describe('shelf', () => {
  it('依狀態分組（不含 ok），計算需處理數', () => {
    const list = [f('out_of_stock'), f('expiring'), f('ok'), f('misplaced'), f('out_of_stock')];
    const g = groupFindings(list);
    expect([g.out_of_stock.length, g.expiring.length, g.misplaced.length]).toEqual([2, 1, 1]);
    expect(issueCount(list)).toBe(4);
  });

  it('剩幾天', () => {
    expect(daysLeft('2026-10-05', '2026-10-04')).toBe(1);
    expect(daysLeft('2026-10-04', '2026-10-04')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('各狀態的說明文字', () => {
    expect(findingDetail(f('out_of_stock', { suggestedQty: 24 }), '2026-10-04')).toBe('建議補貨 24 個');
    expect(findingDetail(f('out_of_stock'), '2026-10-04')).toBe('建議補貨');
    expect(findingDetail(f('expiring', { expiryDate: '2026-10-05' }), '2026-10-04')).toBe('有效期限：2026/10/05（剩 1 天）');
    expect(findingDetail(f('misplaced'), '2026-10-04')).toBe('擺放位置不正確');
    expect(findingDetail(f('misplaced', { note: '放到飲料區' }), '2026-10-04')).toBe('放到飲料區');
  });

  it('圖片等比縮放置中，框選換算成畫面座標', () => {
    const frame = fitContain(4000, 3000, 800, 800);
    expect(frame).toEqual({ left: 0, top: 100, width: 800, height: 600 });
    expect(boxRect({ x: 0.5, y: 0.5, w: 0.25, h: 0.5 }, frame)).toEqual({ left: 400, top: 400, width: 200, height: 300 });
  });

  it('補貨提議的品項數', () => {
    const r: ShelfResult = { mode: 'shelf', findings: [], counts: { outOfStock: 0, expiring: 0, misplaced: 0 } };
    expect(replenishmentCount(r)).toBe(0);
    expect(replenishmentCount({ ...r, replenishment: { toolId: 't', input: { items: [{}, {}, {}] } } })).toBe(3);
  });
});
```

```tsx
    { name: '沙拉－和風雞肉', status: 'expiring', box: { x: 0.4, y: 0.4, w: 0.2, h: 0.2 }, expiryDate: '2026-10-05' },
    { name: '關東煮－蘿蔔', status: 'misplaced', box: { x: 0.6, y: 0.6, w: 0.2, h: 0.2 } },
    { name: '鮪魚御飯糰', status: 'ok', box: { x: 0.7, y: 0.1, w: 0.2, h: 0.2 } },

};

describe('ShelfResultView', () => {
  it('顯示需處理數與分組，按建立補貨單呼叫 onCreate', async () => {

    fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText('建立補貨單（0 項）'));
    expect(onCreate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('全部正常 → 顯示沒有需要處理的項目', () => {
    render(<ShelfResultView photo={photo} result={{ ...result, findings: [result.findings[3]], replenishment: undefined }} today="2026-10-04" wide creating={false} error={null} onRetake={jest.fn()} onCreate={jest.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('這排貨架看起來都沒問題！')).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`app/src/vision/VisionResultStore.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 相機與結果頁之間傳遞辨識結果（照片與結果物件太大，不放路由參數）。 */
import { createContext, useContext, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { ShelfResult } from '@/api/types';
import type { Photo } from './types';

export interface ShelfEntry {
  photo: Photo;
  result: ShelfResult;
}

interface VisionResults {
  shelf: ShelfEntry | null;
  setShelf(v: ShelfEntry | null): void;
}

const Ctx = createContext<VisionResults | null>(null);

export function VisionResultProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [shelf, setShelf] = useState<ShelfEntry | null>(null);
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ shelf, setShelf }), [shelf]);
  return <Ctx.Provider value={value}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

export function useVisionResults(): VisionResults {
  const v = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!v) throw new Error('useVisionResults 必須在 VisionResultProvider 內使用');
  return v;
}
```

`app/src/vision/shelf.ts`：

```ts
/** 貨架辨識結果的顯示邏輯（純函式）。 */
import type { ShelfFinding, ShelfResult, ShelfStatus } from '@/api/types';
import { colors } from '@/theme/tokens';

export const STATUS_META: Record<ShelfStatus, { label: string; color: string; soft: string }> = {
  out_of_stock: { label: '缺貨', color: colors.danger, soft: colors.dangerSoft },
  expiring: { label: '即期品', color: colors.warning, soft: colors.warningSoft },
  misplaced: { label: '擺放錯誤', color: colors.misplaced, soft: colors.misplacedSoft },
  ok: { label: '擺放正確', color: colors.primary, soft: colors.primarySoft },
};

export const ISSUE_ORDER = ['out_of_stock', 'expiring', 'misplaced'] as const;

export function groupFindings(findings: ShelfFinding[]): Record<(typeof ISSUE_ORDER)[number], ShelfFinding[]> {
  return {
    out_of_stock: findings.filter((f) => f.status === 'out_of_stock'),
    expiring: findings.filter((f) => f.status === 'expiring'),
    misplaced: findings.filter((f) => f.status === 'misplaced'),
  };
}

export function issueCount(findings: ShelfFinding[]): number {
  return findings.filter((f) => f.status !== 'ok').length;
}

export function daysLeft(expiry: string, today: string): number {
  return Math.round((Date.parse(`${expiry}T00:00:00Z`) - Date.parse(`${today}T00:00:00Z`)) / 86_400_000);
}

export function findingDetail(f: ShelfFinding, today: string): string {
  switch (f.status) {
    case 'out_of_stock':
      return f.suggestedQty ? `建議補貨 ${f.suggestedQty} 個` : '建議補貨';
    case 'expiring':
      return f.expiryDate ? `有效期限：${f.expiryDate.replace(/-/g, '/')}（剩 ${daysLeft(f.expiryDate, today)} 天）` : '即將到期';
    case 'misplaced':
      return f.note ?? '擺放位置不正確';
    default:
      return '';
  }
}

export interface Frame {
  left: number;
  top: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/** 圖片以 contain 方式放進容器時實際占的區域 */
export function fitContain(imgW: number, imgH: number, boxW: number, boxH: number): Frame {
  const scale = Math.min(boxW / imgW, boxH / imgH);
  const width = imgW * scale;
  const height = imgH * scale;
  return { left: (boxW - width) / 2, top: (boxH - height) / 2, width, height };
}

export function boxRect(box: ShelfFinding['box'], frame: Frame): Frame {
  return {
    left: frame.left + box.x * frame.width,
    top: frame.top + box.y * frame.height,
    width: box.w * frame.width,
    height: box.h * frame.height,
  };
}

export function replenishmentCount(result: ShelfResult): number {
  const items = result.replenishment?.input.items;
  return Array.isArray(items) ? items.length : 0;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3：實作 ShelfResultView 與路由**

`app/src/vision/ShelfResultView.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 貨架辨識結果（稿 2）：左照片＋框選，右分組清單；寬版左右、窄版上下。 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Image, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import type { ShelfResult } from '@/api/types';
import { Mascot } from '@/brand/Mascot';
import { colors, font, radius, space } from '@/theme/tokens';
import { ISSUE_ORDER, STATUS_META, boxRect, findingDetail, fitContain, groupFindings, issueCount, replenishmentCount, type Frame } from './shelf';
import type { Photo } from './types';

export function ShelfResultView({
  photo, result, today, wide, creating, error, onRetake, onCreate,
}: {
  photo: Photo;
  result: ShelfResult;
  today: string;
  wide: boolean;
  creating: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  onRetake(): void;
  onCreate(): Promise<void>;
}) {
  const [size, setSize] = useState<{ w: number; h: number } | null>(null);
  const frame: Frame | null = size ? fitContain(photo.width, photo.height, size.w, size.h) : null;
  const groups = groupFindings(result.findings);
  const issues = issueCount(result.findings);
  const n = replenishmentCount(result);
  const canCreate = n > 0 && !creating;

  const photoPane = (
    <View style={[styles.photo, wide ? { flex: 1.4 } : { height: 320 }]} onLayout={(e) => setSize({ w: e.nativeEvent.layout.width, h: e.nativeEvent.layout.height })}>
      <Image source={{ uri: photo.uri }} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} resizeMode="contain" />
      {frame
        ? result.findings.map((f, i) => {
            const r = boxRect(f.box, frame);
            const meta = STATUS_META[f.status];
            return (
              <View key={i} pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.box, { left: r.left, top: r.top, width: r.width, height: r.height, borderColor: meta.color }]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tag, { backgroundColor: meta.color }]}>{meta.label}</Text>
              </View>
            );
          })
        : null}
      <Pressable style={styles.retake} onPress={onRetake} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="再拍一張">
        <Ionicons name="refresh" size={20} color={colors.primaryDark} />
        <Text style={styles.retakeText}>再拍一張</Text>
      </Pressable>
    </View>
  );

  const listPane = (
    <View style={[styles.list, wide ? { width: '40%' } : { flex: 1 }]}>
      <View style={styles.listHead}>
        <Mascot pose="wave" size={48} />
        <Text style={styles.listTitle}>{issues > 0 ? `我看到 ${issues} 個需要處理的項目：` : '這排貨架看起來都沒問題！'}</Text>
      </View>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ gap: space.md, paddingBottom: space.md }}>
        {ISSUE_ORDER.map((status) =>
          groups[status].length > 0 ? (
            <View key={status} style={{ gap: space.sm }}>
              <Text style={[styles.group, { color: STATUS_META[status].color, backgroundColor: STATUS_META[status].soft }]}>
                {`${STATUS_META[status].label}（${groups[status].length}）`}
              </Text>
              {groups[status].map((f, i) => (
                <View key={i} style={styles.item}>
                  {f.image ? <Image source={{ uri: f.image }} style={styles.thumb} /> : <View style={styles.thumb} />}
                  <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                    <Text style={styles.itemName}>{f.name}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.itemDetail}>{findingDetail(f, today)}</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              ))}
            </View>
          ) : null,
        )}
        {result.warnings?.map((w) => <Text key={w} style={styles.warning}>{w}</Text>)}
      </ScrollView>
      {error ? <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text> : null}
      <Pressable
        style={[styles.create, !canCreate && { opacity: 0.5 }]}
        onPress={() => { if (canCreate) void onCreate(); }}
        accessibilityRole="button"
        accessibilityLabel={`建立補貨單（${n} 項）`}
        accessibilityState={{ disabled: !canCreate }}
      >
        {creating ? <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" /> : <Ionicons name="cart-outline" size={22} color="#fff" />}
        <Text style={styles.createText}>{`建立補貨單（${n} 項）`}</Text>
      </Pressable>
    </View>
  );

  return <View style={[styles.page, { flexDirection: wide ? 'row' : 'column' }]}>{photoPane}{listPane}</View>;
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  page: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg },
  photo: { backgroundColor: '#1d2420', overflow: 'hidden' },
  box: { position: 'absolute', borderWidth: 3, borderRadius: 6 },
  tag: { position: 'absolute', top: -26, left: -3, color: '#fff', fontWeight: '800', fontSize: font.sm, paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 3, borderRadius: 6, overflow: 'hidden' },
```

`app/app/(tabs)/chat/shelf-result.tsx`：

```tsx
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { errorMessage } from '@/api/errors';
import { useChat } from '@/chat/ChatProvider';
import { localToday } from '@/confirm/confirmForm';
import { useIsWide } from '@/layout/useIsWide';
import { colors, font, space } from '@/theme/tokens';
import { ShelfResultView } from '@/vision/ShelfResultView';
import { useVisionResults } from '@/vision/VisionResultStore';

export default function ShelfResultScreen() {
  const { shelf } = useVisionResults();
  const chat = useChat();
  const wide = useIsWide();
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const retake = () => router.push({ pathname: '/camera', params: { mode: 'shelf' } });

  if (!shelf) {
    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: space.md }}>
        <Text style={{ fontSize: font.lg, color: colors.text }}>還沒有辨識結果</Text>
        <Pressable onPress={retake} accessibilityRole="button">
          <Text style={{ color: colors.primaryDark, fontSize: font.md }}>拍一張貨架照片</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
    );
  }

  async function create() {
    const rep = shelf?.result.replenishment;
    if (!rep) return;
    setCreating(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await chat.propose(rep.toolId, rep.input);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <ShelfResultView photo={shelf.photo} result={shelf.result} today={localToday()} wide={wide} creating={creating} error={error} onRetake={retake} onCreate={create} />
  );
}
```

```ts
import { askAiText, fromCouponCheck, fromVision, headline } from './coupon';

const valid = { code: 'COFFEE50', title: '咖啡折 5 元', valid: true, reason: '可使用', applicableTo: '咖啡', validPeriod: '2026-09-29 ~ 2026-10-14', conditions: '限中杯以上' };

describe('coupon', () => {
  it('fromCouponCheck', () => {
    expect(fromCouponCheck(valid)).toEqual({ code: 'COFFEE50', title: '咖啡折 5 元', status: 'valid', reason: '可使用', applicableTo: '咖啡', validPeriod: '2026-09-29 ~ 2026-10-14', conditions: '限中杯以上' });
    expect(fromCouponCheck({ code: 'NOPE', valid: false, reason: '查無此券' })).toMatchObject({ status: 'invalid', reason: '查無此券' });
  });

  it('fromVision：有查詢結果用結果；讀不到券號為 unknown', () => {
    expect(fromVision({ mode: 'coupon', extracted: { code: 'COFFEE50' }, result: valid })).toMatchObject({ status: 'valid', code: 'COFFEE50' });
    expect(fromVision({ mode: 'coupon', extracted: { title: '某券' }, message: '讀不到券號，請掃條碼或手動輸入' })).toEqual({
      code: undefined, title: '某券', status: 'unknown', reason: '讀不到券號，請掃條碼或手動輸入',
    });
  });

  it('headline / askAiText', () => {
    expect(headline(fromCouponCheck(valid))).toBe('這張優惠券可以使用！');
    expect(headline(fromCouponCheck({ code: 'SALAD20', valid: false, reason: '已過期' }))).toBe('這張優惠券不能使用（已過期）');
    expect(headline({ status: 'unknown', reason: 'x' })).toBe('看不清楚這張券的券號');
    expect(askAiText(fromCouponCheck(valid))).toBe('客人的優惠券 COFFEE50 可以用嗎？還有什麼要注意的？');
  });
});
```

`app/src/vision/coupon.ts`：

```ts
/** 優惠券結果統一格式：拍照辨識（vision）與掃條碼（coupon_check）共用同一個畫面。 */
import type { CouponResult } from '@/api/types';

export interface CouponCheck {
  code?: string;
  title?: string;
  valid?: boolean;
  reason?: string;
  applicableTo?: string;
  validPeriod?: string;
  conditions?: string;
}

export interface CouponView {
  code?: string;
  title?: string;
  status: 'valid' | 'invalid' | 'unknown';
  reason: string;
  applicableTo?: string;
  validPeriod?: string;
  conditions?: string;
}

export function fromCouponCheck(r: CouponCheck): CouponView {
  return {
    code: r.code,
    title: r.title,
    status: r.valid === true ? 'valid' : 'invalid',
    reason: r.reason ?? (r.valid ? '可使用' : '無法使用'),
    applicableTo: r.applicableTo,
    validPeriod: r.validPeriod,
    conditions: r.conditions,
  };
}

export function fromVision(r: CouponResult): CouponView {
  if (r.result) {
    const c = r.result as CouponCheck;
    return fromCouponCheck({ ...c, code: c.code ?? r.extracted.code, title: c.title ?? r.extracted.title });
  }
  return { code: r.extracted.code, title: r.extracted.title, status: 'unknown', reason: r.message ?? '看不清楚券號' };
}

export function headline(v: CouponView): string {
  if (v.status === 'valid') return '這張優惠券可以使用！';
  if (v.status === 'invalid') return `這張優惠券不能使用（${v.reason}）`;
  return '看不清楚這張券的券號';
}

export function askAiText(v: CouponView): string {
  return `客人的優惠券 ${v.code ?? ''} 可以用嗎？還有什麼要注意的？`;
}
```

修改 `app/src/vision/VisionResultStore.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 相機與結果頁之間傳遞辨識結果（照片與結果物件太大，不放路由參數）。 */
import { createContext, useContext, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { ShelfResult } from '@/api/types';
import type { CouponView } from './coupon';
import type { Photo } from './types';

export interface ShelfEntry {
  photo: Photo;
  result: ShelfResult;
}

export interface CouponEntry {
  /** 掃條碼時沒有照片 */
  photo?: Photo;
  view: CouponView;
}

interface VisionResults {
  shelf: ShelfEntry | null;
  setShelf(v: ShelfEntry | null): void;
  coupon: CouponEntry | null;
  setCoupon(v: CouponEntry | null): void;
}

const Ctx = createContext<VisionResults | null>(null);

export function VisionResultProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [shelf, setShelf] = useState<ShelfEntry | null>(null);
  const [coupon, setCoupon] = useState<CouponEntry | null>(null);
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ shelf, setShelf, coupon, setCoupon }), [shelf, coupon]);
  return <Ctx.Provider value={value}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

export function useVisionResults(): VisionResults {
  const v = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!v) throw new Error('useVisionResults 必須在 VisionResultProvider 內使用');
  return v;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3：實作畫面與路由**

`app/src/vision/CouponResultView.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 優惠券結果（版型同稿 2）：左券照片（掃條碼時顯示條碼圖示），右狀態與細節。 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Mascot } from '@/brand/Mascot';
import { colors, font, radius, space } from '@/theme/tokens';
import { headline, type CouponView } from './coupon';
import type { Photo } from './types';

const STATUS = {
  valid: { label: '可使用', fg: '#fff', bg: colors.primary, soft: colors.primarySoft },
  invalid: { label: '', fg: '#fff', bg: colors.danger, soft: colors.dangerSoft },
  unknown: { label: '無法辨識', fg: colors.text, bg: '#E3E6E4', soft: colors.bg },
} as const;

export function CouponResultView({
  photo, view, wide, onRetake, onBarcode, onAskAi,
}: {
  photo?: Photo;
  view: CouponView;
  wide: boolean;
  onRetake(): void;
  onBarcode(): void;
  onAskAi(): void;
}) {
  const s = STATUS[view.status];
  const pill = view.status === 'invalid' ? view.reason : s.label;
  const canAsk = Boolean(view.code);
  const rows: [string, string | undefined][] = [
    ['券號', view.code],
    ['適用', view.applicableTo],
    ['條件', view.conditions],
    ['期限', view.validPeriod],
  ];

  return (
    <View style={[styles.page, { flexDirection: wide ? 'row' : 'column' }]}>
      <View style={[styles.left, wide ? { flex: 1.4 } : { height: 260 }]}>
        {photo ? (
          <Image source={{ uri: photo.uri }} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} resizeMode="contain" />
        ) : (
          <Ionicons name="barcode-outline" size={160} color="#fff" />
        )}
        <Pressable style={styles.retake} onPress={onRetake} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="再拍一張">
          <Ionicons name="refresh" size={20} color={colors.primaryDark} />
          <Text style={styles.retakeText}>再拍一張</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
      <ScrollView style={[styles.right, wide ? { width: '42%', flexGrow: 0 } : { flex: 1 }]} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: space.md, padding: space.lg }}>
        <View style={styles.head}>
          <Mascot pose="wave" size={48} />
          <Text style={styles.headline}>{headline(view)}</Text>
        </View>
        <View style={[styles.info, { backgroundColor: s.soft }]}>
          <View style={styles.titleRow}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>{view.title ?? '優惠券'}</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.pill, { color: s.fg, backgroundColor: s.bg }]}>{pill}</Text>
          </View>
          {rows.map(([k, v]) =>
            v ? (
              <View key={k} style={styles.row}>
                <Text style={styles.key}>{k}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.value}>{v}</Text>
              </View>
            ) : null,
          )}
          {view.status === 'unknown' ? <Text style={styles.value}>{view.reason}</Text> : null}
        </View>
        {view.status === 'unknown' || !view.code ? (
          <Pressable style={styles.secondary} onPress={onBarcode} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="改用掃條碼">
            <Ionicons name="barcode-outline" size={20} color={colors.primaryDark} />
            <Text style={styles.secondaryText}>改用掃條碼</Text>
          </Pressable>
        ) : null}
        <Pressable
          style={[styles.primary, !canAsk && { opacity: 0.5 }]}
          onPress={() => { if (canAsk) onAskAi(); }}
          accessibilityRole="button"
          accessibilityLabel="問 AI 這張券的細節"
          accessibilityState={{ disabled: !canAsk }}
        >
          <Ionicons name="chatbubble-ellipses-outline" size={20} color="#fff" />
          <Text style={styles.primaryText}>問 AI 這張券的細節</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  page: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg },
  left: { backgroundColor: '#3a3430', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', overflow: 'hidden' },
  retake: { position: 'absolute', right: space.lg, bottom: space.lg, flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6, alignItems: 'center', backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.lg, paddingHorizontal: space.lg, paddingVertical: space.md },
  retakeText: { fontSize: font.md, fontWeight: '700', color: colors.primaryDark },
  right: { backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  head: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.md },
  headline: { flex: 1, fontSize: font.lg, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },
  info: { borderRadius: radius.lg, padding: space.lg, gap: space.sm },
  titleRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.sm, flexWrap: 'wrap' },
  title: { fontSize: font.xl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },
  pill: { fontSize: font.sm, fontWeight: '800', paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: radius.pill, overflow: 'hidden' },
  row: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: space.md },
  key: { width: 40, fontSize: font.md, color: colors.textMuted },
  value: { flex: 1, fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  secondary: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: space.sm, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.primary, borderRadius: radius.lg, paddingVertical: 14 },
  secondaryText: { color: colors.primaryDark, fontSize: font.md, fontWeight: '700' },
  primary: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: space.sm, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', backgroundColor: colors.primary, borderRadius: radius.lg, paddingVertical: 16 },
  primaryText: { color: '#fff', fontSize: font.lg, fontWeight: '800' },
});
```

`app/app/(tabs)/chat/coupon-result.tsx`：

```tsx
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { Pressable, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useChat } from '@/chat/ChatProvider';
import { useIsWide } from '@/layout/useIsWide';
import { colors, font, space } from '@/theme/tokens';
import { askAiText } from '@/vision/coupon';
import { CouponResultView } from '@/vision/CouponResultView';
import { useVisionResults } from '@/vision/VisionResultStore';

export default function CouponResultScreen() {
  const { coupon } = useVisionResults();
  const chat = useChat();
  const wide = useIsWide();
  const camera = (mode: 'coupon' | 'barcode') => router.push({ pathname: '/camera', params: { mode } });

  if (!coupon) {
    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: space.md }}>
        <Text style={{ fontSize: font.lg, color: colors.text }}>還沒有優惠券結果</Text>
        <Pressable onPress={() => camera('coupon')} accessibilityRole="button">
          <Text style={{ color: colors.primaryDark, fontSize: font.md }}>拍一張優惠券</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <CouponResultView
      photo={coupon.photo}
      view={coupon.view}
      wide={wide}
      onRetake={() => camera(coupon.photo ? 'coupon' : 'barcode')}
      onBarcode={() => camera('barcode')}
      onAskAi={() => {
        void chat.send(askAiText(coupon.view));
        router.navigate('/chat');
      }}
    />
  );
}
```

`app/src/vision/prepareImage.test.ts`：

```ts
const resize = jest.fn();
const saveAsync = jest.fn(async () => ({ uri: 'file:///out.jpg', width: 1600, height: 1200 }));
const renderAsync = jest.fn(async () => ({ saveAsync }));
const manipulate = jest.fn(() => ({ resize, renderAsync }));
jest.mock('expo-image-manipulator', () => ({ ImageManipulator: { manipulate }, SaveFormat: { JPEG: 'jpeg' } }));

import { prepareImage, resizeFor } from './prepareImage';

describe('resizeFor', () => {
  it('長邊超過 1600 才縮，依長邊方向', () => {
    expect(resizeFor(4000, 3000)).toEqual({ width: 1600 });
    expect(resizeFor(3000, 4000)).toEqual({ height: 1600 });
    expect(resizeFor(1600, 1200)).toBeNull();
    expect(resizeFor(800, 600)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('prepareImage', () => {
  beforeEach(() => jest.clearAllMocks());

  it('大圖縮到 1600 並存成 JPEG 0.8', async () => {
    await expect(prepareImage({ uri: 'file:///in.jpg', width: 4000, height: 3000 })).resolves.toEqual({ uri: 'file:///out.jpg', width: 1600, height: 1200 });
    expect(manipulate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('file:///in.jpg');
    expect(resize).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ width: 1600 });
    expect(saveAsync).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ compress: 0.8, format: 'jpeg' });
  });

  it('小圖不縮，但仍轉 JPEG', async () => {
    await prepareImage({ uri: 'file:///in.png', width: 800, height: 600 });
    expect(resize).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(saveAsync).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2：實作純函式**

`app/src/vision/prepareImage.ts`：

```ts
/** 上傳前處理：長邊縮到 1600（較小不放大）、轉 JPEG 品質 0.8，確保在 5MB 上限內。 */
import { ImageManipulator, SaveFormat } from 'expo-image-manipulator';
import type { Photo } from './types';

export const MAX_EDGE = 1600;

export function resizeFor(width: number, height: number, max: number = MAX_EDGE): { width: number } | { height: number } | null {
  if (Math.max(width, height) <= max) return null;
  return width >= height ? { width: max } : { height: max };
}

export async function prepareImage(src: Photo): Promise<Photo> {
  const ctx = ImageManipulator.manipulate(src.uri);
  const r = resizeFor(src.width, src.height);
  if (r) ctx.resize(r);
  const img = await ctx.renderAsync();
  const out = await img.saveAsync({ compress: 0.8, format: SaveFormat.JPEG });
  return { uri: out.uri, width: out.width, height: out.height };
}
```

（若安裝版本的 `expo-image-manipulator` 沒有 `ImageManipulator.manipulate`，改用 `manipulateAsync(uri, r ? [{ resize: r }] : [], { compress: 0.8, format: SaveFormat.JPEG })`，並同步調整測試的 mock；行為不變。）

`app/src/vision/scanGate.ts`：

```ts
/** 掃條碼閘門：相機會對同一張條碼連續觸發，只放行第一次；查詢失敗時釋放讓使用者重掃。 */
export function createScanGate() {
  let busy = false;
  return {
    tryAcquire(): boolean {
      if (busy) return false;
      busy = true;
      return true;
    },
    release(): void {
      busy = false;
    },
  };
}
```

`app/src/vision/visionErrors.ts`：

```ts
import { ApiError, errorMessage } from '@/api/errors';

export function visionErrorHint(e: unknown, mode: 'shelf' | 'coupon'): string {
  if (e instanceof ApiError && e.code === 'VISION_FAILED') {
    return mode === 'coupon' ? '看不太清楚，再拍一張試試，或改用掃條碼' : '看不太清楚，再拍一張試試';
  }
  return errorMessage(e);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3：實作相機畫面並註冊路由**

`app/app/camera.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 相機（全螢幕）：貨架／優惠券拍照辨識，或掃優惠券條碼；也可從相簿選照片。 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { CameraView, useCameraPermissions } from 'expo-camera';
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Linking, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { errorMessage } from '@/api/errors';
import { storeTool } from '@/api/storeTools';
import { useApi } from '@/auth/AuthProvider';
import { Mascot } from '@/brand/Mascot';
import { colors, font, radius, space } from '@/theme/tokens';
import { fromCouponCheck, fromVision, type CouponCheck } from '@/vision/coupon';
import { prepareImage } from '@/vision/prepareImage';
import { createScanGate } from '@/vision/scanGate';
import type { Photo } from '@/vision/types';
import { visionErrorHint } from '@/vision/visionErrors';
import { useVisionResults } from '@/vision/VisionResultStore';

type Mode = 'shelf' | 'coupon' | 'barcode';
const MODES: { key: Mode; label: string; hint: string }[] = [
  { key: 'shelf', label: '貨架', hint: '把整排貨架放進框內' },
  { key: 'coupon', label: '優惠券', hint: '把整張優惠券放進框內' },
  { key: 'barcode', label: '掃條碼', hint: '對準優惠券條碼' },
];

export default function Camera() {
  const params = useLocalSearchParams<{ mode?: Mode }>();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>(params.mode && MODES.some((m) => m.key === params.mode) ? params.mode : 'shelf');
  const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();
  const cam = useRef<CameraView>(null);
  const gate = useRef(createScanGate()).current;
  const api = useApi();
  const vision = useVisionResults();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function analyze(src: Photo) {
    const kind = mode === 'coupon' ? 'coupon' : 'shelf';
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const photo = await prepareImage(src);
      const res = await api.analyzeImage(kind, { uri: photo.uri, name: 'photo.jpg', type: 'image/jpeg' });
      if (res.mode === 'shelf') {
        vision.setShelf({ photo, result: res });
        router.replace('/chat/shelf-result');
      } else {
        vision.setCoupon({ photo, view: fromVision(res) });
        router.replace('/chat/coupon-result');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setError(visionErrorHint(e, kind));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function shoot() {
    if (busy) return;
    const pic = await cam.current?.takePictureAsync({ quality: 0.8 });
    if (pic) await analyze({ uri: pic.uri, width: pic.width, height: pic.height });
  }

  async function pick() {
    if (busy) return;
    const r = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({ mediaTypes: ['images'], quality: 0.8 });
    if (!r.canceled && r.assets[0]) {
      const a = r.assets[0];
      await analyze({ uri: a.uri, width: a.width, height: a.height });
    }
  }

  async function onScanned({ data }: { data: string }) {
    if (mode !== 'barcode' || !data || !gate.tryAcquire()) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const { result } = await api.invokeTool<CouponCheck>(storeTool('coupon_check'), { code: data });
      vision.setCoupon({ view: fromCouponCheck(result) });
      router.replace('/chat/coupon-result');
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
      gate.release();
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  if (!permission) return <View style={styles.black} />;
  if (!permission.granted) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.permission}>
        <Mascot pose="wave" size={120} />
        <Text style={styles.permTitle}>需要相機權限</Text>
        <Text style={styles.permText}>拍貨架與優惠券需要使用相機。</Text>
        {permission.canAskAgain ? (
          <Pressable style={styles.permBtn} onPress={() => void requestPermission()} accessibilityRole="button">
            <Text style={styles.permBtnText}>允許使用相機</Text>
          </Pressable>
        ) : (
          <Pressable style={styles.permBtn} onPress={() => void Linking.openSettings()} accessibilityRole="button">
            <Text style={styles.permBtnText}>前往設定</Text>
          </Pressable>
        )}
        <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()} accessibilityRole="button">
          <Text style={{ color: colors.textMuted, fontSize: font.md }}>返回</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  const meta = MODES.find((m) => m.key === mode)!;
  return (
    <View style={styles.black}>
      <CameraView
        ref={cam}
        style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
        facing="back"
        barcodeScannerSettings={{ barcodeTypes: ['qr', 'code128', 'code39', 'ean13', 'ean8'] }}
        onBarcodeScanned={mode === 'barcode' && !busy ? onScanned : undefined}
      />
      <SafeAreaView style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} pointerEvents="box-none">
        <View style={styles.top}>
          <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()} accessibilityLabel="關閉" accessibilityRole="button" hitSlop={12}>
            <Ionicons name="close" size={30} color="#fff" />
          </Pressable>
          <View style={styles.modes}>
            {MODES.map((m) => (
              <Pressable
                key={m.key}
                onPress={() => { setMode(m.key); setError(null); gate.release(); }}
                style={[styles.mode, mode === m.key && styles.modeOn]}
                accessibilityRole="button"
                accessibilityState={{ selected: mode === m.key }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.modeText, mode === m.key && styles.modeTextOn]}>{m.label}</Text>
              </Pressable>
            ))}
          </View>
          <View style={{ width: 30 }} />
        </View>
        <View style={styles.frameWrap} pointerEvents="none">
          <View style={[styles.frame, mode === 'barcode' && styles.frameBarcode]}>
            <Text style={styles.frameHint}>{meta.hint}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        {error ? <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text> : null}
        <View style={styles.bottom}>
          <Pressable onPress={pick} style={styles.album} accessibilityLabel="從相簿選擇" accessibilityRole="button" disabled={mode === 'barcode'}>
            <Ionicons name="images-outline" size={26} color={mode === 'barcode' ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)' : '#fff'} />
          </Pressable>
          {mode !== 'barcode' ? (
            <Pressable onPress={shoot} style={styles.shutter} accessibilityLabel="拍照" accessibilityRole="button">
              <View style={styles.shutterInner} />
            </Pressable>
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.shutterPlaceholder} />
          )}
          <View style={styles.album} />
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
      {busy ? (
        <View style={styles.busy}>
          <Mascot pose="wave" size={96} />
          <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
          <Text style={styles.busyText}>Open醬看看…</Text>
        </View>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  black: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#000' },
  top: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingHorizontal: space.lg, paddingTop: space.sm },
  modes: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 4, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)', borderRadius: radius.pill, padding: 4 },
  mode: { paddingHorizontal: space.lg, paddingVertical: 8, borderRadius: radius.pill },
  modeOn: { backgroundColor: '#fff' },
  modeText: { color: '#fff', fontSize: font.md, fontWeight: '600' },
  modeTextOn: { color: colors.primaryDark },
  frameWrap: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  frame: { width: '78%', height: '62%', borderWidth: 2, borderStyle: 'dashed', borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)', borderRadius: radius.lg, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  frameBarcode: { height: 140 },
  frameHint: { color: '#fff', fontSize: font.md, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)', paddingHorizontal: space.md, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: radius.md, overflow: 'hidden' },
  error: { alignSelf: 'center', color: '#fff', backgroundColor: colors.danger, paddingHorizontal: space.lg, paddingVertical: space.sm, borderRadius: radius.md, overflow: 'hidden', marginBottom: space.md },
  bottom: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-around', paddingBottom: space.xl },
  album: { width: 52, height: 52, borderRadius: 12, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)' },
  shutter: { width: 80, height: 80, borderRadius: 40, borderWidth: 5, borderColor: '#fff', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  shutterInner: { width: 60, height: 60, borderRadius: 30, backgroundColor: '#fff' },
  shutterPlaceholder: { width: 80, height: 80 },
  busy: { ...StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: space.md },
  busyText: { color: '#fff', fontSize: font.lg, fontWeight: '700' },
  permission: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: space.md, padding: space.xl },
  permTitle: { fontSize: font.xl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },
  permText: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.textMuted },
  permBtn: { backgroundColor: colors.primary, borderRadius: radius.lg, paddingHorizontal: space.xl, paddingVertical: 14 },
  permBtnText: { color: '#fff', fontSize: font.lg, fontWeight: '700' },
});
```

`app/src/tasks/orders.ts`：

```ts
const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

/** 建立時間（裝置時區）：今天顯示「今天 HH:mm」，其他日「MM/DD HH:mm」 */
export function formatCreatedAt(iso: string, now: Date = new Date()): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const time = `${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
  const sameDay = d.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear() && d.getMonth() === now.getMonth() && d.getDate() === now.getDate();
  return sameDay ? `今天 ${time}` : `${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}/${pad(d.getDate())} ${time}`;
}

export function formatArrival(date: string): string {
  const [, m, d] = date.split('-');
  return `${m}/${d}`;
}
```

`app/src/tasks/useReplenishments.ts`：

```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { storeTool } from '@/api/storeTools';
import type { ReplenishmentOrder } from '@/api/types';
import { useApi } from '@/auth/AuthProvider';

export function useReplenishments() {
  const api = useApi();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['replenishments'],
    queryFn: async () => (await api.invokeTool<{ orders: ReplenishmentOrder[] }>(storeTool('replenishment_list'), { limit: 20 })).result.orders,
  });
}
```

`app/src/tasks/OrdersView.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 我的補貨單：卡片列表；寬版明細顯示在右側，窄版以彈出面板顯示。 */
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Modal, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import type { ReplenishmentOrder } from '@/api/types';
import { Mascot } from '@/brand/Mascot';
import { colors, font, radius, shadow, space } from '@/theme/tokens';
import { formatArrival, formatCreatedAt } from './orders';

export function OrdersView({
  orders, loading, error, onRetry, wide,
}: {
  orders?: ReplenishmentOrder[];
  loading: boolean;
  error: boolean;
  onRetry(): void;
  wide: boolean;
}) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<ReplenishmentOrder | null>(null);

  if (loading && !orders) return <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primary} style={{ marginTop: space.xxl }} />;
  if (error && !orders) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.center}>
        <Text style={styles.muted}>暫時無法取得補貨單</Text>
        <Pressable onPress={onRetry} accessibilityLabel="重試" accessibilityRole="button" style={styles.retry}>
          <Text style={{ color: colors.primaryDark, fontWeight: '600' }}>重試</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
    );
  }
  if (!orders || orders.length === 0) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.center}>
        <Mascot pose="idle" size={120} />
        <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>還沒有補貨單</Text>
        <Text style={styles.muted}>可以跟 Open醬說「幫我建立補貨單」，或拍一張貨架照片。</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  const detail = selected ? <OrderDetail order={selected} onClose={() => setSelected(null)} /> : null;

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, flexDirection: wide ? 'row' : 'column' }}>
      <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} contentContainerStyle={[styles.grid, wide && { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap' }]}>
        {orders.map((o) => (
          <Pressable
            key={o.id}
            style={[styles.card, wide && { width: '48%' }, selected?.id === o.id && { borderColor: colors.primary }]}
            onPress={() => setSelected(o)}
            accessibilityRole="button"
            accessibilityLabel={`補貨單 ${o.id}`}
          >
            <View style={styles.cardHead}>
              <Text style={styles.orderId}>{o.id}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.status}>已送出</Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.meta}>{`${o.itemCount} 項・到貨 ${formatArrival(o.arrivalDate)}・${formatCreatedAt(o.createdAt)}`}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.summary}>{o.summary}</Text>
          </Pressable>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>
      {wide ? (
        detail ? <View style={styles.side}>{detail}</View> : null
      ) : (
        <Modal visible={Boolean(selected)} transparent animationType="slide" onRequestClose={() => setSelected(null)}>
          <View style={styles.sheetWrap}>
            <View style={styles.sheet}>{detail}</View>
          </View>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

function OrderDetail({ order, onClose }: { order: ReplenishmentOrder; onClose(): void }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: space.md }}>
      <View style={styles.cardHead}>
        <Text style={styles.orderId}>{order.id}</Text>
        <Pressable onPress={onClose} accessibilityLabel="關閉明細" accessibilityRole="button" hitSlop={8}>
          <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color={colors.text} />
        </Pressable>
      </View>
      <Text style={styles.meta}>{`預計到貨 ${formatArrival(order.arrivalDate)}・建立於 ${formatCreatedAt(order.createdAt)}`}</Text>
      {order.items.map((i) => (
        <View key={i.productId} style={styles.itemRow}>
          <Text style={styles.itemName}>{i.name}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.itemQty}>{`${i.quantity} 個`}</Text>
        </View>
      ))}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  center: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: space.md, padding: space.xl },
  muted: { color: colors.textMuted, fontSize: font.md, textAlign: 'center' },
  emptyTitle: { fontSize: font.xl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },
  retry: { paddingHorizontal: space.lg, paddingVertical: space.sm, borderRadius: radius.md, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border },
  grid: { padding: space.lg, gap: space.md },
  card: { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.lg, padding: space.lg, gap: 6, borderLeftWidth: 5, borderLeftColor: colors.primary, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, ...shadow.card },
  cardHead: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' },
  orderId: { fontSize: font.lg, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },
  status: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.primaryDark, backgroundColor: colors.primarySoft, paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 3, borderRadius: radius.pill, overflow: 'hidden', fontWeight: '700' },
  meta: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.textMuted },
  summary: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  side: { width: 380, backgroundColor: colors.surface, padding: space.lg, borderLeftWidth: 1, borderLeftColor: colors.border },
  sheetWrap: { flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end', backgroundColor: colors.overlay },
  sheet: { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderTopLeftRadius: radius.xl, borderTopRightRadius: radius.xl, padding: space.xl },
  itemRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingVertical: space.sm, borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: colors.border },
  itemName: { fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text },
  itemQty: { fontSize: font.md, fontWeight: '700', color: colors.text },
});
```

`app/app/(tabs)/tasks.tsx`：

```tsx
import { useFocusEffect } from 'expo-router';
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useIsWide } from '@/layout/useIsWide';
import { OrdersView } from '@/tasks/OrdersView';
import { useTaskBadge } from '@/tasks/TaskBadge';
import { useReplenishments } from '@/tasks/useReplenishments';
import { colors, font, space } from '@/theme/tokens';

export default function Tasks() {
  const wide = useIsWide();
  const q = useReplenishments();
  const { clear } = useTaskBadge();
  const { refetch } = q;

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      clear();
      void refetch();
    }, [clear, refetch]),
  );

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }}>
      <Text style={{ fontSize: font.xl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text, paddingHorizontal: space.lg, paddingTop: space.lg }}>我的補貨單</Text>
      <OrdersView orders={q.data} loading={q.isLoading} error={q.isError} onRetry={() => void q.refetch()} wide={wide} />
    </View>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4：實作 MeView 與「我的」分頁**

`app/src/me/MeView.tsx`：

```tsx
/** 我的：店員資訊、朗讀開關、伺服器位址（變更後需重新登入）、登出。 */
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Switch, Text, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
import type { Applicant } from '@hyagent/shared';
import { normalizeServerUrl } from '@/api/config';
import { colors, font, radius, shadow, space } from '@/theme/tokens';

export function MeView({
  user, speakEnabled, onToggleSpeak, serverUrl, onSaveServer, onLogout,
}: {
  user: Applicant | null;
  speakEnabled: boolean;
  onToggleSpeak(on: boolean): void;
  serverUrl: string;
  onSaveServer(url: string): Promise<void>;
  onLogout(): void;
}) {
  const [server, setServer] = useState(serverUrl);
  const changed = normalizeServerUrl(server) !== serverUrl && server.trim() !== '';

  return (
    <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.page}>
      <View style={styles.card}>
        <View style={styles.avatar}>
          <Text style={styles.avatarText}>{(user?.name ?? '?').slice(0, 1)}</Text>
        </View>
        <View>
          <Text style={styles.name}>{user?.name ?? ''}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.sub}>{[user?.department, user?.title ?? '店員'].filter(Boolean).join('・')}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.sub}>{`帳號：${user?.id ?? ''}`}</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.card}>
        <Text style={[styles.label, { flex: 1 }]}>朗讀 AI 回覆</Text>
        <Switch value={speakEnabled} onValueChange={onToggleSpeak} accessibilityLabel="朗讀 AI 回覆" trackColor={{ true: colors.primary }} />
      </View>

      <View style={[styles.card, { flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'stretch' }]}>
        <Text style={styles.label}>伺服器位址</Text>
        <TextInput
          style={styles.input}
          value={server}
          onChangeText={setServer}
          autoCapitalize="none"
          autoCorrect={false}
          keyboardType="url"
          accessibilityLabel="伺服器位址"
        />
        <Text style={styles.sub}>變更後需要重新登入。</Text>
        <Pressable
          style={[styles.save, !changed && { opacity: 0.4 }]}
          onPress={() => { if (changed) void onSaveServer(server); }}
          accessibilityLabel="儲存伺服器位址"
          accessibilityRole="button"
          accessibilityState={{ disabled: !changed }}
        >
          <Text style={styles.saveText}>儲存</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>

      <Pressable style={styles.logout} onPress={onLogout} accessibilityLabel="登出" accessibilityRole="button">
        <Text style={styles.logoutText}>登出</Text>
      </Pressable>
    </ScrollView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  page: { padding: space.lg, gap: space.md, maxWidth: 640, width: '100%', alignSelf: 'center' },
  card: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: space.md, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.lg, padding: space.lg, ...shadow.card },
  avatar: { width: 64, height: 64, borderRadius: 32, backgroundColor: colors.primarySoft, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  avatarText: { fontSize: font.xxl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.primaryDark },
  name: { fontSize: font.xl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },
  sub: { fontSize: font.sm, color: colors.textMuted },
  label: { fontSize: font.md, fontWeight: '600', color: colors.text },
  input: { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, borderRadius: radius.md, paddingHorizontal: space.md, paddingVertical: 12, fontSize: font.md, color: colors.text, marginVertical: space.sm },
  save: { alignSelf: 'flex-end', backgroundColor: colors.primary, borderRadius: radius.md, paddingHorizontal: space.xl, paddingVertical: 10, marginTop: space.sm },
  saveText: { color: '#fff', fontWeight: '700' },
  logout: { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.danger, borderRadius: radius.lg, paddingVertical: 14, alignItems: 'center', marginTop: space.md },
  logoutText: { color: colors.danger, fontSize: font.lg, fontWeight: '700' },
});
```

`app/app/(tabs)/me.tsx`：

```tsx
import { useAuth } from '@/auth/AuthProvider';
import { MeView } from '@/me/MeView';
import { useSettings } from '@/settings/SettingsProvider';

export default function Me() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const { speakEnabled, setSpeakEnabled, serverUrl, setServerUrl } = useSettings();
  return (
    <MeView
      user={user}
      speakEnabled={speakEnabled}
      onToggleSpeak={(on) => void setSpeakEnabled(on)}
      serverUrl={serverUrl}
      onSaveServer={async (url) => {
        await setServerUrl(url);
        await logout();
      }}
      onLogout={() => void logout()}
    />
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1：寫 `app/README.md`**

```md
# 門市小幫手 App（Expo）

7-ELEVEN 門市店員 POC Demo。平板（iPad、Android）為主，手機可用。用 **Expo Go** 開啟，不需要建置開發版。

## 安裝

`app/` 是 monorepo 內的獨立 npm 專案（不在根目錄 workspaces 內，因為 App 用 React 19、網頁版用 React 18）：

    cd app
    npm install

## 啟動

    cd app
    EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE=http://<電腦的區網 IP>:3000 npx expo start

平板與電腦連同一個 Wi-Fi，用 Expo Go 掃終端機上的 QR code。伺服器位址也可以在登入頁的「伺服器設定」或「我的」分頁修改。

| 環境變數 | 用途 | 預設 |
|---|---|---|
| `EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE` | 後端位址 | 空（登入頁填） |
| `EXPO_PUBLIC_STORE_SERVER_ID` | 門市 MCP 服務 id | `store-demo` |
| `EXPO_PUBLIC_STORE_NAME` | 頂部列顯示的門市名稱 | `中山門市` |

## 後端需要的設定

- 門市 Demo 服務（`mcp/store-demo`）已在後台註冊，`PUBLIC_BASE_URL` 必須是**平板連得到**的位址（商品圖片網址由它組成，不能用 `localhost`）。
- 店員帳號需要 `service:agent` 與 `mcp:store-demo` 權限。
- 語音對話需要後端設定 `OPENAI_API_KEY`（可用 `SPEECH_MODEL` 換模型）；沒設定時 App 會自動改成只能打字。
- 設備故障排除、門市作業問答需要在知識庫匯入對應文件。

## 品牌素材

`assets/brand/` 目前是灰底占位圖，拿到正式的 Open醬與標誌後用同檔名覆蓋，見該資料夾的 README。

## 測試

    cd app
    npx jest
    npx tsc --noEmit
```

- [ ] **Step 2：寫 `docs/store-app-demo-checklist.md`**

```md
# 門市小幫手 Demo 檢查清單

## 事前準備

- [ ] 後端啟動，`OPENAI_API_KEY`、`MCP_SIGNING_KEY` 已設定；`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`（或其他 LLM）可用。
- [ ] 門市 Demo 服務啟動，`PUBLIC_BASE_URL` 為平板連得到的位址；後台已註冊並重新整理工具清單（應有 7 個工具）。
- [ ] 店員帳號已授權 `service:agent`、`mcp:store-demo`。
- [ ] 知識庫已匯入設備手冊（含咖啡機紅燈處理）與門市作業 SOP。
- [ ] 平板（iPad、Android 各一）已裝 Expo Go，與後端在同一個網路；App 已登入。
- [ ] 準備一排貨架（或貨架照片）、一張有條碼的優惠券（券號 COFFEE50）。

## 情境

| # | 操作 | 預期 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 首頁按「語音對話」，說「幫我查御飯糰的庫存」，按「結束對話」 | 對話出現文字、庫存表格；朗讀開時唸出一兩句重點 |
| 2 | 首頁按「拍照問 AI」，拍貨架 | 結果頁框出缺貨／即期／擺放錯誤，右側分組清單 |
| 2 | 按「建立補貨單（N 項）」→ 改數量、取消一項 → 確認 | 彈窗關閉，對話出現「已完成」；任務分頁紅點 +1 |
| 3 | 首頁點「今日重點 → 商品庫存偏低」 | 自動問「哪些商品庫存偏低？」並出現表格 |
| 3 | 說或打「幫我建立補貨單」→ 確認 | 跳出補貨確認彈窗；完成後在任務分頁看到新單 |
| 4 | 首頁點「今日重點 → 設備異常」 | 自動問「咖啡機紅燈閃爍怎麼處理？」，依知識庫回答並附來源 |
| 5 | 常用情境「促銷活動查詢」、「會員活動」 | 活動表格；會員活動只列會員類 |
| 5 | 相機切「優惠券」拍券 | 優惠券結果頁顯示「可使用」與條件；按「問 AI」轉到對話 |
| 5 | 相機切「掃條碼」掃券 | 直接跳到優惠券結果頁（只查一次） |

## 現場出狀況時

- 語音辨識不準：改用打字，或按「鍵盤輸入」。
- 拍照辨識失敗：再拍一張；優惠券改用掃條碼。
- 確認彈窗顯示逾時：關閉後重新要求建立補貨單。
- 商品圖不顯示：檢查門市服務的 `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` 是否為平板連得到的位址。
```

- [ ] **Step 3：更新 `INTEGRATION.md`**

在描述工具讀寫判定（`readOnlyHint`）的段落之後加入一段：

```md
**唯讀工具可能被 App 直接呼叫**：標示 `readOnlyHint: true` 的工具除了給 AI 使用，也會被前台（例如門市 App 的首頁摘要、列表、掃條碼查券）透過 `POST /api/tools/:toolId/invoke` 直接呼叫，不經 AI、也不經使用者確認。因此唯讀工具**不得有任何副作用**；權限一樣以 `mcp:<serverId>` 控管，呼叫身分同樣以簽章 JWT 帶給你的服務。
```

（找不到該段落時，放在「讀寫判定」或工具標註相關章節的最後。）
